CODE OF THE REPUBLIC OF UZBEKISTAN ON ADMINISTRATIVE RESPONSIBILITY
PART ONE
GENERAL PROVISIONS

## Chapter I. BASIC PROVISIONS

#### Article 1. Legislation on administrative responsibility

The legislation on administrative responsibility consists of this Code, laws of the Republic of Uzbekistan, resolutions of the Oliy Majlis of the Republic of Uzbekistan, decrees of the President of the Republic of Uzbekistan, resolutions of the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan, laws of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, resolutions of the Jokargy Kenes and the Council of Ministers of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, decisions of regional and Tashkent city Councils of People's Deputies, hokims of regions and the city of Tashkent.

The provisions of this Code also apply to administrative offenses, responsibility for the commission of which is provided for by acts of legislation not included in this Code.

#### Article 2. Tasks of legislation on administrative responsibility

The legislation on administrative responsibility has as its tasks the protection of the rights and freedoms of citizens, property, state and public order, the natural environment, ensuring social justice and legality in the interests of the welfare of the individual and society, timely and objective consideration of cases of administrative offenses, as well as the prevention of these offenses, education of citizens in the spirit of observance of the Constitution and laws of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

To accomplish these tasks, this Code determines what action or inaction constitutes an administrative offense, what administrative penalty, by which body (official) and in what order may be applied to a person who has committed an administrative offense, and executed.

#### Article 3. Principles of legislation on administrative responsibility

The legislation on administrative responsibility is based on the principles of legality, equality of citizens before the law, democracy, humanism, justice and inevitable responsibility for guilt.

#### Article 4

Competence of the Republic of Uzbekistan in the field of legislation on administrative responsibility

The jurisdiction of the Republic of Uzbekistan in the field of legislation on administrative responsibility includes the determination of:

principles and establishment of general provisions of legislation on administrative responsibility;

actions or inactions entailing administrative responsibility, the system of bodies (officials) authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses, the procedure for proceedings in such cases and execution of decisions rendered;

competence of the Republic of Karakalpakstan in the field of legislation on administrative responsibility;

the range of issues on which representative bodies of power and hokims of regions, the city of Tashkent may adopt decisions providing for administrative responsibility.

#### Article 5

#### Article 8

Ensuring legality in the application of measures of influence for administrative offenses

No one may be subjected to a measure of influence in connection with an administrative offense except on the grounds and in the manner established by legislation.

Proceedings in cases of administrative offenses are carried out on the basis of observance of legality.

The application by authorized bodies and officials of measures of administrative influence is carried out within the limits of their competence.

Compliance with the requirements of legislation in the application of measures of influence for administrative offenses is ensured by systematic control by authorized higher bodies and officials, prosecutorial supervision, and the right to appeal.

#### Article 9. Effect of legislation on administrative responsibility

See previous version.

A person who has committed an administrative offense is subject to responsibility on the basis of the legislation in force at the time and place of commission of the offense, except for cases provided for in part three of this article.

(part one of Article 9 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 15, 2023 No. 3RU-823 — National Database of Legislation, 15.03.2023, No. 03/23/823/0150 — Enters into force on June 16, 2023)

Citizens of the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as stateless persons permanently residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan, are subject to administrative responsibility in accordance with this Code if they committed an offense on the territory of a foreign state and were not held accountable in accordance with the legislation of that state for committing an administrative offense provided for in Article 47° of this Code.

#### (Article 9

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 15, 2023 No. 3RU-823 — National Database of Legislation, 15.03.2023, No. 03/23/823/0150 — Enters into force on June 16, 2023)

Acts that mitigate or eliminate responsibility for administrative offenses have retroactive effect, that is, they also apply to offenses committed before the issuance of these acts. Acts that establish or increase responsibility for administrative offenses do not have retroactive effect.

Proceedings in cases of administrative offenses are conducted on the basis of legislation in force at the time and place of consideration of the case of the offense.

PART TWO ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE AND ADMINISTRATIVE RESPONSIBILITY GENERAL PART

## Chapter II. ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE

#### Article 10. Concept of administrative offense

An administrative offense is recognized as a wrongful, culpable (intentional or negligent) act or omission that encroaches upon the person, rights and freedoms of citizens, property, state and public order, the natural environment, for which administrative liability is provided by legislation.

Administrative liability for offenses provided for by this Code arises if these violations by their nature do not entail criminal liability.

#### Article 11. Commission of an administrative offense intentionally

An administrative offense is recognized as committed intentionally if the person who committed it was aware of the wrongful nature of his act or omission, foresaw its harmful consequences, desired or consciously allowed their occurrence.

#### Article 12. Commission of an administrative offense through negligence

An administrative offense is recognized as committed through negligence if the person who committed it foresaw the possibility of harmful consequences of his act or omission, but frivolously counted on their prevention, or did not foresee the possibility of such consequences occurring, although he should have and could have foreseen them.

#### Article 13

Age at which administrative liability arises

See previous version.

Persons who have reached the age of sixteen at the time of committing an administrative offense are subject to administrative liability, unless otherwise provided by this Code.

(text of Article 13 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 9, 2019 No. 3RU-514 — National Database of Legislation, 10.01.2019, No. 03/19/514/2450)

#### Article 14. Liability of minors for administrative offenses

See previous version.

To persons aged sixteen to eighteen who have committed administrative offenses, measures provided for by the Regulation on commissions on children's issues are applied.

(part one of Article 14 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

See previous version.

In the event that these persons commit administrative offenses provided for by Articles 61, 1161, 1162, 1163, 125, 1251, 126, 127, 128, 1281, 1282, 1283, 1284, 1285, 1286, 129, 130, 131, 132, 133, 134, 135, 1351, 136, 138, 183, 185, 1851, 1852, 1853, 194, 1941, 1961, 2061, 2091, 218, 220, 221 of this Code, they are subject to administrative liability on general grounds. Taking into account the nature of the offense committed and the personality of the offender, cases concerning the specified persons (with the exception of persons who have committed offenses provided for by Articles 194, 1941 and 1961 of this Code) may be transferred, and those who have committed offenses provided for by Articles 61, 2061 of this Code are subject to transfer for consideration by district (city) commissions on children's issues.

(part two of Article 14 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 14, 2024 No. 3RU-996 — National Database of Legislation, 14.11.2024, No. 03/24/996/0921. Date of entry into force — May 15, 2025)

#### Article 15. Administrative liability of officials

Officials are subject to administrative liability for committing administrative offenses related to non-compliance with established rules in the sphere of protection of management order, state and public order, the natural environment, public health and other rules, ensuring the implementation of which is part of their official duties.

See previous version.

An official is recognized as a person appointed or elected permanently, temporarily or by special authority, performing the functions of a representative of authority or exercising organizational-administrative, administrative-economic functions in state bodies, bodies of citizens' self-government, at enterprises, institutions, organizations, regardless of forms of ownership and authorized to perform legally significant actions, as well as a person exercising the specified functions in an international organization or in a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial body of a foreign state.

(part two of Article 15 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 20, 2015 No. 3RU-391 — SZ RU, 2015, No. 33, Art. 439)

See previous version.

A representative of authority is recognized as a person representing a state body of authority, permanently or temporarily exercising certain functions and within his competence enjoying the right to perform actions or give orders binding on the majority or all citizens or officials.

#### (Article 15

#### 439) Article 16

Administrative liability of military personnel and other persons to whom disciplinary regulations apply

See previous version.

Military personnel and military reservists called up for training, as well as persons of rank-and-file and commanding staff of internal affairs bodies and the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan bear liability for administrative offenses under disciplinary regulations. For non-execution of an enforcement document, violation of legislation on execution of judicial acts and acts of other bodies, legislation in the sphere of provision of state services, traffic rules, rules of hunting, fishing and protection of fish stocks, customs rules, these persons bear administrative liability on general grounds. Administrative arrest may not be applied to the specified persons. A fine may not be applied to military personnel performing compulsory military service.

Other persons, except those specified in part one of this Article, to whom disciplinary regulations or special provisions on discipline apply, in cases directly provided for by them, bear disciplinary liability for committing administrative offenses, and in other cases — administrative liability on general grounds.

#### Article 17

Administrative liability of foreign citizens and stateless persons

Foreign citizens and stateless persons located on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan are subject to administrative liability on general grounds.

With respect to persons enjoying immunity, this Code is applied to the extent not contradicting international treaties and agreements to which the Republic of Uzbekistan is a party.

See previous version.

#### Article 17

². Administrative liability of persons for offenses recorded by the Unified Automated Information System of the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan

In the case of committing administrative offenses provided for in part two of Article 22710 and part three of Article 22713 of this Code, the declarant or his authorized person shall be subject to administrative liability.

In the case of committing an administrative offense provided for in part one of Article 22716 of this Code, the person who assumed an obligation before the customs authority shall be subject to administrative liability.

In the case of committing an administrative offense provided for in part two of Article 22722 of this Code, the declaring person shall be subject to administrative liability, and if the given offense was committed as a result of indicating unreliable information in documents submitted for customs clearance, then the declarant or his authorized person who submitted the corresponding documents and information shall be subject to administrative liability.

When considering administrative cases for violations of customs legislation recorded by the Unified Automated Information System of the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the repetition of the committed offense shall not be taken into account, except for the case provided for in part three of Article 321 of this Code.

#### Article 18. Necessary defense

Actions provided for by this Code or other normative acts establishing administrative liability, but committed in a state of necessary defense, that is, in defense of the person or rights of the defender or another person, the interests of society and the state from unlawful encroachment by causing harm to the encroacher, if the limits of necessary defense were not exceeded, shall not constitute an administrative offense.

#### Article 19. Extreme necessity

Actions provided for by this Code or other normative acts that have caused harm to rights and legally protected interests, committed in a state of extreme necessity, that is, to eliminate a danger threatening the person or rights of the given person or other citizens, the interests of society or the state, if the danger under the circumstances could not be eliminated by other means and if the harm caused is less significant than the harm prevented, shall not constitute an administrative offense.

#### Article 20. Insanity

See previous version.

A person who at the time of committing an unlawful action or inaction was in a state of insanity, that is, could not be aware of the significance of his actions (inaction) or control them due to chronic or temporary mental disorder, feeble-mindedness or other morbid condition, shall not be subject to administrative liability.

(text of Article 20 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of September 12, 2019 No. ZRU-567 — National Database of Legislation, September 13, 2019, No. 03/19/567/3737)

See previous version.

#### Article 21

Release from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense

In case of insignificance of the committed administrative offense, the court may release the offender from administrative liability and limit itself to a warning. If another body (official) considering the case of an administrative offense comes to the conclusion that the committed administrative offense is insignificant, then the case is referred to the court to decide on the release of the offender from administrative liability in the manner established by Article 3081 of this Code.

#### (Article 21

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art. 168)

See previous version.

#### Article 21. Physical or mental coercion or threat

A person who has caused harm to rights and interests protected by this Code, inflicted as a result of physical or mental coercion or threat of application of such coercion, if as a result of such coercion the person could not control his actions (inaction), shall not be subject to administrative liability.

The question of administrative liability for causing harm to rights and interests protected by this Code, inflicted as a result of physical or mental coercion or threat of application of such coercion, as a result of which the person retained the ability to control his actions (inaction), shall be decided taking into account the provisions of Article 19 of this Code.

#### Article 21

[2]. Release from administrative liability in connection with reconciliation

See previous version.

A person who has committed an administrative offense provided for by Article 40 (defamation), Article 41 (insult), Article 45 (violation of inviolability of citizens' dwelling), Article 46 (disclosure of information that may cause moral or material damage to a citizen), Article 46[1] (violation of privacy), Article 52 (infliction of minor bodily injury), parts one and two of Article 61 (petty theft), Article 61[2] (intentional destruction or damage to property), Article 62 (concealment of found property), Article 104 (trampling of crops, damage or destruction of harvested agricultural crops in the field, damage to plantings), Article 121 (damage to internal equipment of vehicles), Article 133 (violation by vehicle drivers of traffic rules resulting in infliction of minor bodily injury or substantial material damage to the victim), Article 134 (violation by drivers of traffic rules resulting in damage to vehicles or other property), Article 183 (petty hooliganism), Article 200 (arbitrary action) of this Code, may be released from administrative liability if he has acknowledged his guilt, reconciled with the victim and made amends for the harm caused to him.

(part one of Article 21[2] as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 7, 2024 No. 3RU-972 — National Database of Legislation, October 8, 2024, No. 03/24/972/0785)

The body (official) considering the case of an administrative offense shall issue a decision to refer the case to the court to decide on the release from administrative liability in connection with reconciliation of the parties in accordance with Article 3131 of this Code.

A person who has committed an offense provided for by part one of this Article repeatedly within a year shall not be subject to release from administrative liability in connection with reconciliation of the parties.

## CHAPTER III. ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTY AND COERCIVE MEASURE

(title of Chapter III as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 26, 2020 No. 3RU-613 — National Database of Legislation, March 26, 2020, No. 03/20/613/0362)

#### Article 22. Purposes of administrative penalty

An administrative penalty is a measure of liability and is applied for the purposes of educating the person who has committed an administrative offense in the spirit of observance and respect for laws, as well as preventing the commission of new offenses both by the offender himself and by other persons.

#### Article 23. Types of administrative penalties

See previous version.

For committing an administrative offense, the following measures of administrative punishment may be applied:

1) administrative warning;

2) fine;

3) seizure with compensation of the value of an object that was an instrument of committing an administrative offense or a direct object thereof;

4) confiscation of an object that was an instrument of committing an administrative offense or a direct object thereof;

5) deprivation of a certain person of a special right granted to him (right to drive a vehicle, right to hunt);

6) mandatory engagement in paid public works;

7) administrative arrest;

8) administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### 173) Article 24. Principal and Additional Administrative Penalties

See previous version.

Paid seizure, confiscation of items and deprivation of a special right (the right to operate vehicles) may be applied as both principal and

additional administrative penalties, other administrative penalties provided for in Article 23 of this Code may be applied only as principal penalties.

(part one of Article 24 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 22, 2018 No. 3RU-503 — National Database of Legislation, 23.10.2018, No. 03/18/503/2080)

For one administrative offense, a principal or a principal and additional penalty may be imposed.

See previous version.

#### Article 241. Administrative Warning

An administrative warning consists of issuing an official reprimand to a person who has committed an administrative offense, with a warning against committing unlawful acts, by the body (official) authorized to apply administrative punishment measures.

An administrative warning is applied in written (electronic) form.

An administrative warning is applied by the body (official) authorized to apply administrative punishment measures, in the event of a person committing offenses provided for in Article 125 in parts one and two, Articles 1281, 1282, 1286, 1351, as well as Article 138 in parts one and two of this Code, provided the person sincerely repents of what was done, and also in the absence of signs of other offenses provided for in this Code.

The administrative warning measure may not be applied to the same person more than once within one year.

#### Article 25. Fine

A fine is a monetary penalty to the income of the state, imposed on a person guilty of committing an administrative offense.

See previous version.

The amount of the fine is determined based on the basic calculation value established at the time of committing the administrative offense, and in the case of a continuing offense — at the time of its detection.

(part two of Article 25 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

The minimum amount of a fine imposed on citizens may not be less than one-fiftieth, and on officials not less than one-tenth of the basic calculation value.

(part three of Article 25 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

The maximum amount of a fine imposed on citizens and employees may not exceed five, and on officials — ten basic calculation values. For certain offenses, a fine of up to four hundred and fifty basic calculation values may be established.

(part four of Article 25 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 25, 2020 No. 3RU-658 — National Database of Legislation, 26.12.2020, No. 03/20/658/1670)

See previous version.

(paragraph five of Article 25 excluded by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated July 10, 2025 No. 3RU-1075)

#### Article 26. Paid Seizure

Paid seizure of an item that was an instrument of commission or the direct object of an administrative offense consists of its

compulsory seizure and subsequent sale with transfer of the proceeds to the former owner minus the costs of selling the seized item.

See previous version.

Paid seizure of hunting firearms, hunting pneumatic weapons and ammunition for them may not be applied to persons for whom hunting is the main source of livelihood.

(part two of Article 26 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on September 2, 2022)

The procedure for applying paid seizure and the types of items subject to seizure are established by this Code and other acts of legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 27. Confiscation

See previous version.

Confiscation of an item that was an instrument of commission or the direct object of an administrative offense consists of the compulsory gratuitous conversion of this item into state property and is applied by the district (city) court for criminal cases. An item that is not the property of the offender is not subject to confiscation, except for things withdrawn from circulation.

(part one of Article 27 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

Confiscation of hunting firearms, hunting pneumatic weapons and ammunition for them, other hunting instruments may not be applied to persons for whom hunting is the main source of livelihood.

(part two of Article 27 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on September 2, 2022)

The procedure for applying confiscation is established by this Code and other acts of legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 28. Deprivation of a Special Right

See previous version.

Deprivation of a special right granted to a given person (the right to operate vehicles, the right to hunt) is applied by the district (city) court for criminal cases for a term of up to three years. The term of deprivation of such a right may not be less than fifteen days, except for cases provided for in Article 341 of this Code.

(part one of Article 28 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated February 20, 2025 No. 3RU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

Deprivation of the right to operate vehicles may not be applied to persons

(part two of Article 28 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated February 20, 2025 No. 3RU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

Deprivation of the right to hunt may not be applied to persons for whom hunting is the main source of livelihood.

See previous version.

#### Article 281. Mandatory Engagement in Paid Community Work

Mandatory engagement in paid community work consists of the compulsory engagement of an offender in the performance of socially useful community work, which is assigned by courts for a term from eight hours to two hundred and forty hours. If the offender is studying, community work shall be performed during time free from studies.

The places (facilities) at which offenders may serve paid community work are determined by local executive authorities.

Paid community work is not applied to persons who have reached retirement age, persons under eighteen years of age, pregnant women, women with children under three years of age, persons with disabilities of the first and second groups, persons with permanent employment, military personnel.

Fifty percent of the wages of persons engaged in paid community work is directed toward repayment of debts under alimony obligations.

In the event the offender evades serving the penalty, the court shall replace the unserved term of paid community work with a penalty at the rate of one day of administrative arrest for four hours of paid community work or a fine in the amount of one base calculation unit for eight hours of paid community work. In this case, the term of administrative arrest must not exceed fifteen days, and the amount of the fine must not exceed twenty times the base calculation unit.

The time of evasion from serving the penalty is not counted toward the term of the penalty served.

(article 28i introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

#### Article 29. Administrative Arrest

See previous version.

Administrative arrest is applied for a term from three to fifteen days, and under conditions of a state of emergency for encroachments on public order — up to thirty days. Administrative arrest is assigned by the district (city) court for criminal cases, and under conditions of a state of emergency — also by the military commandant or head of the internal affairs body.

(part one of article 29 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

Administrative arrest may not be applied to pregnant women, women with children under three years of age, persons raising alone a child under fourteen years of age, to persons under eighteen years of age, to persons with disabilities of the first and second groups.

(part two of article 29 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 17, 2022 No. 3RU-770 — National Database of Legislation, 18.05.2022, No. 03/22/770/0424)

See previous version.

Article 29i. Administrative Expulsion of Foreign Citizens and Stateless Persons Beyond the Borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

Administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan consists of their forced or controlled independent departure with subsequent restriction of the right to enter the Republic of Uzbekistan for a term from one year to three years. Administrative expulsion is applied by the district (city) court for criminal cases.

(part one of article 29i as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

Administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan may be applied in the event of commission of offenses provided for by articles 51i, 51ii, 51iii, 56, 57, 58, part one of article 61i, articles 94, 165i, 184i, 184ii, 189, 189i, 201, 202i, parts three and four of article 224i, parts seven and eight of article 225, articles 239, 240, 241 of this Code.

(part two of article 291 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 15, 2022 No. 3RU-753 — National Database of Legislation, 16.02.2022, No. 03/22/753/0133)

See previous version.

Administrative expulsion from the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan is not applied to foreign citizens and stateless persons who have obligations to pay alimony to a citizen of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### (article 291

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

See previous version.

#### Article 29

². Application of Compulsory Medical Measures in Conditions of Emergence and Spread of Quarantine and Other Infections Dangerous to Humans

A compulsory medical measure in the form of treatment or quarantine confinement may be applied in the presence of appropriate medical grounds by state sanitary supervision bodies with respect to a person who has committed, in conditions of emergence and spread of quarantine and other infections dangerous to humans, an offense related to violation of rules for combating epidemics.

A compulsory medical measure is applied together with the imposition of an administrative penalty for a term of up to thirty days and is executed in places determined by state sanitary supervision bodies.

Execution of a compulsory medical measure is ensured by state sanitary supervision bodies, internal affairs bodies and the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### (article 29

² introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 26, 2020 No. 3RU-613 — National Database of Legislation, 26.03.2020, No. 03/20/613/0362)

## Chapter IV. APPLICATION OF ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTY

#### Article 30. General Rules for Application of Administrative Penalty

A penalty for an administrative offense is applied within the limits and procedure established by this Code and other normative acts.

When applying a penalty, the nature of the offense committed, the personality of the offender, the degree of his guilt, financial situation, circumstances mitigating and aggravating liability are taken into account.

#### Article 31. Circumstances Mitigating Administrative Liability

Circumstances mitigating administrative liability are:

1) sincere repentance of the guilty person;

2) prevention by the guilty person of harmful consequences of the offense, voluntary compensation for damage or elimination of harm caused;

3) commission of an offense under the influence of strong emotional distress or in the confluence of difficult personal, family or other circumstances;

4) commission of an offense under the influence of threat, coercion or by virtue of official, material or other dependence;

5) commission of an offense by a minor;

6) commission of an offense by a pregnant woman or a person raising alone a child under fourteen years of age.

The body (official) considering the case of an administrative offense may recognize other circumstances as mitigating.

#### Article 32. Circumstances Aggravating Administrative Liability

Circumstances aggravating administrative liability are:

1) continuation of unlawful conduct despite demands by persons authorized to do so to cease it;

2) repeated commission within one year of a similar offense for which the person has already been subjected to an administrative penalty, as well as commission of an offense by a person having a criminal record;

3) involvement of a minor in an offense;

4) commission of an offense by a group of persons;

5) commission of an offense in conditions of natural disaster or other emergency circumstances;

See previous version.

6) commission of an offense in a state of intoxication or under the influence of narcotic drugs, their analogues, psychotropic or other substances affecting the intellectual-volitional activity of the person. The body (official) applying the administrative penalty, depending on the nature of the administrative offense, may not recognize this circumstance as aggravating.

(paragraph 6 of Article 32 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. 3RU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, February 21, 2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

#### Article 33. Application of a Milder Administrative Penalty

See previous version.

A court, when applying an administrative penalty, taking into account circumstances mitigating liability and the financial situation of the offender, may apply a penalty below the lowest limit provided in the sanction of articles of the Special Part of this Code or another, milder penalty not provided for by that article, with mandatory indication of motives and grounds. Another body (official) considering a case of administrative offense, taking into account circumstances mitigating liability and the financial situation of the offender, refers the case to a court for resolution of the issue of applying a milder administrative penalty in the procedure established by Article 3081 of this Code.

(text of Article 33 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, January 30, 2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

#### Article 34

Application of Administrative Penalty for Commission of Several Offenses

When a person commits two or more administrative offenses, an administrative penalty is applied for each offense separately.

If a person has committed several administrative offenses, cases of which are simultaneously considered by one and the same body (official), then the final penalty applied to that person is determined within the limits of the sanction providing for a stricter administrative penalty.

If a person has committed an act (omission) for which administrative liability is provided under several articles of the Special Part of this Code, cases of which are considered by different bodies (officials), then the penalty is applied within the limits of the sanction providing for a stricter administrative penalty.

See previous version.

If a person has committed an act (omission) for which administrative liability is provided under the corresponding article of the Special Part of this Code, with involvement of a minor in the commission of an administrative offense (

#### Article 1881 of this Code), then the case materials are transferred to a court for consideration

In cases provided for by parts two, three and four of this article, one of the additional penalties provided for by articles on liability for any of the committed offenses may be added to the principal penalty.

(part four of Article 34 replaced by parts four and five by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 16, 2008 No. ZRU-153 — SZ RU, 2008, No. 16, Art. 117)

See previous version.

#### Article 341

Application of Administrative Penalty for Exceeding the Number of Penalty Points Accrued for Violations of Traffic Rules

For commission of offenses provided for by parts three and five of Article 125, Article 126, part one of Article 127, 1283, part two of Article 1284, Articles 1285, 1286, 1288, 1289, 129, 130, part one of Article 137 of this Code, penalty points are accrued against the driver of a vehicle along with the principal punishment provided for in the specified articles. At the same time, the maximum number of points accrued for each committed offense may not exceed two points.

Penalty points are accrued within one year from the day of commission for the first time of one of the offenses provided for by part one of this article. Penalty points accrued for offenses provided for in part one of this article are removed from the driver of a vehicle automatically upon expiration of one year.

In cases where the accrued penalty points exceed twelve within a year, the driver is deprived of the right to operate vehicles as an additional punishment for the last committed offense in accordance with Articles 24 and 28 of this Code. The term of deprivation of such right may not be less than six months.

An administrative penalty for exceeding the established number of penalty points that are accrued for violations of traffic rules is applied by a district (city) court for criminal cases.

The procedure for accruing penalty points to a driver of a vehicle for violation of traffic rules is established by the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### (Article 341

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, February 21, 2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

#### Article 35. Calculation of Terms of Administrative Arrest and Deprivation of Special Right

The term of administrative arrest is calculated in days, deprivation of a special right — in days, months, years.

#### Article 36. Terms for Application of Administrative Penalty

See previous version.

An administrative penalty may be applied no later than one year from the day of commission of the offense, and in case of a continuing offense — one year from the day of its discovery.

(part one of Article 36 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 10, 2015 No. ZRU-389 — SZ RU, 2015, No. 32, Art. 425)

See previous version.

In case of refusal to initiate a criminal case or termination of a criminal case, but in the presence of signs of an administrative offense in the actions of the offender, an administrative penalty may be applied no later than one month from the day of adoption of the decision to refuse to initiate or terminate a criminal case, if the terms provided for by part one of this article have not expired.

(part two of Article 36 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. ZRU-463 — National Database of Legislation, January 30, 2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

See previous version.

In cases where a violation of traffic rules is recorded by means of special automated technical means of photo and video recording, an administrative punishment may be applied no later than one month from the day of commission of the offense, and in case of appeal of the decision to bring to

administrative liability — no later than one year from the day of commission of the offense.

Unofficial translation.

#### Article 36

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 4, 2026 No. ZRU-1116. See official text in Uzbek language.

The terms provided for in this article do not extend to cases of application of confiscation of items carried out on the basis of customs legislation.


This excerpt sits under: Кучга кириш санаси > Chapter IV. APPLICATION OF ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTY

#### Article 37

Period after which a person is considered not to have been subjected to administrative penalty

If a person subjected to an administrative penalty does not commit a new administrative offense within one year from the date of completion of execution of the penalty, that person is considered not to have been subjected to administrative penalty.

#### Article 38. Imposition of obligation to compensate damage caused

A person who has committed an administrative offense is obliged to compensate the damage caused by the administrative offense.

See previous version.

If the property damage caused as a result of committing an administrative offense to a natural person, enterprise, institution, organization, citizens' self-government body or the state does not exceed the established base calculation value, the body (official) when applying the penalty has the right to decide the question of compensation by the guilty party for this damage, and the court in criminal cases — regardless of its amount.

(part two of Article 38 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

In other cases, compensation for property damage caused by an administrative offense is decided in civil proceedings.

#### Article 39

Performance of obligation for non-fulfillment of which administrative penalty was applied

Application of an administrative penalty does not release a person who has committed an administrative offense from performance of the obligation for non-fulfillment of which the administrative penalty was applied.

SPECIAL PART

## Chapter V

ADMINISTRATIVE LIABILITY FOR OFFENSES ENCROACHING ON THE RIGHTS AND FREEDOMS OF CITIZENS

#### Article 40. Defamation

Defamation, that is, dissemination of knowingly false fabrications defaming another person, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from twenty to sixty base calculation values.

(sanction of Article 40 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Insult, that is, intentional humiliation of the honor and dignity of a person —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from twenty to forty base calculation values.

(sanction of Article 41 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 41

¹. Sexual harassment

Sexual harassment, that is, commission against a person of a single gross or repeated act of a sexual nature unwanted by that person and humiliating their honor and dignity, expressed in description of their appearance or physique, gesticulation, touching, summoning having sexual inclination, —

entails imposition of a fine from two to five base calculation values or administrative arrest for up to five days.

The same offenses committed repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entail imposition of a fine from five to seven base calculation values or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

Violation of citizens' rights to free choice of language in upbringing and education, creation of obstacles and restrictions in the use of language, disrespect for the state language, as well as for other languages of nations and nationalities residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to two base calculation values.

Unlawful entry into a dwelling against the will of persons residing in it, committed by a person who is not an official, —

(sanction of Article 45 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of seven, and on officials — fifty basic calculation units.

entails administrative arrest for fifteen days or mandatory engagement in paid community work for up to one hundred twenty hours or imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, these types of administrative penalties may not be applied.

#### Article 47

[5]. Evasion of maintenance of parents

Evasion of maintenance, that is, non-payment by adult persons for more than two months in total of funds subject to collection by court decision for the maintenance of incapacitated parents or persons replacing them who are in need of material assistance, —

See previous version.

entails administrative arrest for fifteen days or mandatory engagement in paid community work for up to one hundred twenty hours or imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, these types of administrative penalties may not be applied.

#### Article 47

[6]. Leaving a child without supervision on the territory of a foreign state

Leaving a child having citizenship of the Republic of Uzbekistan without supervision on the territory of a foreign state by the father, mother or person replacing them, or by an accompanying person, —

entails imposition of a fine from ten to fifty basic calculation units.

When considering a case of administrative offense concerning leaving a child without supervision on the territory of a foreign state, the court shall impose on the person who committed this administrative offense an obligation to return the child to the Republic of Uzbekistan from the territory of the foreign state with establishment of a maximum period of thirty days or to take measures to eliminate the offense. At the request of the father, mother or person replacing them or the accompanying person, this period may be extended for another thirty days.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from one to five basic calculation units.

The same offenses committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty, —

entail imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine from fifteen to thirty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

#### (Article 47

[8] introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 31, 2023 No. 3RU-874 — National Database of Legislation, 11.01.2023, No. 03/23/874/0818)

#### Article 48. Abuse of guardianship right

Use of guardianship or custodianship for selfish purposes to the detriment of the ward or leaving him without supervision and necessary material assistance —

[0]. Violation of the procedure for issuing ballot papers (voting ballots)

Issuance by a member of an election commission or commission for conducting a referendum to citizens of ballot papers (voting ballots) for voting several times or on behalf of other persons —

entails imposition of a fine up to one basic calculation unit.

#### Article 51

[1]. Violation of the procedure for providing financial reports on state funds allocated for participation in elections

Failure to submit or untimely submission by a political party of an interim or final report to the Central Election Commission of the Republic of Uzbekistan on state funds allocated to it for participation in elections, or provision in the report of knowingly unreliable information —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

(articles 51[0] and 51[1] introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of September 4, 2024 No. 3RU-953 — National Database of Legislation, 05.09.2024, No. 03/24/953/0683)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to two basic calculation units.

See previous version.

Being in public places without a mask in conditions of occurrence and spread of quarantine and other infections dangerous to humans contrary to special requirements of the authorized body —

entails imposition of a fine up to five basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of twenty, and on officials — thirty basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of article 54 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 29, 2021 No. 3RU-688 — National Database of Legislation, 29.04.2021, No. 03/21/688/0394)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to two basic calculation units with confiscation of narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances.

See previous version.

fire-hazardous places, at petrol stations and other public places, including public transport, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one third of a basic calculation unit.

Use of tobacco products on aircraft —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of two basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one hundred to one hundred and fifty, and on officials — from one hundred and fifty to two hundred basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offence or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of objects of the offence.

#### Article 563

Illegal manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or sending without the purpose of sale, of potent substances of special category that are not narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances

Illegal manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or sending without the purpose of sale of potent substances of special category that are not narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances —

entails imposition of a fine from one to two basic calculation units, with confiscation of potent substances of special category that are not narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of potent substances of special category that are not narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances.

A person who has committed actions provided for by this article shall be released from liability if he voluntarily came forward with a confession to the authorities and surrendered potent substances of special category that are not narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances.

#### Article 59

². Domestic (household) violence

Obstruction in exercising the right to property, education, health care and (or) labor, intentional damage to property and personal belongings, as well as humiliation of honor and dignity, intimidation, isolation from close relatives, committed against a spouse, former spouse, a person living together on the basis of a single household or a person having a common child, resulting in health disorder, in the absence of elements of a crime, as well as other offenses provided for by this Code, —

entail imposition of a fine from ten to twenty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to ten days.

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from ten to twenty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

#### Article 59

³. Violation of the procedure for providing psychological assistance

Provision of psychological assistance by a person not having such right, or non-compliance with restrictions established by legislation on provision of psychological assistance to the population, in the absence of elements of a crime, as well as other offenses provided for by this Code, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to twenty, and on officials — from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

#### Article 594. Violation of the procedure for carrying out educational activities

Carrying out educational activities by a person not having the right to engage in such activities, or non-compliance with restrictions on carrying out educational activities established by legislation in the absence of elements of a crime, as well as other offenses provided for by this Code, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

#### Article 595. Violation of legislation on folk medicine

Provision to citizens of services for medical diagnosis and their treatment and public promotion of these services by a person not having the right to carry out activities in the field of folk medicine, in the absence of elements of a crime, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from fifteen to twenty, and on officials — from twenty to twenty-five basic calculation units.

Provision to citizens of services for medical diagnosis and their treatment using methods not permitted in the field of folk medicine for receipt of material valuables or property benefit, as well as public promotion of these services, in the absence of elements of a crime, —

entails imposition of a fine from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

Commission of offenses provided for by parts one or two of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine from twenty to thirty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 5, 2024 No. ZRU-991 — National Database of Legislation, 05.11.2024, No. 03/24/991/0891)

A person who has committed for the first time an offense provided for in part two of this article shall be released from liability if he or she has ensured the return of the unlawfully seized land plot and eliminated the consequences of the unauthorized seizure.

(parts two and three of Article 60 replaced by parts two — four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated August 16, 2021 No. ZRU-708 — National Database of Legislation, 17.08.2021, No. 03/21/708/0799)

Failure by a land owner, land user or lessee of a land plot to take measures to prevent unauthorized seizure of irrigated lands, including failure to notify authorized bodies of the fact of unauthorized seizure of a land plot, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of twenty, and on officials — fifty base calculation values.

(text of Article 60¹ as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated August 16, 2021 No. ZRU-708 — National Database of Legislation, 17.08.2021, No. 03/21/708/0799)

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten base calculation values.

#### Article 61¹. Bribery of an employee of a non-state commercial or other non-state organization

Knowingly unlawful provision to an employee of a non-state commercial or other non-state organization of material valuables or property benefit for performance or non-performance in the interests of the person carrying out the bribery of a certain action which the employee should have or could have committed using his or her powers, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine up to twenty base calculation values.

(sanction of part one of Article 61¹ as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine up to twenty base calculation values.

(sanction of part two of Article 61¹ as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

An employee of a non-state commercial or other non-state organization means a person carrying out labor activity on the basis of a labor or civil law contract, not possessing the characteristics of an official.

#### Article 64

Violation of rules for protection and use of objects of material cultural heritage

See previous version.

Violation of rules for protection and use of objects of material cultural heritage —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from thirty to fifty, and on officials — from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 64 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Construction or destruction in protected zones of immovable objects of material cultural heritage taken under state protection, in specially protected historical and cultural territories, including territories included by their historical and cultural value in the World Heritage List and in their protected zones, of buildings, structures and other objects not considered objects of material cultural heritage, without obtaining permission in the established procedure —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from fifty to one hundred, and on officials — from one hundred to one hundred fifty basic calculation units or administrative arrest up to fifteen days.

(sanction of part two of Article 64 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

Disrespectful attitude toward objects of material cultural heritage, that is  None

None

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from five to ten basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this Article that caused minor damage —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this Article that caused small damage —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one, two or three of this Article repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

entails administrative arrest for fifteen days or imposition of a fine in the amount from twenty to thirty basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, administrative arrest may not be applied.

Minor damage means an amount within the limits of up to thirty basic calculation units, small damage means an amount within the limits from thirty to one hundred basic calculation units.

Commission of an offense provided for in part four of this Article, repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from fifteen to twenty-one, and on officials — from twenty-one to thirty basic calculation units.

LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation. Sanction of part five of Article 74 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 5, 2025 No. ZRU-1062. See official text in Uzbek language. Date of entry into force — August 6, 2025.

#### Article 75. Violation of the rules for maintaining state water records

Violation of the rules for maintaining primary records of the quantity of water withdrawn from water bodies and discharged into them and determining the quality of discharged water, as well as violation of the established procedure for maintaining the state water cadastre —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from three to five basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 75 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

The same offense committed repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 75 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

Damage to water management facilities and devices, violation of the rules for their operation, as well as violation of legislation on the safety of hydraulic structures

See previous version.

Violation of the rules for the operation of water management facilities and devices, as well as violation of legislation on the safety of hydraulic structures —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from seven to ten basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 76 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from one third to one, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation units.

The same offenses committed repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty —

See previous version.

entail the imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 77 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

Violation of the rules for grazing livestock on forest fund lands —

See previous version. entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

(sanction of part three of Article 77 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty –

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to twenty basic calculation units.

#### Article 78. Violation of the procedure for using the logging fund

Violation of the procedure for using the logging fund, harvesting and removal of timber, as well as non-compliance with other requirements provided for in the logging ticket (order) or forest ticket, —

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from one third to one, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation units.

#### Article 79

Illegal felling, grubbing, damage, destruction or transplantation of trees, shrubs, other plants and young growth

Illegal felling, grubbing, damage, destruction or transplantation of trees, shrubs, other plants and young growth, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from twenty-five to fifty, and on officials — from fifty to seventy-five basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 79 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated February 29, 2024 No. ZRU-916 — National Database of Legislation, 01.03.2024, No. 03/24/916/0167)

See previous version.

Intentional damage, trampling or destruction of trees, shrubs or other plants in violation of established requirements with the use of chemical, biological means during the construction, reconstruction of buildings, structures or other facilities on one's own territory —

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from thirty-five to fifty, and on officials — from sixty to seventy-five basic calculation units.

#### (Article 79

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 21, 2024 No. ZRU-1004 — National Database of Legislation, 22.11.2024, No. 03/24/1004/0948)

See previous version.

Commission of the offenses provided for in parts one and two of this article repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from fifty to seventy-five, and on officials — from seventy-five to one hundred basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of the object that was the instrument of commission or the direct object of the administrative offense.

(part three of Article 79 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 21, 2024 No. ZRU-1004 — National Database of Legislation, 22.11.2024, No. 03/24/1004/0948)

Failure to take measures established by law for the protection and preservation of trees on territories owned by or assigned to legal entities, —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from ten to twenty basic calculation units.

#### (Article 79

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2020 No. ZRU-653 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2020, No. 03/20/653/1592)

See previous version.

Development of project documentation leading to the felling, grubbing, damage or destruction of valuable species of trees and shrubs, —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from fifty to seventy-five basic calculation units.

#### (article 79

supplemented with part four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 29, 2024 No. 3RU-916 — National Database of Legislation, 01.03.2024, No. 03/24/916/0167)

See previous version.

#### Article 79

¹. Violation of the procedure and conditions for collection, procurement and use of flora objects

Violation of the established procedure and conditions for collection and procurement of wild plant technical and medicinal raw materials, wild plants for food purposes, as well as use of flora objects for cultural-educational, educational, health, recreational and aesthetic purposes —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

#### (article 79

See previous version.

(sanction of part two of article 81 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 85

Emission of pollutants and biological organisms into the atmospheric air, harmful physical impact on it or consumption of atmospheric air in violation of established requirements

Exceeding the standards of maximum permissible emissions of pollutants and biological organisms into the atmospheric air; exceeding the standards of maximum permissible harmful physical impacts on the atmospheric air; emission of pollutants and biological organisms into the atmosphere without permission from specially authorized state bodies; harmful physical impact on the atmospheric air without permission from specially authorized state bodies in cases when obtaining such permission is necessary in accordance with legislation, as well as failure to fully implement measures to reduce emissions of pollutants and biological organisms creating increased levels of atmospheric pollution during unfavorable meteorological conditions; failure to implement measures to prevent surge emissions —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 85 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Consumption of atmospheric air for production needs in excess of established standards, as well as consumption of atmospheric air without approved standards or without permission from specially authorized state bodies —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from two to five basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 85 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

#### Article 85

¹. Failure to comply with requirements for atmospheric air protection at construction sites

Violation of mandatory requirements to eliminate the lifting into the air of dust and sand particles at a construction site of five hundred square meters or more, on the adjacent territory and exit routes from it, or allowing on these territories exceeding the standards of maximum permissible emissions of pollutants into the atmospheric air —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of ten basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of fifty basic calculation units.

#### (Article 85

¹ introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 3, 2025 No. 3RU-1042 — National Database of Legislation, 04.03.2025, No. 03/25/1042/0212. Date of entry into force — June 5, 2025)

#### Article 86

Violation of operating rules, as well as non-use of equipment for purification of emissions into the atmosphere

Violation of operating rules or non-use of installed structures, equipment, apparatus for purification of emissions into the atmosphere, as well as means of control over the quantity and composition of emitted pollutants —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from one to three basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 86 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

The same offense committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 86 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

#### Article 86

¹. Violation of requirements for prevention of harmful impact on the ozone layer

Failure by enterprises, institutions and organizations in the design, construction, reconstruction, major repair of facilities, implementation of economic and other activities to take measures to regulate the use and application of ozone-depleting substances, equipment and technical devices containing ozone-depleting substances, as well as failure to maintain records of ozone-depleting substances, failure to ensure their replacement with ozone-safe substances, allowing their emission into the atmospheric air, failure to implement recirculation (primary purification of ozone-depleting substances for their reuse) —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one half to one, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to seven basic calculation units.

#### (Article 86

¹ introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated August 25, 2021 No. 3RU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force from November 27, 2021)

#### Article 87

Manufacture, commissioning of transport, other mobile means and installations with exceeding standards of pollutant content in emissions

See previous version.

Manufacture, commissioning of automobiles, aircraft, vessels and other mobile means and installations in which the content of pollutants in emissions, as well as the level of noise and other physical impacts produced by them in operation, exceed established standards, or failure to ensure inspection and adjustment of the content of pollutants in exhaust gases and harmful impact of their physical factors for compliance with standards during repair and maintenance of mobile means and installations —

(disposition of part one of Article 87 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated August 30, 1997, No. 485-I — Gazette of the Oliy Majlis, 1997, No. 9, Art. 241)

entails imposition of a fine on officials from one to three basic calculation units.

Operation of mobile installations in which the content of pollutants in emissions, as well as the level of noise produced by them during operation, exceed established standards —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one tenth to one half of a basic calculation unit.

(sanction of part two of Article 87 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Commission of the offense provided for in part one or two of this article repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to two, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation units.

(sanction of part three of Article 87 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 90

Violation of the rules of hunting and fishing, as well as the rules for carrying out other types of use of animal life

Violation of the rules of hunting and fishing, as well as the rules for carrying out other types of use of animal life in the absence of signs of a crime —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from twenty to thirty, and on officials — from thirty to forty basic calculation values.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part one of this article repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from thirty to forty, and on officials — from forty to fifty basic calculation values with confiscation of instruments and objects of commission of the said offense.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part one of this article repeatedly within a year after two applications of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from forty to fifty, and on officials — from fifty to seventy basic calculation values with confiscation of instruments and objects of commission of the said offense or deprivation of the right to hunt for up to three years with confiscation of instruments and objects of commission of the said offense.

Hunting or fishing without proper authorization or in prohibited places or during prohibited periods, using prohibited instruments or methods (except for cases of use of electric current, explosive, chemical substances or biological preparations) —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from fifty to seventy, and on officials — from seventy to one hundred basic calculation values with confiscation of instruments and objects of commission of the said offense or deprivation of the right to hunt for up to three years with confiscation of instruments and objects of commission of the said offense.

(text of Article 90 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 21, 2024 No. ZRU-1004 — National Database of Legislation, 22.11.2024, No. 03/24/1004/0948)

See previous version.

Manufacturing, import, storage, transportation or shipment, acquisition or sale of prohibited hunting or fishing implements or means —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from seven to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of implements and objects of the offense.

(sanction of part one of Article 90¹ as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from seven to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units with confiscation of implements and objects of the offense.

(sanction of part two of Article 90¹ as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from twenty to thirty basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from thirty to fifty basic calculation units with confiscation of the object that served as an instrument for committing the administrative offense.
