¹ supplemented by parts three and four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2020 No. ZRU-653 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2020, No. 03/20/653/1592)

#### Article 91

². Violation of requirements for placement and operation of sanitary cleaning infrastructure facilities

Violation of requirements for placement and operation of sanitary cleaning infrastructure facilities —

See previous version.

Entails imposition of a fine on citizens from two to five, and on officials — from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 93 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 94

Destruction, extraction of rare or endangered animals or commission of other actions that may lead to death, reduction in numbers or disturbance of the habitat of such animals

Destruction, extraction of rare or endangered animals belonging to animal species listed in the Red Book of the Republic of Uzbekistan, or destruction of their egg clutches, roe, dwellings, destruction of nests, burrows and other shelters or commission of other actions that may lead to death, reduction in numbers or disturbance of the habitat of such animals, or extraction of these animals in violation of the conditions specified in the permit for extraction, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units, with confiscation of objects that served as instruments for committing the specified offenses or without such.

(sanction of part one of Article 94 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

The same offenses, committed repeatedly within one year after application of an administrative penalty, —

See previous version.

entail imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units with confiscation of objects that served as instruments for committing the specified offenses.

(sanction of part two of Article 94 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 95

Failure to take measures to restore the natural environment, reproduce natural resources and eliminate the consequences of harmful impact on the natural environment

Failure to take measures to restore the natural environment, reproduce natural resources and eliminate the consequences of harmful impact on the natural environment —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 95 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 96

Implementation of projects without a positive conclusion of state environmental (sanitary-ecological) expertise

Implementation of projects without a positive conclusion of state environmental (sanitary-ecological) expertise or with deviation from it, as well as opening of financing or financing of facilities without such conclusion —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one-half to one, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 96 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. 3RU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force on November 27, 2021)

The same offenses committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty, —

entail imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to seven basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of Article 96 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. ZRU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force on November 27, 2021)

#### Article 99. Violation of urban planning legislation

Violation of requirements when performing design, construction and installation works, as well as intentional evasion from execution or untimely execution of instructions of inspections for control in the sphere of construction and housing and communal services and for control over the quality of road construction works (with the exception of non-residential premises of no more than 300 cubic meters and individual housing construction facilities with no more than two floors (excluding the basement floor), height of no more than 12 meters from the ground surface and (or) total area of no more than 500 square meters) —

See amendments entering into force on May 10, 2026

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of thirty, and on officials — in the amount of seventy basic calculation units.

The same offense, committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one hundred, and on officials — in the amount of one hundred fifty basic calculation units.

Incomplete fulfillment of mandatory instructions issued by the design organization to the customer according to the project during construction and installation works, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of forty, and on officials — in the amount of seventy basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article, repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of seventy, and on officials — in the amount of one hundred basic calculation units.

Violation of the requirements of technical regulations in the production of construction materials, structures and products —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of one hundred fifty basic calculation units.

Commission of offenses provided for in part five of this article, repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of two hundred basic calculation units.

Violation of the rules for acceptance into operation of buildings and structures completed by construction —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of seventy, and on officials in the amount of one hundred fifty basic calculation units.

Construction, reconstruction and major repair of buildings, structures or other facilities in zones where prohibitions (restrictions) on conducting such works are established in accordance with urban planning legislation, or design, reconstruction and construction of buildings and structures in violation of approved master plans (not provided for by master plans), planning and development projects of settlements —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one hundred fifty, and on officials in the amount of two hundred basic calculation units.

Construction of facilities without permission of inspections for control in the sphere of construction and housing and communal services and for control over the quality of road construction works —

See amendments entering into force on May 10, 2026

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one hundred fifty, and on officials in the amount of two hundred basic calculation units.

Construction of facilities without a positive conclusion of expert organizations on urban planning documentation —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of seventy, and on officials in the amount of one hundred basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part ten of this article, repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one hundred, and on officials in the amount of one hundred fifty basic calculation units.

#### (article 99

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 25, 2025 No. 3RU-1050 — National Database of Legislation, 26.03.2025, No. 03/25/1050/0276. Date of entry into force — June 27, 2025)

See previous version.

Issuance by expert organizations of a positive conclusion on urban planning documentation in the absence of a separate section (part) of the project on creating conditions for persons with disabilities or on this separate section (part) that does not comply with urban planning norms and rules, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty to seventy basic calculation units.

Issuance by the bodies of the Inspection for Control in the Sphere of Construction and Housing and Communal Services under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan of permission for operation of buildings and structures completed by construction in case of non-compliance in the urban planning documentation with a separate section (part) of the project on creating conditions for persons with disabilities, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty to seventy basic calculation units.

#### Article 100. Wasteful consumption of electrical and thermal energy

Wasteful consumption of electrical and thermal energy, that is, systematic, without production necessity underloading or use in idle mode of electric motors, electric furnaces and other electrical and thermal equipment,

systematic loss of compressed air, water and heat, use without permission of the energy supply organization of electrical energy for heating office and other premises, as well as for purposes not provided for by the production process, mismanagement use of electrical energy for lighting —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to fifteen basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of article 100 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

The same offense, committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from fifteen to thirty basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of article 100 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

#### Article 101. Violation of the rules for use of electrical, thermal energy, gas

See previous version.

Unauthorized connection to electrical, thermal, gas networks of general use or other violations of the rules for their use, or intentional damage to metering devices for electrical, thermal energy, natural gas including their seals or external interference with metering devices with the purpose of changing their readings —

(disposition of article 101 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of June 13, 2017 No. ZRU-436 — SZ RU, 2017, No. 24, art. 487)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from twenty to twenty-five basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of article 101 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 16, 2021 No. ZRU-708 — National Database of Legislation, 17.08.2021, No. 03/21/708/0799)

See previous version.

Unauthorized connection to electrical, thermal, gas networks of buildings and structures erected on illegally seized land plots, by organizations of electric power, heat supply and gas supply —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from seventy to eighty-five basic calculation units.

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 16, 2021 No. ZRU-708 — National Database of Legislation, 17.08.2021, No. 03/21/708/0799)

See previous version.

This article does not apply to actions related to petty theft for commercial purposes by unauthorized connection to public electrical or gas networks, or intentional damage to electricity or natural gas metering devices, including their seals, or external interference with metering devices for the purpose of altering their readings.

#### (article 101

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 13, 2023 No. ZRU-822 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2023, No. 03/23/822/0143 — Enters into force on June 15, 2023)

#### Article 102. Violation of rules for protection of electrical networks

Violation of rules for protection of electrical networks with voltage up to 1000 volts (overhead, underground and underwater power transmission lines, input and distribution devices), which caused or could have caused interruptions in the supply of

electrical energy to consumers, damage to electrical networks or other harm to the national economy, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one tenth to one half, and on officials — from one half to two basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of article 102 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Violation of rules for protection of electrical networks with voltage exceeding 1000 volts, which caused or could have caused an interruption in the supply of electrical energy to consumers, damage to electrical networks or other harm to the national economy, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one third to one, and on officials — from one to three basic calculation values.

(sanction of part two of article 102 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 103. Violations related to the use of gas as fuel or raw material

See previous version.

Starting gas supply to gas-consuming installations without permission or failure to comply with the established gas consumption regime, or consumption of gas at gas-consuming installations without approved specific gas consumption rates or exceeding these rates —

(disposition of part one of article 103 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2004, No. 714-II — Collection of Legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan, 2004, No. 51, art. 514)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from two to three basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of article 103 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Operation of gas-consuming installations without accounting for gas consumption or without accounting for thermal energy and products produced using gas, or absence (malfunction) of automatic gas combustion process control means, heat engineering control devices or heat recovery equipment provided for by the gas-consuming installation design, ensuring rational and efficient use of gas, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from one to two basic calculation values.

(sanction of part two of article 103 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Unpreparedness for operation of reserve fuel facilities provided for a gas-consuming enterprise, institution, organization, or unpreparedness of gas-consuming installations for operation on established reserve types of fuel —

See previous version.

(sanction of part three of article 103 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 107

Violation of phytosanitary requirements (measures) and other rules for combating plant quarantine objects and other harmful organisms

Violation of phytosanitary requirements and other rules for combating plant quarantine objects and other harmful organisms in the production, procurement, storage, transportation, processing, sale and destruction of quarantine products —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to five basic calculation units.

Failure to comply with phytosanitary measures, including failure to take measures to prevent the spread of plant quarantine objects and other harmful organisms, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to five basic calculation units.

See previous version.

Persons who have discovered plant quarantine objects and other harmful organisms on their territory, voluntarily contacted the Agency for Quarantine and Plant Protection under the Ministry of Agriculture of the Republic of Uzbekistan regarding the conduct of phytosanitary field control and taken urgent measures to localize and eliminate identified foci of their spread, are released from administrative liability.

#### Article 109. Violation of veterinary, veterinary-sanitary rules and norms

Violation of veterinary, veterinary-sanitary rules and norms, failure to take measures to combat epizootics —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to five basic calculation units.

Violation of rules of restrictive measures (quarantine) upon detection of foci of contagious animal diseases, as well as concealment from state veterinary supervision bodies and failure to report to them information about simultaneous mass disease or sudden death of animals, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation units.

Violation of safety norms for veterinary preparations and feed additives and rules for their production, preparation, storage, transportation or shipment, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten basic calculation units with confiscation of instruments and objects of the offense.

(text of Article 109 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 15, 2023 No. 3RU-878 — National Database of Legislation, 16.11.2023, No. 03/23/878/0852 — Enters into force on February 17, 2024)

See previous version.

#### Article 110. Violation of rules for keeping animals

Violation of rules for keeping animals in cities and other populated areas, as well as failure by officials to take measures to catch and keep stray animals —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

The same offense, which has caused damage to the health of people, property of legal entities and individuals, in the absence of signs of a crime, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from fifteen to thirty basic calculation units.

#### (Article 110

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 16, 2025 No. 3RU-1019 — National Database of Legislation, 17.01.2025, No. 03/25/1019/0041)

#### Article 111. Cruelty to Animals

Cruelty to animals, that is, their torture or causing them suffering from mercenary or other base motives, —

entails imposition of a fine from three to five basic calculation units.

The same offense, committed repeatedly within one year after application of an administrative penalty, or commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article in the presence of a minor or resulting in the death or injury of animals, —

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

#### (article 111

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. 3RU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force on November 27, 2021)

entails imposition of a fine from one-half to one basic calculation unit.

entails imposition of a fine from one-fifth to one-half of a basic calculation unit.

Crossing railway tracks in unauthorized places —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one-twentieth of a basic calculation unit.

(sanction of part four of article 113 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Crossing of railway tracks by drivers of vehicles in an unauthorized place —

entails imposition of a fine from two to three basic calculation units.

See previous version.

#### Article 118. Violation of safety rules on water transport

Violation on water transport of established rules for entry of vessels into port and exit from port, rules of movement, mooring and towing of vessels in port waters, or violation of the established procedure for displaying signal lights, exchange of sound, as well as visual and day signals, or installation of devices for catching fish within the vessel passage —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one-fifth to one-half of the basic calculated value.

(sanction of part one of Article 118 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version. entails imposition of a fine on officials from one-fifth to one-half of the basic calculated value.

(sanction of part two of Article 118 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Damage to structures and devices for signaling and communication ensuring safety on water transport, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens up to one-tenth, and on officials — from one-fifth to one-half of the basic calculated value.

(sanction of part three of Article 118 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 1251. Violation of the Rules for Transportation of Cargo, Towing Rules

See previous version.

Violation of the rules for transportation of cargo, as well as towing rules —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten basic calculation values.

Entry onto the road in vehicles whose dimensional parameters with or without cargo, as well as total actual weight, axle loads exceed the established norm by up to ten percent —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten basic calculation values.

Entry onto the road in vehicles whose dimensional parameters with or without cargo, as well as total actual weight, axle loads exceed the established norm by ten to twenty percent —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen basic calculation values.

Transportation of heavy, oversized, dangerous cargo, entry onto the road in vehicles whose dimensional parameters with or without cargo, as well as total actual weight, axle loads exceed the established norm by twenty percent, without having the appropriate permit, as well as with deviation from the route of movement specified in the permit, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty basic calculation values.

When committing offenses provided for in parts two, three and four of this article, an administrative penalty is applied taking into account the total permissible error of the measuring device when determining the dimensional and weight parameters of motor vehicles, minus a two percent difference from the dimensional and weight parameters recorded by the measuring device.

(text of Article 1251 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. 3RU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 1252

Illegal Use of Motor Vehicles Registered in Other States for Transportation of Cargo and (or) Passengers

Transportation of cargo and passengers by motor vehicles belonging to foreign carriers, including those temporarily imported by them into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan, between points located on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan (cabotage), unless otherwise stipulated by international treaties of the Republic of Uzbekistan, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty basic calculation values.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of five, and on officials — ten basic calculation values.

#### Article 1283

Exceeding the established speed limit by drivers of vehicles

Exceeding the established speed limit by drivers of vehicles by an amount not exceeding 20 kilometers per hour —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculated value.

Exceeding the established speed limit by drivers of vehicles by an amount of more than 20, but not more than 40 kilometers per hour —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculated values.

Exceeding the established speed limit by drivers of vehicles by an amount of more than 40 kilometers, but not more than 60 kilometers per hour —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of nine basic calculated values.

Exceeding the established speed limit by drivers of vehicles by an amount of more than 60 kilometers per hour —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen basic calculated values.

In the commission of offenses provided for in parts one through four of this article, administrative penalty is applied taking into account the permissible total margin of error of special devices measuring speed and the speedometer of vehicles, with a deduction of 5 kilometers per hour from the speed recorded by them.

#### (article 1283

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. 3РУ-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 1285

Creation by drivers of vehicles of an obstruction or emergency situation, entry onto the side of the road or lane intended for oncoming traffic

See previous version.

Creation by drivers of vehicles of an obstruction to the unimpeded passage of vehicles of operational and special services approaching with flashing beacons of blue or red or blue and red colors and a special sound signal activated, —

(disposition of part one of article 1285 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of July 21, 2020 No. ЗРУ-629 — National Database of Legislation, 22.07.2020, No. 03/20/629/1087)

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculated values.

(sanction of part one of article 1285 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ЗРУ-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Entry by drivers of vehicles in violation of traffic rules onto the side of the road or lane intended for oncoming traffic, or commission by them of an offense that resulted in the creation of an emergency situation, that is, forcing other traffic participants to abruptly change speed, direction of movement or take other measures to ensure their own safety or the safety of other citizens, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of ten basic calculated values.

(sanction of part two of article 1285 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ЗРУ-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

(part three of article 1285 excluded by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ЗРУ-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 1286. Violation by drivers of vehicles of stopping or parking rules

Violation by drivers of vehicles of stopping or parking rules —

See previous version.

entails an administrative warning or imposition of a fine in double the amount of a basic calculated value.

#### Article 1287

Movement of vehicles on a road with a separately designated lane for route vehicles

Movement of vehicles on a road with a separately designated lane for route vehicles (except in permitted cases) —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculated value.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part one of this article, repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculated values.

#### (article 1287

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 10, 2025 No. ЗРУ-1055 — National Database of Legislation, 11.04.2025, No. 03/25/1055/0330)

See previous version.

#### Article 1288. Violation of rules for transporting people in a vehicle

Violation of rules for transporting people —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of two basic calculated values.

Transportation of people in buses and minibuses without escort by internal affairs bodies, if transportation of people under their escort is mandatory —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculated values.

#### Article 1289. Violation of vehicle overtaking rules

Violation of vehicle overtaking rules —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation units.

Article 12810. Road hooliganism

Road hooliganism, that is, intentional creation by drivers of vehicles of obstacles to the movement of other vehicles, unjustified spinning (drift) by a vehicle while maintaining angle and (or) speed of movement, sharp change without reason of the roadway in one's direction continuously three or more times (except when committed in specially designated areas) or demonstrative commission of other actions in a vehicle threatening the safe movement of road users or showing disrespect to other road users, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty-five basic calculation units with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term from one year to two years.

The same offense committed by a person who does not have the right to drive vehicles or is deprived of the right to drive vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for fifteen days.

(articles 1288, 1289 and 12810 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 129

Participation of drivers of vehicles in group movement creating a threat to road safety

(title of article 129 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, art. 168)

See previous version.

Participation of drivers of vehicles in group driving threatening road safety or resulting in an emergency situation, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation units.

(text of article 129 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

#### Article 130

Violation by drivers of vehicles of rules for crossing railway crossings

Violation by drivers of vehicles of rules for crossing railway crossings —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of article 130 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

(part two of article 130 excluded by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 131. Driving vehicles while intoxicated

Driving vehicles by drivers while intoxicated with alcohol or under the influence of narcotic drugs, their analogues, psychotropic or other substances affecting the intellectual-volitional activity of a person, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of forty basic calculation units with deprivation of the right to drive vehicles for a term of three years.

The same offense committed by a person who does not have the right to drive vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for fifteen days.

#### (article 131

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

#### Article 132. Operation of small vessels by vessel operators while intoxicated

Operation of small vessels by vessel operators while intoxicated, as well as transfer of control of small vessels to a person who is in a state of alcoholic, narcotic or other intoxication, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation units or deprivation of vessel operators of the right to operate a vessel for two years.

(sanction of article 132 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. ZRU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193 — Enters into force on March 26, 2020)

See changes entering into force on May 10, 2026

#### Article 133

Violation by drivers of vehicles of traffic rules resulting in causing the victim minor bodily injury or substantial material damage

See previous version.

Violation by drivers of vehicles of traffic rules resulting in causing the victim minor bodily injury or substantial material damage, except for cases when, upon commission of a road traffic accident that did not result in causing the victim minor bodily injury, the drivers involved in the road traffic accident have drawn up a notice of road traffic accident —

(disposition of part one of article 133 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. ZRU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force on November 27, 2021)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from five to seven basic calculation units or deprivation of the right to drive vehicles from one to three years.

(sanction of part one of article 133 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

Substantial material damage means damage exceeding five basic calculation units.

(part two of article 133 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 134

Violation by drivers of traffic rules resulting in damage to vehicles or other property

See previous version.

Violation by drivers of vehicles of traffic rules resulting in damage to vehicles, traffic control devices or other property, not causing substantial material damage, except for cases when, upon commission of a road traffic accident that did not result in damage to traffic control devices or other property, the drivers involved in the road traffic accident have drawn up a notice of road traffic accident —

(disposition of article 134 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. ZRU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825 — Enters into force on November 27, 2021)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from two to four basic calculation units or deprivation of the right to drive vehicles from six months to one year.

(sanction of article 134 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

#### Article 135

Operation of vehicles by persons not having documents provided for by traffic rules

(title of Article 135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art. 168)

See previous version.

Operation of vehicles by drivers not having with them documents for the right to operate them, documents on registration of the vehicle, as well as confirming the right of ownership, use or disposal of the vehicle in the absence of its owner, insurance policy for compulsory insurance of civil liability of vehicle owners, appropriate permit for tinting (darkening) of windows, documents issued by customs authorities for a vehicle imported into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan from abroad, and in cases provided for by legislation, a license card or waybill, certificate of completion of advanced training for drivers of vehicles of legal entities, including those engaged in activities for the transportation of passengers or cargo (with the exception of a two-year period from the moment of obtaining a permit for the right to operate vehicles, except for cases of its replacement or issuance instead of a lost one), as well as operation of vehicles on documents issued by customs authorities with an appropriate mark confirming the import of vehicles, with an expired validity period, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculated value.

(part one of Article 135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

Operation of vehicles by persons not having the right to operate vehicles or not having documents giving them the right to operate these particular vehicles, as well as entrusting the operation of vehicles to a person not having the right to operate vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculated values.

(part two of Article 135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

Operation of vehicles by persons deprived of this right, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculated values.

(sanction of part three of Article 135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

In case of having with oneself a biometric passport or identification ID-card issued by internal affairs bodies or consular institutions of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the driver is not required to have with him a document for the right to operate a vehicle, documents on registration of the vehicle, as well as confirming the right of ownership, use or disposal of the vehicle in the absence of its owner, insurance policy for compulsory insurance of civil liability of vehicle owners, appropriate permit for tinting (darkening) of windows.

(part four of Article 135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 1351

Violation of requirements of legislation on compulsory insurance of civil liability of vehicle owners

Operation of vehicles during the period of their use not provided for by insurance policies for compulsory insurance of civil liability of vehicle owners, as well as operation of vehicles in violation of the conditions provided for by these insurance policies for the operation of these vehicles only by drivers specified in these insurance policies —

See previous version.

entails administrative warning or imposition of a fine in the amount of one half of a basic calculated value.

#### Article 136

Evasion by drivers of vehicles and other road users from undergoing examination for state of intoxication

See previous version.

Evasion by drivers of vehicles from undergoing in accordance with the established procedure examination for state of alcohol intoxication or being under the influence of narcotic drugs, their analogues, psychotropic or other substances affecting the intellectual-volitional activity of a person, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of thirty basic calculated values with deprivation of the right to operate vehicles for three years.

(part one of Article 136 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

The same offense committed by a person not having the right to operate vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculated values or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

(part two of Article 136 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article by a person operating a scooter, moped, bicycle, personal mobility device, driving a horse-drawn carriage, —

(disposition of part three of Article 136 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 28, 2024 No. ZRU-985 — National Database of Legislation, 28.10.2024, No. 03/24/985/0861. Date of entry into force — November 29, 2024)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculated values.

(sanction of part three of Article 136 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 137. Leaving the scene of a traffic accident

See previous version.

Leaving in violation of established rules the scene of a traffic accident by its participants —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen basic calculated values.

Leaving in violation of established rules the scene of a traffic accident by its participants in case this traffic accident resulted in causing the victim minor bodily injury or substantial material damage, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of thirty basic calculated values or deprivation of the right to operate vehicles for a period of three years or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

(sanction of part two of Article 137 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 138

Violation of traffic rules by pedestrians and other road users

(title of Article 138 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art. 168)

See previous version.

Failure of pedestrians to obey traffic control signals, crossing the roadway in unauthorized places, using a telephone, reading books or periodicals, viewing video materials and listening to audio materials, as well as using other electronic devices that distract attention while pedestrians are moving on the roadway, including on a pedestrian crossing, as well as failure to obey traffic control signals, failure to comply with the requirements of priority road signs, prohibitory or prescriptive road signs by persons operating scooters, mopeds and bicycles, personal mobility devices, driving horse-drawn carts and other persons using the roads.

(disposition of part one of Article 138 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 28, 2024 No. ZRU-985 — National Database of Legislation, 28.10.2024, No. 03/24/985/0861. Effective date — November 29, 2024)

See previous version.

entails an administrative warning or imposition of a fine in the amount of one third of the basic calculation value.

Presence of citizens on the roadway for the purpose of providing various services to drivers, grazing livestock in the road right-of-way zone —

entails an administrative warning or imposition of a fine in the amount of one half of the basic calculation value.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculation value.

Operation of personal mobility devices, scooters, mopeds and bicycles not equipped in the prescribed manner with reflectors and lights, as well as transportation of people by drivers of personal mobility devices and scooters, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one half of the basic calculation value.

supplemented by part five by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 28, 2024 No. ZRU-985 — National Database of Legislation, 28.10.2024, No. 03/24/985/0861. Effective date — November 29, 2024)

See previous version.

See previous version.

#### Article 139

Release onto the line of vehicles having malfunctions and other violations of vehicle operation rules

Release onto the line of vehicles not registered in the prescribed manner or not having passed mandatory technical inspection by a person responsible for the technical condition and operation of vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of two basic calculation values.

Release onto the line of vehicles having malfunctions or conditions under which operation of vehicles is prohibited, or modified without appropriate permission by a person responsible for the technical condition and operation of vehicles, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation values.

#### (Article 139

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 140

Allowing or transferring control of a vehicle to a person in a state of intoxication

Allowing drivers in a state of alcohol intoxication or under the influence of narcotic drugs, their analogues, psychotropic or other substances affecting the intellectual-volitional activity of a person, or persons not having the right to operate vehicles, to operate vehicles by a person responsible for its operation, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of thirty basic calculation values.

Transferring control of a vehicle to a person knowingly in a state of alcohol intoxication or under the influence of narcotic drugs, their analogues, psychotropic or other substances affecting the intellectual-volitional activity of a person, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of thirty basic calculation values with deprivation of the right to operate vehicles for a period of two to three years.

Commission of the offense specified in part two of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculation values or administrative arrest for a period of fifteen days.

#### (Article 140

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

#### Article 142. Violation of rules for transportation of dangerous substances and objects on transport

Violation of rules for transportation on railway, air and water transport as hand luggage of flammable, poisonous, easily flammable, explosive and toxic substances and objects —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to three basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of Article 142 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Depositing in a storage room explosive, easily flammable, toxic and malodorous substances and objects —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to two basic calculation values with compensated seizure or confiscation of the specified substances and objects or without such.

(sanction of part two of Article 142 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Transportation in a trolleybus, tram, metro, bus, route taxi of explosive, easily flammable, poisonous and toxic substances and objects —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one half to one basic calculation value.

(sanction of part three of Article 142 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 143. Unpaid Baggage Transportation

Unpaid baggage transportation in trolleybus, tram, city and suburban bus and route taxi —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one-tenth of the base calculation value for each piece of baggage.

(sanction of Article 143 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Ticketless travel of a passenger, transportation without a ticket of children aged from ten to sixteen years:

a) on suburban trains —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one-fifth of the base calculation value;

(sanction of subparagraph "a" of part one of Article 144 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

b) on local and long-distance trains —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one-fifth of the base calculation value.

(sanction of part two of Article 144 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three base calculation values.

Ticketless travel on intercity bus —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one base calculation value.

Ticketless travel of a passenger in trolleybus, tram, city and suburban bus —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one-tenth of the base calculation value.

(sanction of part five of Article 144 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 145. Ticketless Transportation of Passengers

Ticketless transportation of passengers on air, railway transport —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from three to five base calculation values.

(sanction of part one of Article 145 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Ticketless transportation of passengers on water, road transport and urban electric transport —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from two to three base calculation values.

#### Article 146

Violation of Rules for Ensuring Safety of Cargo on Air, Railway, Water and Road Transport

Damage to rolling stock, containers, floating and other vehicles intended for cargo transportation, as well as transportation devices —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to three base calculation values.

(sanction of part one of Article 146 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Damage to seals and locking devices of aircraft, freight wagons, automobiles, automobile trailers, containers, holds and other cargo compartments of floating vessels, removal of seals from them, damage to individual cargo items and their packaging, packages, fences of freight yards, railway stations, freight automobile stations, container points (sites), ports (wharves) and warehouses that are used for performing operations related to cargo transportation, as well as presence without proper permission on the territory of freight yards, container points (sites), freight areas (sections), ports (wharves), locks and the above-mentioned warehouses —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one-half to two base calculation values.

(sanction of part two of Article 146 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

Violation of rules for ensuring safety of cargo on air, railway, water and road transport —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to seven base calculation values.

#### Article 1461

Failure of Carrier to Fulfill Obligation for Compulsory Insurance of Its Civil Liability

Failure of carrier to fulfill the obligation established by law for compulsory insurance of its civil liability for causing harm to life, health and (or) property of passengers —

#### Article 1553

Illegal alteration of the international unique identification code of a mobile device or the identification module of a subscriber device

Alteration of the international unique identification code of a mobile device without the permission of its lawful manufacturer, as well as the development, distribution of special programs or their use for this purpose —

entails the imposition of a fine from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units with confiscation of the instrument of commission of the offense.

Alteration or creation of a copy of the identification module of a subscriber device without the permission of its lawful manufacturer or lawful owner —

entails the imposition of a fine up to twenty basic calculation units with confiscation of the instrument of commission of the offense.

#### Article 1554. Violation of legislation in the sphere of crypto-asset circulation

Illegal acquisition, sale or exchange of crypto-assets, carrying out the activities of service providers in the sphere of crypto-asset circulation without obtaining a license in the established procedure —

entails administrative arrest for up to fifteen days or the imposition of a fine from twenty to thirty basic calculation units on persons in respect of whom, in accordance with this Code, administrative arrest may not be applied, with confiscation of crypto-assets and instruments of commission of the specified offenses.

Carrying out operations with anonymous crypto-assets by service providers in the sphere of crypto-asset circulation —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from thirty to forty basic calculation units.

#### Article 1555. Illegal conduct of mining activity

Conduct of mining activity in violation of the established procedure —

entails administrative arrest for up to five days or imposition of a fine from twenty to thirty basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, administrative arrest may not be applied, with confiscation of the instruments of commission of the said offense.

Engagement in mining of anonymous crypto-assets —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from thirty to forty basic calculation units with confiscation of crypto-assets and instruments of commission of the offense.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article, on a significant scale —

entails administrative arrest for up to ten days or imposition of a fine from thirty to fifty basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, administrative arrest may not be applied, with confiscation of the instruments of commission of the said offense.

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article, on a large scale —

entails administrative arrest for up to fifteen days or imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units on persons to whom, in accordance with this Code, administrative arrest may not be applied, with confiscation of the instruments of commission of the said offense.

(articles 1553, 1554 and 1555 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of 19 January 2024 No. 3RU-899 — National Database of Legislation, 19.01.2024, No. 03/24/899/0048 — Enters into force on 20 April 2024)

See previous version.

#### Article 159

Violation of the rules of maintenance and use of residential premises, rules and norms of technical operation of housing stock, rules of management of multi-apartment buildings

Violation of the rules of maintenance and use of residential premises, sanitary maintenance of common areas, violation of the rules and norms of technical operation of housing stock (except for cases provided for in parts two and three of this article), keeping of livestock in residential premises of multi-apartment residential buildings, as well as animals dangerous to human health —

entail imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

Violation of the rules of management of multi-apartment buildings —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of fifteen basic calculation units.

Alteration and (or) reconstruction of load-bearing and (or) enclosing structures in multi-apartment buildings in violation of legislation and regulatory documents in the field of technical regulation, including carrying out works on unauthorized erection of structures (facilities) in multi-apartment buildings and on land plots adjacent to multi-apartment buildings, —

See previous version.

entail imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of fifty, and on officials — in the amount of one hundred basic calculation units

(sanction of part three of article 159 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of 25 March 2025 No. 3RU-1050 — National Database of Legislation, 26.03.2025, No. 03/25/1050/0276. Date of entry into force — 27 June 2025)

A person who has committed for the first time the offense provided for in part three of this article (with respect to enclosing structures), shall be released from liability if he or she voluntarily eliminated the alteration made to the enclosing structures in a multi-apartment building or violations committed by their reconstruction, unauthorized erected structures (facilities) on the land plot adjacent to the multi-apartment building, as well as their consequences.

#### Article 165

Violation of legislation on licensing, permit and notification procedures

Submission to the authorized body of false or unreliable information about the fulfillment by a person carrying out activities (actions) in the notification procedure of the requirements and conditions established by legislation for such activities, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

Engaging in activities (actions) subject to implementation in the notification procedure without sending a notification —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

Engaging in activities (actions) for the implementation of which obtaining a permit document is required, without obtaining a permit document —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount from ten to fifteen, and on officials — from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

Engaging in activities subject to licensing without a license —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount from fifteen to twenty, and on officials — from twenty to twenty-five basic calculation units.

#### (Article 165

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 12, 2021 No. ZRU-721 — National Database of Legislation, 12.10.2021, No. 03/21/721/0952)

See previous version.

#### Article 1651

Production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation for the purpose of sale or sale of substandard or falsified medicines or medical devices, sale of medicines or medical devices outside pharmacies and their branches, as well as violation of the procedure for retail sale of medicines by prescription

Production, manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation for the purpose of sale or sale of substandard or falsified medicines or medical devices, as well as sale of medicines or medical devices outside pharmacies and their branches, —

entails imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units with confiscation of the instruments and objects of commission of the specified offenses.

Violation of the procedure for retail sale by prescription of medicines not containing potent substances, narcotic drugs, their analogues or psychotropic substances, —

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units.

Please note. Amendments have been made to the sanction of part two of Article 1651, entering into force on June 8, 2026, by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 6, 2026 No. ZRU-1120.

Violation of the procedure for retail sale by prescription of medicines containing potent substances, —

entails imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units.

#### (Article 1651

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of July 21, 2020 No. ZRU-629 — National Database of Legislation, 22.07.2020, No. 03/20/629/1087 — Enters into force on October 23, 2020)

Please note. Amendments have been made to the sanction of part three of Article 1651, entering into force on June 8, 2026, by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 6, 2026 No. ZRU-1120.

See previous version.

Part one of this article does not apply when committing actions related to medicines containing potent substances.

#### (Article 1651

supplemented by part four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 25, 2021 No. ZRU-711 — National Database of Legislation, 26.08.2021, No. 03/21/711/0825)

See previous version.

#### Article 166

Sale of goods (products) without documents certifying their quality, or violation of mandatory digital marking rules

Sale, as well as purchase for the purpose of sale by economic entities of food products and industrial goods (products) without documents certifying their quality and production mark, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculated values with confiscation of goods (products).

The same offense, committed repeatedly within a year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculated values with confiscation of goods (products).

Sale, as well as purchase for the purpose of sale of goods in whose trademark there is no information about the name and location of the manufacturer, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from ten to fifteen basic calculated values.

Violation of the rules of mandatory digital marking of goods (products) with identification means by manufacturers and importers for whom mandatory digital marking is established by legislation, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of one hundred basic calculated values.

#### (article 166

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 11, 2022 No. ZRU-758 — National Database of Legislation, 12.03.2022, No. 03/22/758/0207 — Enters into force from June 13, 2022)

#### Article 1711. Conclusion of a transaction contrary to the interests of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Conclusion of a knowingly unprofitable transaction, including one that resulted in the importation into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan of previously used, physically worn out, morally obsolete or equipment or technologies that do not meet modern requirements, as well as their installation and implementation, which caused damage to the interests of the republic in a significant amount, as well as the issuance of an expert opinion or other document by an official of an authorized state body or other organization, which resulted in the conclusion of such a transaction, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from seventy-five to one hundred basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of article 1711 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from one hundred to one hundred and fifty basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of article 1711 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 175

Violation of the established procedure for maintaining accounting and reporting of taxation objects, as well as payment discipline

Absence of accounting of profit (income) or other taxation objects or maintenance of such accounting with violations of the established procedure, if these actions led to understatement of the due amount of taxes or fees, as well as failure to submit other documents necessary for calculation and payment of taxes and fees, or failure to submit justification of identified discrepancies or amended tax reporting within the established period based on the results of desk tax audit, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation values.

See previous version.

Failure to submit timely tax reporting (with the exception of microfirms and small enterprises) —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one, and on officials — in the amount of ten basic calculation values.

Failure to submit timely tax reporting by microfirms and small enterprises —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation values.

entail imposition of a fine on officials in the amount of fifteen basic calculation values.

#### Article 1758. Violation of legislation on public procurement

Failure to comply with the requirements of legislation on the procedure for forming and posting public procurement plans, including schedule plans, as well as violation of the procedure for posting announcements of public procurement on a special information portal, violation of the procedure and deadlines for conducting mandatory discussion and violation of deadlines for posting the results of public procurement when carrying out public procurement in cases provided for by legislation on public procurement, —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

Inclusion in announcements of public procurement and procurement documentation of information, references and requirements prohibited by legislation and restricting competition, violation of procedures for approval of procurement documentation and formation of applications —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

Violation of requirements for determining suppliers of goods, works and services by conducting a tender, as well as making a decision to carry out public procurement from a single supplier or on the basis of direct contracts in those cases when the determination of a supplier of goods, performing work and providing services through a competitive procurement method is established in accordance with legislation on public procurement, —

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials from twenty to thirty basic calculation units.

#### Article 176. Illegal entrepreneurial activity

Carrying out entrepreneurial activity without state registration

entails the imposition of a fine from five to seven basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

(sanction of part one of Article 176 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article, with receipt of uncontrolled income in a significant amount —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from seven to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

(sanction of part two of Article 176 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Commission of the offense provided for in part one of this article, with receipt of uncontrolled income in a large amount —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from ten to fifty basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

(sanction of part three of Article 176 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

A person who has committed an offense for the first time is released from liability without confiscation of objects of the offense if, within thirty days from the moment of detection of the offense, they voluntarily compensated the damage caused to the state in the form of taxes and fees, ensured registration of the business entity and executed the necessary permit documents.

(part four of Article 176 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 11, 2022 No. ZRU-758 — National Database of Legislation, 12.03.2022, No. 03/22/758/0207 — Enters into force on June 13, 2022)

#### Article 178

Violation of competition legislation and consumer protection legislation

Failure to provide or untimely provision to consumer protection authorities of information, or provision to them of knowingly false information —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from one to three, and on officials — from three to five basic calculation units.

Evasion of execution, untimely or improper execution of instructions of the authorized state body in the field of competition to cease violations, to restore the initial situation, to eliminate violations of consumer rights —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

Evasion by a manufacturer of execution, untimely or improper execution of instructions of state administration bodies exercising control over the safety and quality of goods (works, services), —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

Violation of requirements against restriction of competition in mergers, accessions of economic entities and execution of transactions for acquisition of shares (stakes) —

shall entail imposition of a fine from one to three basic calculation units.

Conclusion of anticompetitive agreements and execution of coordinated actions, as well as abuse of dominant position in the commodity or financial market, and also violation of requirements against restriction of competition in tenders —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

Causing damage to consumers by goods (works, services) not meeting the requirements of normative documentation, as well as failure to provide consumers with information or provision of knowingly false information about goods (works, services), including misleading consumers regarding consumer properties or quality of goods, substitution of goods with other products similar to them, intentional alteration of properties and quality of products, and also production, import and sale of falsified food products —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from three to seven, and on officials — from seven to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

Acceptance for sale and sale of goods with expired shelf life, as well as goods without indication of production date and shelf life, for which legislation provides for mandatory indication of production date and shelf life, —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from three to ten, and on officials — from seven to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

Failure to provide or untimely provision to the authorized state body in the field of competition of information, or provision to it of knowingly false information —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from three to ten, and on officials — from ten to twenty basic calculation units.

Unlawful restriction of free movement and sale of raw materials, goods and provision of services by an official of state authorities and administration —

shall entail imposition of a fine in the amount from thirty to fifty basic calculation units.

See previous version.

Adoption of decisions restricting competition by state institutions,  None
None

(disposition of part ten of Article 178 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 7, 2025 No. ZRU-1025 — National Database of Legislation, 07.02.2025, No. 03/25/1025/0116. Date of entry into force — May 8, 2025)

shall entail imposition of a fine in the amount from thirty to fifty basic calculation units.

Commission of offenses provided for by parts one through seven of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to twenty basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

Commission of the offense provided for by part eight of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

shall entail imposition of a fine on citizens from ten to twenty, and on officials from twenty to thirty basic calculation units.

Commission of offenses provided for by parts nine and ten of this article, repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

shall entail imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units.

shall entail imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

Violation of legislation on funded pension provision for citizens

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from two to four basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of article 1792 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from three to five basic calculation values.

(sanction of part three of article 1792 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to seven basic calculation values.

#### Article 1793

Violation of legislation on counteracting legalization of proceeds from criminal activity, financing of terrorism and financing of proliferation of weapons of mass destruction

Violation of requirements for organization and implementation of internal control, documentation and storage of results of due diligence of clients, identification, assessment and documentary recording of risks, refusal to conduct a transaction, as well as failure to provide, untimely provision or provision of unreliable information to the specially authorized state body about suspicious transactions, failure to comply with the requirement of legislation regarding freezing of funds or other property or suspension of a transaction, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from fifteen to thirty basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of article 1793 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Violation by relevant controlling, licensing and registration bodies of the established procedure for monitoring and control over compliance with internal control rules, as well as unlawful demand, receipt or disclosure of information constituting commercial, banking or other secrets protected by law, in connection with counteracting legalization of proceeds from criminal activity, financing of terrorism and financing of proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from twenty to forty basic calculation values.

(sanction of part two of article 1793 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Commission of an offense provided for by part one or two of this article, repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from thirty to fifty basic calculation values.

(sanction of part three of article 1793 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Failure to comply with instructions of authorized bodies for regulation of exchange activity on elimination of violations —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation values.

(sanction of part one of article 1794 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails imposition of a fine on officials from ten to twenty basic calculation values.

#### Article 1795. Intentional insolvency

Intentional insolvency, that is intentional creation or increase of insolvency, committed by an official, founder (participant) or owner of property of a legal entity or natural person or individual entrepreneur in personal interests or interests of other persons, resulting in insolvency of this legal entity or natural person or individual entrepreneur, causing damage to creditors, —

entails imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred fifty basic calculation values.
