## Chapter XIV

ADMINISTRATIVE LIABILITY FOR OFFENSES ENCROACHING ON JUSTICE

#### Article 180. Showing disrespect to the court

Disrespect to the court, expressed in malicious evasion of appearance in court by a witness,  None
None
None

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to three basic calculation values.

(sanction of article 180 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 181. Failure to take measures on private ruling (resolution) of the court

Leaving without consideration by an official of a private ruling (resolution) of the court or failure to take measures to eliminate violations of law indicated therein, as well as untimely response to a private ruling (resolution) —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from three to five basic calculation values.

(sanction of article 181 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 182. Obstruction of appearance in court of a people's assessor

Obstruction by an official of appearance in court of a people's assessor to perform duties assigned to him —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from one to three basic calculation values.

(sanction of article 182 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

## Chapter XV

ADMINISTRATIVE LIABILITY FOR OFFENSES ENCROACHING ON PUBLIC ORDER

#### Article 183. Petty Hooliganism

Petty hooliganism, that is, intentional disregard for the rules of conduct in society, expressed in obscene language in public places, offensive harassment of citizens and other similar actions that violate public order and the peace of citizens, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from three to five basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

(sanction of Article 183 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

#### Article 1842

Illegal Production, Storage, Import or Distribution of Materials of Religious Content

Illegal production, storage, import into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan for the purpose of distribution or distribution of materials of religious content, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from twenty to one hundred, and on officials — from fifty to one hundred and fifty basic calculation units with confiscation of materials and corresponding means for their production and distribution.

(sanction of Article 1842 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 1843

Production, Storage or Distribution of Materials Promoting National, Racial, Ethnic or Religious Hatred

Production, storage for the purpose of distribution or distribution of materials promoting national, racial, ethnic or religious hatred, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from fifty to one hundred, and on officials — from one hundred to one hundred and fifty basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of materials and corresponding means for their production and distribution.

(sanction of Article 1843 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 1844

Being in Public Places in a Form That Does Not Allow Identification of the Person

Being in public places with a covered face to a degree that does not allow identification of the person, except for cases of being with a covered face on the basis of medical indication or in special headgear worn to ensure safety in road traffic, or being with a covered face in cases related to labor and official activities or participants of sports and cultural events during their conduct, as well as other cases of being with a covered face directly permitted by legislation, —

entails the imposition of a fine from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

#### (Article 1844

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 31, 2023 No. 3RU-874 — National Database of Legislation, 01.11.2023, No. 03/23/874/0818)

See previous version.

#### Article 185. Use of Weapons in Violation of the Established Procedure

Shooting from pneumatic, throwing, signal weapons in violation of the established procedure, as well as the use of cold bladed weapons (hunting knives, hunting stilettos and daggers, rapiers, swords and sabers) in violation of the established procedure —

entail the imposition of a fine in the amount from five to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of weapons and ammunition or without such.

Shooting from firearms in violation of the established procedure —

entails the imposition of a fine in the amount from ten to fifteen basic calculation units with confiscation of weapons and ammunition or without such.

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force from 2

entails the imposition of a fine from fifteen to twenty-five basic calculation units with confiscation of the objects of the offense.

Illegal sale or import into the Republic of Uzbekistan of pyrotechnic products, —

entails the imposition of a fine from twenty-five to fifty basic calculation units with confiscation of the objects of the offense.

entails the imposition of a fine from one to five basic calculation units with confiscation of the objects of the offense or without such.

#### Article 1853

Illegal Circulation of Objects Whose Circulation as Civilian and Service Weapons Is Prohibited on the Territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Illegal production, repair, sale, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, collecting, exhibiting, use, import into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan and export from the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as transit through the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan of cold bladed weapons and knives with a blade or edge length of more than ninety millimeters that are automatically extracted from the handle when a button or lever is pressed and fixed by them or extended by gravity or accelerated movement and automatically fixed, as well as objects of striking-crushing and throwing action specially adapted for use as weapons (blackjacks, brass knuckles, shurikens, boomerangs and other objects), —

entails the imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of the specified objects.

(Articles 1852 and 1853 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 5, 2020 No. ZRU-645 — National Database of Legislation, 06.11.2020, No. 03/20/645/1469)

See previous version.

#### Article 1854

Illegal Import, Sale, Acquisition, Storage or Use of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles

Illegal import, sale, acquisition, storage or use of unmanned aerial vehicles, components and spare parts for them, —

entails the imposition of a fine from ten to twenty basic calculation units with confiscation of unmanned aerial vehicles, components and spare parts for them.

A person who has committed the actions provided for in part one of this article is released from administrative liability if he voluntarily surrendered unmanned aerial vehicles, components and spare parts for them.

Production for the purpose of sale or sale by citizens of strong alcoholic beverages of home production

Production for the purpose of sale or sale by citizens of strong alcoholic beverages of home production —

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of sale and means of production.

#### Article 1861

Illegal circulation or production of ethyl alcohol, alcoholic and tobacco products, as well as devices for consumption of tobacco and nicotine

Illegal circulation of ethyl alcohol, alcoholic and tobacco products, as well as tobacco heating systems, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

Production, acquisition, storage, transportation, sale, import into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan or export from the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan of tobacco products, electronic nicotine delivery systems, including electronic cigarettes, the circulation of which is prohibited on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as production of electronic nicotine delivery systems, including electronic cigarettes, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount from one hundred to two hundred basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

Acquisition or storage of tobacco products, electronic nicotine delivery systems, including electronic cigarettes, the circulation of which is prohibited on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the value of which does not exceed the amount of one basic calculation unit, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculation unit.

Commission of an offense provided for by part three of this article, repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine from two to five basic calculation units with confiscation of objects of the offense.

A person who committed actions provided for by part two of this article is released from administrative liability if he voluntarily appeared with a confession to the authorities and surrendered tobacco products, electronic nicotine delivery systems, including electronic cigarettes, the circulation of which is prohibited on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 192. Violation of requirements for combating domestic noise

Violation of peace and normal rest of citizens during nighttime — from 23:00 to 6:00 hours — that is, loud singing, playing musical instruments, sounding signals, use of televisions, radio receivers, tape recorders and other sound-reproducing equipment set at increased volume in apartments, entrances and yards of residential buildings, on streets, conducting in residential premises and outside them works accompanied by noise not caused by urgent necessity, as well as commission of other actions violating requirements for combating domestic noise, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one third, and on officials — from one third to one half of a basic calculation unit.

(sanction of part one of article 192 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of one half, and on officials — from one half to one basic calculation unit.

#### Article 1921

Non-compliance with requirements for the procedure for conducting weddings, family celebrations, ceremonies and rituals

Non-compliance by a person conducting weddings, family celebrations, ceremonies and rituals with requirements for the procedure for conducting these events —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of ten basic calculation units.

Non-compliance by an official of wedding houses, cafes and restaurants with requirements for the procedure for conducting weddings, family celebrations, ceremonies and rituals —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of thirty basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part two of this article repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifty basic calculation units.

#### Article 1931

Bribery of an employee of a state body, organization with state participation or citizens' self-government body

Knowingly unlawful provision to an employee of a state body,  None
None
None

(sanction of part one of Article 1931 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

An organization with state participation means:

a commercial organization in whose charter fund there is a state share;

a non-commercial organization created or established wholly or partially by a state body or state organization.

An employee of a state body, organization with state participation or citizens' self-government body means a person carrying out labor activity on the basis of an employment or civil law contract, not possessing the characteristics of an official.

#### Article 1932

Knowingly unlawful receipt by an employee of a state body, organization with state participation or citizens' self-government body of material valuables or property benefit for performance or non-performance in the interests of the person carrying out the bribery of a certain action which the employee should have or could have committed using his official position, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine up to thirty basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of Article 1932 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

An organization with state participation means:

a non-commercial organization created or established wholly or partially by a state body or state organization.

An employee of a state body, organization with state participation or citizens' self-government body means a person carrying out labor activity on the basis of an employment or civil law contract, not possessing the characteristics of an official.

#### Article 1934. Failure to comply with requirements of legislation on conflict of interest

Failure by an official or employee of a state body or other organization to report the existence of a conflict of interest provided for by legislation on conflict of interest —

entails imposition of a fine from three to five basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units.

Failure to take measures to resolve an actual or potential conflict of interest of which an official of a state body or other organization became aware, provided for by legislation on conflict of interest, —

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

Commission of offenses provided for in parts one and three of this article in the sphere of public procurement —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from twenty to thirty basic calculation units.

#### Article 1982

Violation of legislation on the execution of judicial acts and acts of other bodies

Violation of legislation on the execution of judicial acts and acts of other bodies, that is, submission of false information about the income and property status of a debtor, loss of an enforcement document, violation of the procedure for execution, accounting and storage of enforcement documents sent for deduction from wages and payments equated thereto, as well as failure by a debtor to report a new place of work, study, place of receipt of pension and other income —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from fifteen to twenty basic calculation units.

entails the imposition of a fine from seven to ten basic calculation units.

entails the imposition of a fine from ten to twenty basic calculation units.

entails the imposition of a fine from five to fifteen basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

#### Article 2002. Unlawful Seizure of a Land Plot

Unlawful seizure of a land plot —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from one hundred to one hundred fifty basic calculation units.

The same offense that resulted in demolition of buildings, other structures, constructions or plantings or their parts on the seized land plot, without prior and full compensation of the market value of the specified property, as well as losses caused to its owner in connection with such seizure, which caused minor damage, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from one hundred fifty to two hundred basic calculation units.

Minor damage means an amount within the range from thirty to one hundred basic calculation units.

#### Article 214. Violation of certification and conformity assessment rules

Violation of certification and conformity assessment rules, —

entails imposition of a fine from two to three base calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials from five to ten base calculation units.

Violation by conformity assessment bodies of conformity assessment rules that resulted in release of products not complying with requirements of technical regulations, or causing damage to a natural or legal person who provided products for conformity assessment, including loss of profit as a result of unjustified refusal to issue a certificate of conformity, suspension or termination of a certificate of conformity or declaration of conformity, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from ten to forty base calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article repeatedly within a year after application of administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from forty to eighty base calculation units.

#### Article 2152

Obstruction of lawful activities of the Chamber of Accounts of the Republic of Uzbekistan, expressed in evasion of execution or untimely execution of instructions and representations of the Chamber of Accounts of the Republic of Uzbekistan, failure to provide access to databases and information, provision of knowingly false information, as well as interference in the activities of the Chamber of Accounts of the Republic of Uzbekistan and influence in any form on its officials with the purpose of obstructing the lawful performance of assigned tasks and functions —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

(sanction of article 2152 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

entails imposition of a fine on officials from three to five basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from three to five basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

#### Article 2158

Violation of legislation in the sphere of leasing state property

Provision for lease of state property without concluding in the established procedure a lease agreement, unjustified obstruction of the lessee in accepting the leased state property, access to the territory of the object of lease relations and its use, or provision for lease of state property with indication in the lease agreement of incorrect parameters and quantity of state property being leased, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from five to ten basic calculation units.

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after application of administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

#### Article 2159

Violation of legislation in the sphere of provision of social services and assistance, as well as failure to provide benefits and social guarantees established by legislative acts

Violation of legislation in the sphere of provision of social services and assistance, expressed in refusal to accept an application from an applicant, violation of the procedure and deadlines for consideration of applications in the provision of social services and assistance, demanding from the applicant documents not provided for by legislation, failure to provide benefits and social guarantees established by legislative acts for persons in need of social protection, as well as non-compliance with national standards for the provision of social services, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials and employees in the amount of three to five basic calculation units.

The same offense, committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine on officials and employees in the amount of five to ten basic calculation units.

#### Article 220. Violation of the Rules for Circulation of Weapons and Ammunition

Violation of the rules for sale, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, collecting, exhibiting of civilian, award, collectible, exhibition weapons and ammunition by individuals having the appropriate permit from internal affairs bodies, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five to seven base calculation units or confiscation of weapons and ammunition for compensation.

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten to fifteen base calculation units with confiscation of weapons and ammunition or without such.

Violation of the rules for storage, carrying and use of service weapons and ammunition by officials of state bodies and employees of legal entities with special service tasks having the right to use service weapons and ammunition, as well as violation of the rules for acquisition, transfer, accounting, sale, transportation, storage, collecting, exhibiting of weapons and ammunition, main parts of firearms by responsible employees of legal entities having the appropriate permits from internal affairs bodies, who are assigned the duties of ensuring the safety, issuance-acceptance and accounting in the established manner of weapons and ammunition, main parts of firearms, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five to ten base calculation units.

Commission of the offense provided for in part three of this article, repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten to fifteen base calculation units.

Violation of the rules for acceptance, accounting and sale of weapons and ammunition, main parts of firearms by officials of legal entities having a license for sale of civilian and service weapons and ammunition, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen base calculation units.

Installation on civilian and service weapons for which the appropriate permits from internal affairs bodies have been issued, of devices for silent shooting and night vision sights (except for hunting sights) —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of five base calculation units with confiscation of devices for silent shooting and night vision sights.

#### Article 2201. Illegal Circulation of Weapons

Sale, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, collecting, exhibiting, use, import into the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan or export from the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as transit through the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan of pneumatic, signal, throwing weapons, self-defense weapons without the appropriate permits from internal affairs bodies —

entail imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of fifteen to thirty, and on officials — from twenty-five to fifty base calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of weapons.

Production, repair, sale of pneumatic, signal, cold bladed, throwing weapons, self-defense weapons without the appropriate licenses from internal affairs bodies —

entail imposition of a fine on citizens in the amount of fifty to one hundred, and on officials — from one hundred to one hundred fifty base calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days with confiscation of weapons.

#### (article 2201

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on December 2, 2022)

See previous version.

A person who committed the actions provided for in parts one and two of this article is released from administrative liability if he voluntarily surrendered pneumatic, signal, cold bladed, throwing weapons and self-defense weapons.

#### Article 223. Violation of the Rules of the Passport System

Residence of a citizen of the Republic of Uzbekistan without a passport or identification ID-card, with a passport or identification ID-card that needs to be replaced due to expiration, as well as residence of a foreign citizen and a stateless person permanently residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan without an identification ID-card (residence permit), with an identification ID-card (residence permit) that needs to be replaced due to expiration, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of one-half of the basic calculation value.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part one of this article repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation values.

Damage or loss of a passport or identification ID-card or a biometric passport of a citizen of the Republic of Uzbekistan for travel abroad, damage or loss of an identification ID-card (residence permit) or travel document of a stateless person permanently residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as damage or loss of an identification ID-card (residence permit) of a foreign citizen permanently residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of one basic calculation value.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part three of this article repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation values.

Residence of a citizen of the Republic of Uzbekistan, a foreign citizen and a stateless person permanently residing in the Republic of Uzbekistan without registration at the place of residence or place of stay, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of three basic calculation values.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part five of this article repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation values.

Allowing by persons responsible for compliance with the rules of the passport system the residence of citizens of the Republic of Uzbekistan, foreign citizens and stateless persons without identity documents, as well as allowing by persons the residence in residential premises occupied by them of citizens of the Republic of Uzbekistan, foreign citizens and stateless persons without identity documents, without registration at the place of residence or place of stay, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of five basic calculation values.

Commission of the same offense provided for in part seven of this article repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

shall result in the imposition of a fine in the amount of ten basic calculation values.

A person shall not be held administratively liable for offenses provided for in this article if such person:

1) has not reached eighteen years of age;

2) is older than sixty years of age;

3) is on full state support;

4) has suffered as a result of emergency situations of natural and man-made character, including domestic disasters (fires, collapses of residential or non-residential premises, fires of vehicles) on the territory

of the Republic of Uzbekistan or a foreign state (for committing offenses provided for in parts one through six of this article);

5) was unlawfully convicted or was involved in a case as an accused, defendant, whose passport or identification ID-card was suspended by court order (for committing offenses provided for in part one of this article);

6) is serving a sentence in a penal institution or applied within one month for the issuance of an identification ID-card after serving a sentence in a penal institution (for committing offenses provided for in parts one through six of this article);

7) reported a passport or identification ID-card (residence permit) that has become unusable or their loss as a result of unlawful actions of third parties or the commission of a crime (in case these facts are established in the manner prescribed by law);

8) reported damage to a passport or identification ID-card (residence permit) or their loss during the performance of official duties or the performance of civic duty, as well as due to force majeure, extreme necessity or necessary defense (in case these facts are established in the manner prescribed by law).

A person may be exempted from liability for offenses provided for in this article in other cases provided for by law.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to ten basic calculation units.

(sanction of part one of article 2241 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

The same offense committed repeatedly within one year after the application of an administrative penalty, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on citizens from five to ten, and on officials — from ten to fifteen basic calculation units.

(sanction of part two of article 2241 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

A foreign citizen or stateless person in case of non-payment of a fine for violation of the regime at checkpoints across the State Border of the Republic of Uzbekistan, established by part one of this article, shall be subject to administrative expulsion beyond the limits of the Republic of Uzbekistan with subsequent restriction of the right to enter the Republic of Uzbekistan for a period of one year.

A foreign citizen or stateless person in case of repeated violation of the regime at checkpoints across the State Border of the Republic of Uzbekistan, committed within one year after the application of an administrative penalty and non-payment of the fine established by part two of this article, shall be subject to administrative expulsion beyond the limits of the Republic of Uzbekistan with subsequent restriction of the right to enter the Republic of Uzbekistan for a period of three years.

#### Article 225. Violation of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

See previous version.

Violation by a foreign citizen or stateless person of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan, that is, residing on travel documents whose validity period has expired, failure to comply with the established procedure for registration at the place of stay, movement or choice of place of residence, evasion of departure upon expiration of the period of stay, as well as failure to comply with the rules of transit passage through the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan for up to ten days, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of twenty-five basic calculation units.

entails imposition of a fine on the official in the amount of twenty-five basic calculation units.

Failure by a person who invited foreign citizens and stateless persons to the Republic of Uzbekistan on private matters to take measures to ensure registration at the place of stay of foreign citizens and stateless persons, as well as their departure upon expiration of the period of stay determined for them, as well as provision by a person of living space, vehicles or rendering of other services to foreign citizens and stateless persons who arrived on private matters, when this knowingly entails violation of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan, —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of fifteen basic calculation units.

A foreign citizen and stateless person under the age of eighteen years shall not be subject to administrative liability for violation of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan.

A foreign citizen and stateless person in case of non-payment of a fine for violations of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan established by parts one through three of this article shall be subject to expulsion beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan with subsequent restriction of the right to enter the Republic of Uzbekistan for a period of one year.

A foreign citizen and stateless person in case of repeated violation of the rules of stay in the Republic of Uzbekistan committed within one year after application of an administrative penalty shall be subject to expulsion beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan with subsequent restriction of the right to enter the Republic of Uzbekistan for a period of three years.

See previous version.

#### Article 2251

Failure to comply with a court order on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Failure by foreign citizens and stateless persons to comply with court orders on administrative expulsion beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan adopted in respect of them —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from seventy-five to one hundred basic calculation units or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

#### Article 227. Violation of the customs control zone regime

#### Article 239

Violation of the procedure for carrying out activities by non-governmental non-profit organizations

See previous version.

Carrying out activities by non-governmental non-profit organizations (with the exception of religious organizations), as well as their representative offices and branches without undergoing state registration —

entails imposition of a fine in the amount of ten to twenty-five base calculation values.

Participation of representative offices and branches of international and foreign non-governmental non-profit organizations whose parent organization is located outside the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as their employees who are foreign citizens and stateless persons, and employees of other non-governmental non-profit organizations who are foreign citizens and stateless persons, in any political activity, or in other activity not corresponding to the goals and objectives defined by the charters of the said non-governmental non-profit organizations, as well as their financing of practical actions and events conducted by political parties and mass movements, initiating the creation of such organizations and supporting the creation of such organizations —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from fifteen to thirty, and on officials — from thirty to fifty base calculation values or administrative arrest for up to fifteen days.

Use by non-governmental non-profit organizations of symbols not registered in the established procedure, —

entails imposition on officials of a fine in the amount of three to seven base calculation values.

Non-compliance by non-governmental non-profit organizations (with the exception of political parties), their representative offices and branches with the procedure for notification of holding events or holding events prohibited by legislation, or refusal of free access to a representative of the registering body to events held by non-governmental non-profit organizations, —

entails imposition on officials of a fine in the amount of twenty to forty base calculation values.

Receipt by non-governmental non-profit organizations of monetary funds and property from foreign states, international and foreign organizations, citizens of foreign states or on their behalf from other persons without the consent of the registering body, or failure to publish in the established procedure information on the use of monetary funds and property received from these sources, as well as information on the result of a project implemented on the territory of the Republic of Uzbekistan at the expense of these sources, —

entails imposition on officials of a fine in the amount of ten to twenty-five base calculation values.

Failure to submit, untimely submission or submission in  None  None
None  None
None  None

entails imposition on officials of a fine in the amount of ten to twenty-five base calculation values.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from twenty to forty base calculation values with confiscation of monetary funds in the amount exceeding the maximum amount of expenses.

#### Article 2392. Violation of requirements of legislation on charity

Violation of the procedure for collecting charitable donations —

entails imposition of a fine on citizens from three to five, and on officials — from five to seven basic calculation units.

Violation of the procedure for installation and use of a donation box —

entails imposition on citizens of a fine in the amount from three to five, and on officials — from five to seven basic calculation units, with confiscation of items that are instruments of committing the offense.

Exceeding by a charitable organization of the amount of financial resources established by legislation, spent on its maintenance (including all expenses related to its activities) during the financial year, if these actions do not contain signs of a crime, —

entails imposition on officials of a fine in the amount from three to five basic calculation units.

■ LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation.

#### Article 240. Violation of legislation on religious organizations

See previous version.

Carrying out illegal religious activity, evasion by leaders of religious organizations from registration of the charter, organization and conduct by ministers of worship and members of religious organizations of special children's and youth meetings, as well as labor, literary and other circles and groups not related to performance of worship, and equally engagement in another type of activity or conduct of events by a religious organization not provided for by its charter, —

(disposition of part one of Article 240 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 31, 2023 No. ZRU-874 — National Database of Legislation, 01.11.2023, No. 03/23/874/0818)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units or administrative arrest up to fifteen days.

(sanction of part one of Article 240 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from fifty to one hundred basic calculation units or administrative arrest up to fifteen days.

(sanction of part two of Article 240 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

Teaching religious doctrines without special religious education and without permission of the central governing body of a religious organization, and equally teaching religious doctrines in a private manner —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from five to ten basic calculation units or administrative arrest up to fifteen days.

(sanction of Article 241 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

## Chapter XVI

ADMINISTRATIVE LIABILITY FOR OBSTRUCTION, ILLEGAL INTERFERENCE IN ENTREPRENEURIAL ACTIVITY AND OTHER OFFENSES ENCROACHING ON THE RIGHTS AND LEGITIMATE INTERESTS OF ECONOMIC ENTITIES

#### Article 2411. Violation of the right of private property

Causing damage to private owners by an official or employee of a controlling, law enforcement or other state body and state organization through violation of their rights, that is, illegal restriction and (or) deprivation of the right of ownership, encroachment on private property, imposition on the owner of knowingly unacceptable conditions, including unjustified demand for transfer of property or property rights, as well as seizure of property or coercion of him to renounce the right to his own property in the absence of signs of petty theft —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from forty to eighty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2411 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2412

Violation of the procedure for conducting inspections and audits of financial and economic activity of business entities

Violation of the established procedure for conducting inspections and audits of financial and economic activity of business entities, and equally initiation and

(or) conduct of illegal inspections of activity of business entities —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from forty to eighty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2412 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2413

Illegal suspension of activity of business entities and (or) operations on their bank accounts

Illegal suspension of activity of business entities and (or) operations on their bank accounts —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials from forty to eighty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2413 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2414

Forced involvement of business entities in charity and other events

Forced involvement of business entities by an official or employee of a controlling, law enforcement or other state body and state organization in charity and other events related to diversion of monetary resources and other material valuables, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2414 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2415

Violation of legislation on licensing and legislation on permitting procedures

Violation of the established procedure and terms for issuance of a license and other documents of permitting nature, including illegal introduction of new types of licenses and permitting procedures —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials and employees from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2415 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2416

Unlawful refusal, non-application or obstruction in application of benefits and preferences

Unlawful refusal, non-application or obstruction in application of benefits and preferences granted to business entities, —

See previous version.

entails imposition of a fine on officials and employees from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2416 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2417

Unjustified delay in the issuance of funds to economic entities and other organizations

See previous version.

Unjustified delay by an official or employee of a bank in the issuance to economic entities and other organizations of funds for the payment of wages, pensions, benefits, stipends and other payments equated to them —

(disposition of Article 2417 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 26, 2016 No. 3RU-416 — SZ RU, 2016, No. 52, Art. 597)

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2417 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2418

Unlawful demand for information on the availability of funds in the accounts of business entities

Demanding information on the availability of funds in the accounts of business entities in cases not provided for by legislation, —

See previous version.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials and employees from forty to eighty basic calculation units.

(sanction of Article 2418 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

#### Article 2419

Unlawful transfer or withdrawal of funds or their untimely return

Unlawful transfer or withdrawal of funds from bank accounts of business entities without their consent or untimely return of excessively collected funds in the absence of signs of petty theft, —

entails the imposition of a fine from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

Article 24110. Demanding documents from business entities in violation of legislation

See previous version.

Demanding from business entities statistical, financial, tax and other reports not included in the unified list of reports and statistical data submitted by business entities to state bodies, as well as other documents not provided for by legislation, including excessive documents when opening a bank account, or demanding reports included in this Unified List contrary to the established deadlines and procedure for submission —

(disposition of part one of Article 24110 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 21, 2024 No. 3RU-979 — National Database of Legislation, 22.10.2024, No. 03/24/979/0836)

entails the imposition of a fine on officials and employees from twenty to forty basic calculation units.

entails the imposition of a fine on officials and employees from forty to sixty basic calculation units.

PART THREE BODIES (OFFICIALS) AUTHORIZED TO CONSIDER CASES OF ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSES

## Chapter XVII. GENERAL PROVISIONS

#### Article 242

Bodies (officials) authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses

Cases of administrative offenses are considered by:

See previous version.

1) district (city) courts for criminal cases, and in cases provided for by law, economic courts and courts for civil cases;

(paragraph 1 of Article 242 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

(paragraph 2 of Article 242 excluded by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 20, 2014 No. 3RU-365 — SZ RU, 2014, No. 4, Art. 45)

See previous version.

3) district (city) commissions on children's issues;

(paragraph 3 of Article 242 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

See previous version.

4) bodies (officials) of internal affairs, bodies (officials) of state inspections and other bodies (officials) authorized by this Code.

(paragraph 4 of part one of Article 242 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 23, 2019 No. 3RU-542 — National Database of Legislation, 24.05.2019, No. 03/19/542/317)

See previous version.

See previous version.

#### Article 243. Powers of commissions on children's issues

(title of Article 243 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

See previous version.

Commissions on children's issues have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses under their jurisdiction in the presence of at least half of the members of their composition, apply administrative penalties provided for by the Special Part of this Code within the limits of the powers granted to them and make decisions by a simple majority of votes.

(text of Article 243 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

#### Article 244. Powers of officials

The list of officials considering cases of administrative offenses on behalf of the bodies mentioned in paragraph 4 of Article 242 of this Code is determined by legislation.

Officials authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses may apply administrative penalties provided for by the Special Part of this Code within the limits of the powers granted to them and only in the performance of official duties.

by persons, with the exception of cases provided for by Articles 50, 51 of this Code, None
None

#### Article 2451. Economic Courts

See previous version.

Economic courts, in cases of establishing administrative offenses in the course of resolving economic disputes, consider cases of administrative offenses provided for in part four of Article 175, Articles 180 and 181 of this Code.

#### Article 2452. Justice Bodies

Justice bodies have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Article 2155 of this Code. The following have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of a fine on behalf of justice bodies:

the Minister of Justice of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

the Head of the Department of Public Services of the Ministry of Justice of the Republic of Uzbekistan;

the Minister of Justice of the Republic of Karakalpakstan and his deputies, heads of justice departments of regions and the city of Tashkent and their deputies.

See previous version.

Officials of justice bodies, upon establishing offenses provided for in part three of Article 2392, in Articles 1971, 1983, 1984, 2391, 2391 of this Code, draw up a protocol on an administrative offense.

LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation. Part two of Article 2452 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 21, 2025 No. 3RU-1083. See official text in Uzbek language. Date of entry into force of Article 2392 — November 23, 2026.

The protocol on an administrative offense is sent for consideration to court in the procedure established by Article 282 of this Code.

(text of Article 2452 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 16, 2024 No. 3RU-896 — National Database of Legislation, January 16, 2024, No. 03/24/896/0037)

See previous version.

Bodies of the Bureau of Compulsory Enforcement under the Prosecutor General's Office of the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

Bodies of the Bureau of Compulsory Enforcement under the Prosecutor General's Office of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Article 1981 and parts one through four of Article 1982 of this Code.

(part one of Article 2453 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2022 No. 3RU-762 — National Database of Legislation, April 12, 2022, No. 03/22/762/0290)

See previous version.

The Chief State Bailiff of the Republic of Uzbekistan, chief state bailiffs of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions and the city of Tashkent, their deputies, as well as heads of district (city) departments of the Bureau of Compulsory Enforcement under the Prosecutor General's Office of the Republic of Uzbekistan have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties on behalf of the Bureau of Compulsory Enforcement under the Prosecutor General's Office of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(part two of Article 2453 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of July 4, 2023 No. 3RU-851 — National Database of Legislation, July 5, 2023, No. 03/23/851/0447)

See previous version.

#### Article 2454. Courts on Civil Cases

Courts on civil cases, in cases of establishing administrative offenses in the course of proceedings in civil cases, consider cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 180 and 181 of this Code.

#### Article 2456. Accounts Chamber of the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

Officials of the Accounts Chamber of the Republic of Uzbekistan, upon establishing offenses provided for in Article 61 (in part of petty theft of property of budgetary organizations, recipients of budgetary funds, state targeted funds and other funds created in the established procedure by appropriation or embezzlement), Articles 1752, 1757, 1758 and 2152 of this Code, draw up a protocol on an administrative offense.

#### Article 2457

Bodies of the Inspectorate for Control over the Agro-Industrial Complex under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Bodies of the Inspectorate for Control over the Agro-Industrial Complex under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 65, 68 (in part of agricultural lands), Article 69 (in part of boundary markers of agricultural land boundaries), Article 87 (in part of agricultural machinery, land reclamation and road construction), Articles 89 and 89 (in part of agriculture and water management), Articles 1041, 1042,

112, parts five, six and seven of Article 125, Articles 135, 139, 140 (in part of agricultural machinery, land reclamation and road construction), Articles 200, 212, 213, 214, 22714 (in part of agriculture) of this Code.

The head of the Inspectorate and his deputies, heads and deputy heads of territorial departments of the Inspectorate and departments of the Inspectorate of districts (city of Kuvasay) have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties on behalf of the Inspectorate for Control over the Agro-Industrial Complex under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 2458

Business Ombudsman under the President of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Protection of Rights and Legitimate Interests of Business Entities and Employees of His Office

The Business Ombudsman under the President of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Protection of Rights and Legitimate Interests of Business Entities has jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 43 (in part of appeals of business entities), 2002 (in part of violation of rights of business entities), 2411 — 2414, 2416, 2418, 24110 of this Code.

Deputies and chief inspectors of the office of the Business Ombudsman under the President of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Protection of Rights and Legitimate Interests of Business Entities have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of a fine on behalf of the Business Ombudsman under the President of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Protection of Rights and Legitimate Interests of Business Entities.

Authorized Bodies in the Field of Licensing, Permitting and Notification Procedures

Authorized bodies in the field of licensing, permitting and notification procedures, as well as heads and deputies of their territorial subdivisions, upon detecting offenses provided for in Article 165 of this Code, draw up a protocol on an administrative offense.

The protocol on an administrative offense is sent for consideration to court in the procedure established by Article 282 of this Code.

Uzbekistan

#### Article 2481. Bodies of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

The bodies of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in part one of Article 47, Articles 561, 63, parts one and four of Article 72 (regarding pollution or contamination of waters, violation of water protection regime in watersheds, violation of requirements on conditions for permissible discharge of wastewater into water bodies, with the exception of offenses concerning groundwater), parts one and four of Article 79, Article 88 (with the exception of non-compliance with requirements for protection of atmospheric air during bitumen melting), part one of Article 90, Article 91 (regarding violation of environmental protection requirements during collection and transportation of industrial, household and other waste), parts one, two and three of Article 911, Articles 912, 161, part one of Article 187, Articles 188, 1882, 192 of this Code.

The following officials have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and impose administrative penalties in the form of fines on behalf of the bodies of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan:

the head of the Department for Organization of Public Order Protection of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan, his deputy and heads of divisions;

the head of the Department for Coordination of Inquiry of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputy;

the head of the Main Department of Protection of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

the head of the Department for Protection of State Bodies Facilities of the Main Department of Protection of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

#### Article 2482

Border Troops of the State Security Service of the Republic of Uzbekistan

See previous version.

The Border Troops of the State Security Service of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Article 224, part one of Article 2241, parts one, two and three of Article 225 of this Code.

(part one of Article 2482 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated April 25, 2023 No. 3RU-832 — National Database of Legislation, 26.04.2023, No. 03/23/832/0228)

The heads of units of the Border Troops of the State Security Service of the Republic of Uzbekistan and their deputies have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and impose administrative penalties in the form of fines on behalf of the Border Troops.

#### Article 249. State Fire Supervision Bodies

State fire supervision bodies have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 84, 124, 211 of this Code.

The following officials have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and impose administrative penalties on behalf of state fire supervision bodies:

See previous version.

the Chief State Inspector of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Fire Supervision and his deputies—to impose fines on citizens up to seven, and on officials—up to twenty basic calculation units;

(paragraph two of part two of Article 249 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 13, 2024 No. 3RU-919 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2024, No. 03/24/919/0208 — Enters into force on June 15, 2024)

See previous version.

chief state inspectors of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions, the city of Tashkent, at facilities of particularly important state significance or increased fire and explosion hazard, for fire supervision and their deputies, as well as senior assistants to the Chief State Inspector of the Republic of Uzbekistan for fire

supervision—to impose fines on citizens up to five, and on officials—up to fifteen basic calculation units;

(paragraph three of part two of Article 249 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 13, 2024 No. ZRU-919 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2024, No. 03/24/919/0208 — Enters into force on June 15, 2024)

See previous version.

senior assistants to chief state inspectors of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions, the city of Tashkent, at facilities of particularly important state significance or increased fire and explosion hazard, for fire supervision—to impose fines on citizens up to four, and on officials—up to ten basic calculation units;

(paragraph four of part two of Article 249 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 13, 2024 No. ZRU-919 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2024, No. 03/24/919/0208 — Enters into force on June 15, 2024)

See previous version.

senior state inspectors of districts (cities), at facilities of particularly important state significance or increased fire and explosion hazard, for fire supervision and their deputies—to impose fines on citizens up to three, and on officials—up to seven basic calculation units;

(paragraph five of part two of Article 249 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 13, 2024 No. ZRU-919 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2024, No. 03/24/919/0208 — Enters into force on June 15, 2024)

See previous version.

state inspectors of districts (cities), at facilities of particularly important state significance or increased fire and explosion hazard, for fire supervision—to impose fines on citizens up to one, and on officials—up to three basic calculation units.

(paragraph six of part two of Article 249 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 13, 2024 No. ZRU-919 — National Database of Legislation, 14.03.2024, No. 03/24/919/0208 — Enters into force on June 15, 2024)

#### Article 249¹. Bodies of the Ministry for Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Officials of the bodies of the Ministry for Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan, upon establishing offenses provided for by Articles 119, 132 and 204¹ of this Code, shall draw up a protocol on an administrative offense.

See amendments entering into force on May 10, 2026

The protocol on an administrative offense shall be sent for consideration to the court in the manner established by Article 282 of this Code.

In the event of administrative offenses provided for by Articles 119 and 132 of this Code, the protocol on an administrative offense shall be drawn up by officials of the Small Vessel Inspectorate of the Ministry for Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

See amendments entering into force on May 10, 2026

Officials of the Small Vessel Inspectorate of the Ministry for Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan are the chief, senior officer and chief specialist of the Small Vessel Inspectorate, as well as chief inspectors of territorial emergency situations departments.

#### (Article 249¹

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. ZRU-597 — National Database of Legislation, December 25, 2019, No. 03/19/597/4193 — Enters into force on March 26, 2020)

#### Article 250. Bodies of railway transport and metro

Bodies of railway transport have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by part one of Article 56¹ (use of tobacco products in places not specially designated on railway transport), Articles 113, 114, as well as Articles 53, 121, 123, 124, parts one and three of Article 142, Articles 144, 145, parts one and two of Article 146 (for committing violations on railway transport) of this Code.

LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation. Part one of Article 250 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of July 10, 2025 No. ZRU-1075. See the official text in the Uzbek language.

Bodies of the metro have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 115 of this Code.

The following shall have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties on behalf of bodies of railway transport and metro:

chief of station and his deputy, chief of terminal and his deputy, chief of locomotive (car) depot, chief of passenger train (mechanic-foreman of passenger train);

controller-auditor of passenger train, auditor-instructor of passenger train, revenue control auditor, track foreman, chief of track section, chief of signaling and communications section;

See previous version.

chief of paramilitary security unit and his deputy, chief of detachment (team, fire train) and his deputy, senior instructor and instructor for fire prevention, chief of fire train team, paramilitary security team (guard);

(paragraph four of part three of Article 250 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 28, 2023 No. ZRU-881 — National Database of Legislation, November 29, 2023, No. 03/23/881/0906)

chief sanitary physician (deputy chief of medical and sanitary service), chief sanitary physician of the Central Sanitary and Epidemiological Station, chief sanitary physician of the sanitary and epidemiological station.

See previous version.

The amount of fine imposed by the chief of passenger train (mechanic-foreman of passenger train), track foreman, chief of section (guard) of team, fire train, paramilitary security, chief sanitary physician (deputy chief of medical and sanitary service), chief sanitary physician of the Central Sanitary and Epidemiological Station, chief sanitary physician of the sanitary and epidemiological station, may not exceed one-half of the base calculation value.

(part four of Article 250 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, December 4, 2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

chief of the State Inspectorate of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Flight Safety Supervision and his deputy, chiefs of departments and divisions of the State Inspectorate of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Flight Safety Supervision;

general director of the National Air Carrier and his deputies, director of the Flight Complex of the air carrier and his deputy, director of airport (aviation enterprise), chief inspector of the air carrier, chief of medical and sanitary service of the air carrier.

#### Article 254

Bodies of passenger urban and intercity road transport and electric transport

See previous version.

#### Article 2541. Bodies of the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Bodies of the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Article 1252 of this Code.

The right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of a fine on behalf of the bodies of the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Uzbekistan is vested in:

the head of the Department of Control of Road Transportation of the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

heads of departments for control over ensuring the safety of road transportation of the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, transport departments of regions and the city of Tashkent and their deputies.

#### Article 255. Officials of the State Labor Inspectorate

See previous version.

Officials of the State Labor Inspectorate consider cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 49, 492, 493, 50, 51, 229 of this Code.

state labor inspectors, state legal labor inspectors — for violations of labor legislation and legislation on employment of the population;

#### Article 2551. Migration Agency under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Officials of the Migration Agency under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses provided for in Article 501 of this Code and apply measures of administrative punishment.

The right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative fines on behalf of the Migration Agency under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan is vested in the director and deputy directors of the Migration Agency under the Cabinet of Ministers of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 256. Bodies of the Ministry of Defense

Bodies of the Ministry of Defense of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for in Articles 125, 1251, 126, part one of Article 127, parts one, two and three of Article 128, Articles 1281, 1282, parts one, two and three of Article 1283, parts one and two of Article 1284, parts one and two of Article 1285, Article 1286, part one of Article 129, part one of Article 130, Articles 135, 1351, 138, 139, 140 (committed by drivers of vehicles — military personnel and persons liable for military service called up for training) and Articles 235, 236 of this Code.

heads of district (city) departments for defense affairs and heads of district (city) departments of alternative service for administrative offenses provided for in Articles 235, 236 of this Code;

See previous version.

head, senior inspector of the military automobile inspectorate — for administrative offenses provided for in Articles 125, 1251, 126, part one of Article 127, parts one, two and three of Article 128, Articles 1281, 1282, parts one, two and three of Article 1283, parts one and two of Article 1284, parts one and two of Article 1285, Article 1286, part one of Article 129, part one of Article 130, Articles 135, 1351, 138, 139, 140 of this Code.

#### Article 257. State Sanitary Supervision Bodies

See previous version.

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by Articles 53, 54, 55, Article 85 (for violation of standards for maximum permissible harmful physical impacts on atmospheric air and emissions of biological organisms into atmospheric air, as well as harmful physical impact on atmospheric air and emission of biological organisms into atmospheric air without permission from specially authorized state bodies, if these offenses are violations of sanitary-hygienic rules), Articles 86, 88, 89, 95 (when physical impact on atmospheric air is exerted and these offenses are violations of sanitary-hygienic rules), Article 87 (for exceeding established noise level standards produced during operation of automobiles, aircraft, vessels and other means and installations), Article 96 (in the part of sanitary-ecological expertise) of this Code are under the jurisdiction of state sanitary supervision bodies.

(part one of Article 257 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated January 3, 2018 No. ZRU-456 — National Database of Legislation, 05.01.2018, No. 03/18/456/0512)

See previous version.

The following are authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposing fines on behalf of state sanitary supervision bodies:

See previous version.

Chief State Sanitary Physician of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

chief state sanitary physicians of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions and the city of Tashkent and their deputies;

chief state sanitary physicians of districts (cities).

(paragraphs two through four of part two of Article 257 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated April 26, 2021 No. ZRU-685 — National Database of Legislation, 27.04.2021, No. 03/21/685/0373)

See previous version.

#### Article 258

Medical Services of the Ministry of Defense, Ministry of Internal Affairs, State Security Service and National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan Exercising Sanitary Supervision

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by Articles 53, 54, 55 (including for violation of sanitary-hygienic rules and norms for protection of atmospheric air) of this Code are under the jurisdiction of the medical services of the Ministry of Defense, Ministry of Internal Affairs, State Security Service and National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan exercising sanitary supervision.

The following are authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties on behalf of the bodies listed in part one of this Article:

chief sanitary physician of the Ministry of Defense of the Republic of Uzbekistan — head of the central sanitary-epidemiological laboratory and his deputy;

See previous version.

chief sanitary physician of the medical department of the Ministry of Internal Affairs of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputy (head of the main center of sanitary-epidemiological supervision), chief sanitary physician of the medical department of the Ministry of Internal Affairs of the Republic of Karakalpakstan and his deputy (head of the center of sanitary-epidemiological supervision), chief sanitary physicians of the medical departments of the main directorates of internal affairs of the city of Tashkent and Tashkent region, directorates of internal affairs of regions and their deputies (heads of centers of sanitary-epidemiological supervision);

(paragraph three of part two of Article 258 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 27, 2022 No. ZRU-797 — National Database of Legislation, 28.10.2022, No. 03/22/797/0967)

head of the sanitary-epidemiological station of the military medical service of the State Security Service;

See previous version.

chief sanitary physician — of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(paragraph five of part two of Article 258 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 29, 2021 No. ZRU-726 — National Database of Legislation, 30.10.2021, No. 03/21/726/1001)

See previous version.

#### Article 259. State Veterinary Service

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 109 of this Code are under the jurisdiction of the State Veterinary Service.

The following are authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposing fines on behalf of the state veterinary service:

See previous version.

chief state veterinary inspector of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies, chief state veterinary inspectors of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions and the city of Tashkent, their deputies, chief state veterinary inspector at the state border and on transport and his deputies — up to two basic calculation values;

(paragraph two of part two of Article 259 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. ZRU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

state veterinary inspectors of the Committee for Veterinary and Livestock Development under the Ministry of Agriculture of the Republic of Uzbekistan, chief state veterinary inspectors of districts and cities, state veterinary inspectors of border and transport veterinary stations (sections) and state veterinary inspectors of veterinary sections and veterinary-sanitary expertise laboratories at markets — up to one basic calculation value.

(paragraph three of part two of Article 259 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated January 17, 2024 No. 3RU-898 — National Database of Legislation, 18.01.2024, No. 03/24/898/0046)

See previous version.

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 55, Article 70 (in the part of safety of work during use of subsoil), Articles 97, 971, 98 of this Code are under the jurisdiction of the bodies of the State Committee for Industrial Safety of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

The following are authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposing fines on behalf of the bodies of the State Committee for Industrial Safety of the Republic of Uzbekistan:

chairman of the State Committee and his deputies — up to fifteen basic calculation values;

heads of inspectorates and their deputies — up to ten basic calculation values;

(paragraph three of part two of Article 260 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

See previous version.

heads of mining-technical inspectorates and territorial departments and their deputies — up to seven basic calculation values;

(paragraph four of part two of Article 260 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 3, 2019 No. 3RU-586 — National Database of Legislation, 04.12.2019, No. 03/19/586/4106)

state inspectors — up to three basic calculation values.

#### Article 2601

Inspectorate for Control in the Sphere of Mining Industry and Geology under the Ministry of Mining Industry and Geology of the Republic of Uzbekistan

The Inspectorate for Control in the Sphere of Mining Industry and Geology under the Ministry of Mining Industry and Geology of the Republic of Uzbekistan has jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 70 (with the exception of safety of work in the use of subsoil), Article 71 (with the exception of rules for the protection of nature), Article 72 (in the part concerning underground waters), parts four and five of Article 74 of this Code.

 LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation. Part one of Article 2601 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 5, 2025 No. 3RU-1062. See official text in the Uzbek language. Date of entry into force — August 6, 2025.

The following officials are entitled to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposition of a fine on behalf of the Inspectorate for Control in the Sphere of Mining Industry and Geology under the Ministry of Mining Industry and Geology of the Republic of Uzbekistan:

the head of the inspectorate and his deputy — up to one hundred basic calculation units;

heads of regional inspectorates and their deputies — up to seventy basic calculation units;

inspectors — up to fifty basic calculation units.

Officials of the Inspectorate for Control in the Sphere of Mining Industry and Geology under the Ministry of Mining Industry and Geology of the Republic of Uzbekistan, upon establishing administrative offenses provided for by Article 702 of this Code, shall draw up a protocol on the administrative offense and, in the manner established by Article 282 of this Code, send it for consideration to the court.

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 28, 2024 No. 3RU-984 — National Database of Legislation, 28.10.2024, No. 03/24/984/0860)
