#### Article 2602

Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan

The Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan has jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 101 (in relation to officials in the part concerning the use of electric energy and gas).

The following officials are entitled to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposition of a fine on behalf of the Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan:

the head of the Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

heads of territorial departments of the Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Officials of the Inspectorate for Control over the Use of Electric Energy, Petroleum Products and Gas under the Ministry of Energy of the Republic of Uzbekistan and its territorial departments, upon establishing an administrative offense provided for by Article 1986 of this Code, shall draw up a protocol on the administrative offense.

The protocol on the administrative offense, in the manner established by Article 282 of this Code, shall be sent for consideration to the court.

#### Article 261

Bodies of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change

LexUz Commentary Unofficial translation. The title of Article 261 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated July 10, 2025 No. 3RU-1075. See the official text in the Uzbek language.

The bodies of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 63, part four of Article 65 (with the exception of damage to agricultural and other lands), Article 68 (with respect to lands for nature conservation,

health improvement, recreational purposes, lands of the water fund), Article 70 (with the exception of safety of work in the use of subsoil), part one of Article 701, Article 71 (with respect to nature conservation), Article 72 (with the exception of the part concerning groundwater), parts one and three of Article 74, Article 75 (with the exception of accounting for water taken from irrigation systems, as well as for violation of the established procedure for maintaining the state water cadastre), parts one and two of Article 77, Article 78, parts one, two, four and five of Article 79, Articles 791, 80, 81, 82, 83, 84, Article 85 (with the exception of harmful physical impact on atmospheric air, release of biological organisms into atmospheric air, which constitutes a violation of sanitary-hygienic rules), Articles 851, 86, 861, 87, 88, 89 (with the exception of harmful physical impact on atmospheric air), Article 891, part one of Article 90, Article 91, parts one, two and three of Article 911, Articles 912, 913, 92, 93, 95, Article 96 (with respect to state environmental expertise), Article 148 (with the exception of cases of administrative offenses for lighting fires on the right-of-way and closer than 100 meters from wooden bridges, smoking on wooden bridges and bridges with wooden decking), Article 214 (with respect to nature conservation), Article 228 (in cases where seals (stamps) were imposed for violation of environmental legislation) of this Code.

chief state inspectors of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Uzbekistan and their deputies, chief state inspectors of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, departments of ecology, environmental protection and climate change of regions and the city of Tashkent and their deputies — on citizens up to one hundred, and on officials — up to one hundred fifty basic calculation units;

senior state inspectors of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, departments of ecology, environmental protection and climate change of regions and the city of Tashkent — on citizens up to seventy, and on officials — up to one hundred basic calculation units;

state inspectors of the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the Ministry of Ecology, Environmental Protection and Climate Change of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, departments of ecology, environmental protection and climate change of regions and the city of Tashkent, district (city) inspections for control in the field of ecology and environmental protection, employees of the protection of state nature reserves, complex (landscape) sanctuaries, natural parks, state biosphere reserves — on citizens up to fifty, and on officials — up to seventy basic calculation units.

#### Article 2611. Bodies of State Forestry

The bodies of the State Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan on Forestry have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by parts one and two of Article 77, Article 78, Article 79 (with respect to lands of the forest fund), Articles 80, 83, Article 84 (with respect to lands of the forest fund), part one of Article 90 (with respect to lands of the forest fund) of this Code.

The right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposing fines on behalf of the State Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan on Forestry is vested in:

chief state inspectors of the State Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan on Forestry, chief state inspectors of the Committee of the Republic of Karakalpakstan on Forestry, departments of forestry of regions — on citizens up to fifteen, and on officials — up to twenty basic calculation units;

state inspectors of the State Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan on Forestry, state inspectors of the Committee of the Republic of Karakalpakstan on Forestry, departments of forestry of regions, state forestry enterprises, state forest-hunting enterprises, specialized state forestry enterprises, state forest-industrial enterprises and employees of national natural parks — on citizens up to ten, and on officials — up to fifteen basic calculation units.

See previous version.

#### Article 263

Bodies of the State Inspection for Control in the Sphere of Informatization and Telecommunications under the Ministry of Digital Technologies of the Republic of Uzbekistan

The bodies of the State Inspection for Control in the Sphere of Informatization and Telecommunications under the Ministry of Digital Technologies of the Republic of Uzbekistan have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by part one of Article 152, Articles 153, 154 of this Code.

The head of the State Inspection for Control in the Sphere of Informatization and Telecommunications under the Ministry of Digital Technologies of the Republic of Uzbekistan, as well as the heads of territorial subdivisions of this state inspection in the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions and the city of Tashkent shall have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and impose administrative penalties on behalf of the bodies specified in part one of this Article.

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by parts one, two, three, four, six and seven of Article 175 of this Code have jurisdiction of state tax authorities.

Officials of the Ministry of Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan and territorial financial authorities, upon establishing offenses provided for by Article 1756 of this Code, shall draw up a protocol on administrative offense.

The protocol on administrative offense shall be sent for consideration to the court in the procedure established by Article 282 of this Code.

#### Article 265

Bodies of technical regulation, standardization, metrology and certification

Bodies of state supervision over compliance with mandatory requirements of technical regulations or standards, rules of metrology and certification have jurisdiction over cases of administrative offenses provided for by Articles 212, 213, Article 214 (with the exception of the part concerning nature protection) of this Code.

The following shall have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply measures of administrative penalty in the form of imposition of a fine on behalf of bodies of state supervision over compliance with mandatory requirements of technical regulations or standards, rules of metrology and certification:

the chief state inspector of the Republic of Uzbekistan for ensuring compliance with the requirements of legislation in the field of technical regulation, standardization, conformity assessment and metrology and his deputies — up to eighty basic calculation values;

state inspectors for ensuring compliance with the requirements of legislation in the field of technical regulation, standardization, conformity assessment and metrology — up to three basic calculation values.

#### Article 2661

Bodies of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by parts two, three and four of Article 60, Article 60¹, Article 65 (in the part concerning the use of land plots not in accordance with the main type of permitted use, with the exception of agricultural lands), Article 68 (with the exception of lands for nature conservation, health improvement, recreational purposes, lands of the water fund), Article 68¹ and Article 69 (with the exception of agricultural lands in the part of boundary markers) of this Code are under the jurisdiction of the bodies of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

The following persons have the right to consider cases of administrative offenses and apply administrative penalties in the form of imposing fines on behalf of the bodies of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan:

the Director of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies;

heads of departments of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan in the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regions, the city of Tashkent and their deputies;

heads of district (city) departments of the Cadastre Agency under the Ministry of Economy and Finance of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 2684. Bodies for supervision of the quality of road construction works

Officials of the Inspectorate for Quality Control of Road Construction Works under the Ministry of Transport of the Republic of Uzbekistan, upon establishing administrative offenses provided for in Article 99 (regarding violations of legislation in the field of road construction), part one of Article 147 (in case of unauthorized excavation of highways, erection of artificial irregularities and obstacles on them, failure to comply with the requirements of a permit issued for carrying out works on a highway, as well as violation of road maintenance rules) of this Code, draw up a protocol on the administrative offense.

The protocol on the administrative offense is sent for consideration to the court in the manner established by Article 282 of this Code.

heads of the Agency for Quarantine and Plant Protection of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, departments for quarantine and plant protection of regions and the city of Tashkent and their deputies;

heads of district (city) departments for quarantine and plant protection.

See previous version.

When the Agency for Quarantine and Plant Protection under the Ministry of Agriculture of the Republic of Uzbekistan and its territorial subdivisions identify administrative offenses provided for in part two of Article 107, as well as part two of Article 108 of this Code, a protocol on the administrative offense is drawn up and sent to the court for consideration in the manner established by Article 282 of this Code.

(part three of Article 2685 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated January 17, 2024 No. 3RU-898 — National Database of Legislation, 18.01.2024, No. 03/24/898/0046)

SECTION FOUR PROCEEDINGS IN CASES OF ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSES Chapter XIX. GENERAL PROVISIONS

#### Article 269. Objectives of proceedings in cases of administrative offenses

The objectives of proceedings in cases of administrative offenses are: timely, comprehensive, complete and objective clarification of the circumstances of each case, resolution of it in accordance with legislation, ensuring the execution of the issued decision, as well as identifying the causes of administrative offenses and conditions conducive to them, prevention of offenses, education of citizens in the spirit of compliance with the Constitution and laws of the Republic of Uzbekistan, strengthening of legality.

See previous version.

#### Article 2691. Cases of administrative offenses

Cases of administrative offenses are formed on the basis of documents submitted by persons participating in the case, documents requested by the body (official) during consideration of the case, as well as acts executed on paper media.

Cases of administrative offenses may be formed in electronic form.

When forming cases of administrative offenses in electronic form, persons participating in the case have the right to submit documents to the body (official) in electronic form, confirmed by their electronic digital signature.

When forming cases in electronic form, written documents submitted to the court by persons participating in the case are attached to the case in electronic form, after which the written documents are returned to the persons who submitted them.

In the case of forming cases of administrative offenses in electronic form, acts executed by the body (official) are confirmed by the electronic digital signature of the presiding officer of the body and the secretary, or the official considering the case, respectively.

Transfer of cases of administrative offenses in electronic form to another body is carried out through the information system.

Cases of administrative offenses formed in electronic form may have a copy on paper media.

#### (Article 2691

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 24, 2019 No. 3RU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193)

See previous version.

#### Article 2692

Appeals of persons participating in proceedings on a case of administrative offense

Persons participating in proceedings on a case of administrative offense have the right to appeal in cases of administrative offenses in the manner and within the time limits established by this Code.

The appeal and documents attached to it may be sent to the court in the form of an electronic document.

Original documents are submitted when the circumstances of the case are subject to confirmation only by such documents, as well as in other necessary cases at the request of the body (official) in whose proceedings the case of administrative offense is located.

#### (Article 2692

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated December 24, 2019 No. 3RU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193)

See previous version.

#### Article 2693

Proceedings on a case of administrative offense based on a victim's application

Administrative offenses provided for in Articles 40, 41, 44, 45, 46, 46, 52, Article 61, (with the exception of enterprises, institutions, organizations), Article 193, as well as part one of Article 202 of this Code, entail liability based on the victim's application to bring the offender to liability.

In exceptional cases, when the victim, due to a helpless state, minority, dependence on the accused or for other reasons, is unable to protect his rights and legitimate interests himself, the official of the authorized body is obliged to initiate an administrative case even without the victim's application.

Offenses provided for in parts one and two of Article 61 of this Code, committed by an employee against an enterprise, institution, organization in whose charter fund there is no state share, entail liability based on the application of the head, owner or authorized management body of such enterprise, institution, organization.

An offense provided for in Article 61 of this Code, committed by an employee against an enterprise, institution, organization in whose charter fund there is no state share, entails liability based on the application of the head, owner or authorized management body of such enterprise, institution, organization.

Commentary LexUz

Unofficial translation.

#### Article 2693

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated July 10, 2025 No. 3RU-1075. See official text in Uzbek language.

#### Article 270. Procedure for proceedings in cases of administrative offenses

The procedure for proceedings in cases of administrative offenses is determined by this Code and other acts of legislation.

#### Article 271

Circumstances excluding proceedings in a case of administrative offense

Proceedings in a case of administrative offense cannot be initiated, and initiated proceedings are subject to termination in the presence of the following circumstances:

1) absence of the event or corpus delicti of an administrative offense;

2) failure of the person to reach the age of sixteen at the time of committing the administrative offense;

3) insanity of the person who committed the unlawful action or inaction;

4) action of the person in a state of necessary defense or extreme necessity;

See previous version.

41) action of the person as a result of physical or mental coercion or threat;

#### (article 271

supplemented by subparagraph 41 by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 9, 2018 No. 3RU-459 — National Database of Legislation, 10.01.2018, No. 03/18/459/0536)

5) issuance of an act of amnesty, if it eliminates the application of an administrative penalty;

6) repeal of an act establishing administrative liability;

7) expiration, at the time of consideration of the case of an administrative offense, of the time limits provided for by Article 36 of this Code;

8) existence, on the same facts in relation to the person brought to administrative liability, of a decision of a competent body (official) on the application of an administrative penalty or an unrepealed decision on termination of a case of an administrative offense, as well as the existence on the given facts of a criminal case;

See previous version.

81) absence of a statement by the victim in cases where proceedings are subject to initiation upon application, with the exception of circumstances provided for by part two of Article 2693 of this Code;

#### Article 272

Consideration of a case of an administrative offense on the basis of equality of citizens

See previous version.

Consideration of a case of an administrative offense is carried out on the basis of equality before the law and the body (official) considering the case, of all citizens regardless of sex, race, nationality, language, religion, beliefs, social origin, social status.

(text of Article 272 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of September 20, 2024 No. 3RU-963 — National Database of Legislation, 21.09.2024, No. 03/24/963/0735)

#### Article 273

Language in which proceedings in a case of an administrative offense are conducted

Proceedings in a case of an administrative offense are conducted in the Uzbek, Karakalpak languages or in the language of the majority of the population of the given locality. Persons participating in the case who do not speak the language in which the proceedings are conducted are ensured the right to full familiarization with the materials of the case and participation in its consideration on the merits through an interpreter, as well as the right to speak in their native language.

#### Article 274. Open consideration of a case of an administrative offense

A case of an administrative offense is considered openly.

In order to enhance the educational and preventive role of proceedings in cases of administrative offenses, such cases may be considered in collectives at the place of work, study or residence of the offender.

#### Article 275

Prosecutorial supervision over the execution of laws in proceedings in cases of administrative offenses

The prosecutor, exercising supervision over the execution of laws in proceedings in cases of administrative offenses, has the right to:

initiate proceedings on an administrative offense;

familiarize himself with the materials of the case;

verify the legality of the actions of bodies (officials) in proceedings in the case;

participate in the consideration of the case;

file petitions, give conclusions on issues arising during the consideration of the case;

verify the correctness of application by the relevant bodies (officials) of measures of influence for administrative offenses;

protest a decision in a case of an administrative offense or a decision on a complaint;

perform other actions provided for by law.


This excerpt sits under: Кучга кириш санаси > Chapter XVIII. JURISDICTION OVER CASES OF ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSES

#### Article 276. Evidence

Evidence in a case of an administrative offense shall be any factual data on the basis of which, in the manner established by law, bodies (officials) establish the presence or absence of an administrative offense, the guilt of a given person in its commission, and other circumstances relevant to the proper consideration of the case.

See previous version.

The data provided for in part one of this article shall be established by the following means: a protocol on an administrative offense, explanations of the person brought to administrative responsibility, and testimony of the victim, witnesses, expert opinion, consultation (explanations) of a specialist, material, written and digital evidence, protocol on seizure of things and documents, control devices (tachographs) of drivers on vehicles carrying out intercity and international freight and passenger transportation, audio, video and photography materials, as well as other materials.

(part two of article 276 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. 3RU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

#### Article 277. Evaluation of evidence

A body (official) shall evaluate evidence according to its conviction, based on a comprehensive, complete and objective examination of all circumstances of the case in their totality, guided by law.

#### Article 278. Transfer of materials to the prosecutor

If, when considering a case, a body (official) comes to the conclusion that the offense contains signs of a crime, it shall transfer the materials to the prosecutor.

## Chapter XX. PROTOCOL ON AN ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE

#### Article 279. Drawing up a protocol on an administrative offense

A protocol shall be drawn up by an official authorized to do so regarding the commission of an administrative offense.

A protocol shall not be drawn up in cases provided for by Article 283 of this Code.

#### Article 280

Persons authorized to draw up a protocol on an administrative offense

A protocol on an administrative offense shall be drawn up by an authorized official of the relevant body, which is entrusted by legislation with control and (or) supervision over compliance with rules, violation of which entails administrative responsibility.

(text of article 280 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of June 13, 2017 No. ZRU-436 — SZ RU, 2017, No. 24, art.

#### 487) Article 281. Content of the protocol on an administrative offense

The protocol on an administrative offense shall indicate: the date and place of its drawing up; the position, surname, first name, patronymic of the person who drew up the protocol; information about the identity of the offender; the place, time of commission and nature of the administrative offense; the normative act providing for responsibility for the given offense; surnames, addresses of witnesses and victims, if any; explanations of the offender; other information necessary for resolution of the case. If material damage has been caused by the offense, this shall also be indicated in the protocol.

The protocol shall be signed by the person who drew it up and the person who committed the administrative offense, and in the presence of witnesses and victims — also by these persons.

In case of refusal of the person who committed the offense to sign the protocol, a note to this effect shall be made in it. The person who committed the offense shall have the right to submit explanations and comments on its content attached to the protocol, as well as to set forth the reasons for his refusal to sign.

When drawing up the protocol, the offender shall be informed of his rights and obligations provided for by Article 294 of this Code, which shall be noted in the protocol.

#### Article 282. Forwarding of the protocol on an administrative offense

See previous version.

The protocol together with other documents and evidence in the case shall be forwarded no later than three days from the moment of commission or detection of the offense to the body (official) authorized to consider the case of an administrative offense.

(part one of article 282 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

In cases of appointment of an expert examination in the case, taking measures to assess the damage caused, sending requests to obtain a driver's license, the protocol shall be forwarded to the body (official) authorized to consider cases of administrative offenses within three days from the moment of receipt of these documents.

#### (article 282

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 20, 2025 No. ZRU-1033 — National Database of Legislation, 21.02.2025, No. 03/25/1033/0164)

See previous version.

The following shall be attached to the protocol on an administrative offense:

1) a copy of the document confirming the identity of the person brought to administrative responsibility;

See previous version.

1) if available — the personal identification number of the victim — an individual or the taxpayer identification number — a legal entity;

2) a certificate from internal affairs bodies on the presence or absence of the fact of prior bringing to administrative responsibility of the person brought to administrative

responsibility;

3) an explanation of the person brought to administrative responsibility;

4) testimony of the victim, witnesses, expert opinion, if any;

5) other materials confirming the fact of commission of an administrative offense;

See previous version.

6) a statement of the victim regarding administrative offenses provided for by parts one, three and four of Article 2693 of this Code.

1) driver's license, copy of vehicle registration certificate and power of attorney;

2) copy of insurance policy (if available);

1) statement of the victim;

2) decision on appointment of pre-trial medical expert examination and expert opinion;

#### Article 283

Cases when a protocol on an administrative offense is not drawn up

See previous version.

A protocol on an administrative offense is not drawn up if the citizen does not dispute the fact of committing the offense and the fine imposed on the spot does not exceed one-half of the base calculation value, as well as in other cases provided for by legislation. For violations of traffic rules, an administrative protocol is drawn up regardless of the amount of the fine, with the exception of cases provided for in part four of this article.

(part one of Article 283 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 28, 2024 No. 3RU-985 — National Database of Legislation, October 28, 2024, No. 03/24/985/0861)

A receipt of the established form is issued to the guilty person for payment of the fine.

If the citizen disputes the fine imposed on him, a protocol is drawn up in accordance with Article 279 of this Code.

See previous version.

In case of recording by special automated technical means of photo and video recording of violations of traffic rules using a vehicle, as well as recording by the Unified Automated Information Systems of the State Tax Committee or the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan of violations of tax or customs legislation, a protocol on an administrative offense is not drawn up.

(part one of Article 283 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated March 11, 2022 No. 3RU-758 — National Database of Legislation, March 12, 2022, No. 03/22/758/0207 — Enters into force on June 13, 2022)

See previous version.

In case of violation of guarantees of deputy and senator activities provided for by Article 193 of this Code, respectively, the offices of the chambers of the Oliy Majlis of the Republic of Uzbekistan and the secretariats of local Kengashes of People's Deputies have the right to apply to court without drawing up a protocol on an administrative offense, indicating in their application information about the identity of the offender, the place, time of commission and nature of the administrative offense, as well as other information necessary for resolving the case.

supplemented by part five by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated September 10, 2019 No. 3RU-566 — National Database of Legislation, September 11, 2019, No. 03/19/566/3734)

#### (Article 283

#### Article 284. Delivery of the offender

See previous version.

For the purpose of drawing up a protocol on an administrative offense when it is impossible to draw it up on the spot, if drawing up a protocol is mandatory, the offender may be delivered to an internal affairs body or to the premises of a citizens' self-government body by an employee of an internal affairs body or by another person authorized to draw up a protocol on an administrative offense.

(part one of Article 284 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 23, 2019 No. 3RU-542 — National Database of Legislation, May 24, 2019, No. 03/19/542/317)

See previous version.

In case of violation of rules for using means of transport, for protection of order and traffic safety, for ensuring the safety of cargo on transport, fire safety rules, sanitary-hygienic and sanitary-anti-epidemic

rules on transport, the offender may be delivered by a person authorized to do so to an internal affairs body if he has no identity documents and there are no witnesses who can provide necessary information about him.

(part two of Article 284 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 23, 2019 No. 3RU-542 — National Database of Legislation, May 24, 2019, No. 03/19/542/317)

See previous version.

In case of violation of legislation on the protection and use of flora and fauna, if the identity of the offender cannot be established at the place of commission of the offense, employees of bodies exercising control over the protection and use of flora and fauna, officials of protected natural territories, as well as employees of internal affairs bodies and servicemen (employees) of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan may, for the purpose of drawing up a protocol, deliver the offender to an internal affairs body or to the premises of a citizens' self-government body. Delivery of the offender may also be carried out by public inspectors of ecology and environmental protection, public hunting inspectors, public fishery protection inspectors and public forest inspectors.

(part three of Article 284 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated July 11, 2023 No. 3RU-854 — National Database of Legislation, July 12, 2023, No. 03/23/854/0463 — Enters into force on October 13, 2023)

See previous version.

In case of committing offenses related to encroachment on protected objects, other property of legal entities, the offender may be delivered by employees of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan or workers and employees of paramilitary security units to a body of the National Guard of the Republic of Uzbekistan, an official premises of a paramilitary security unit or internal affairs bodies for suppression of the offense, establishment of the identity of the offender and drawing up a protocol on the offense.

(part four of Article 284 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 28, 2023 No. 3RU-881 — National Database of Legislation, November 29, 2023, No. 03/23/881/0906)

Delivery of the offender must be carried out in the shortest possible time.

The stay of a person delivered to the premises of a citizens' self-government body may not last more than one hour.

## Chapter XXI

ADMINISTRATIVE DETENTION, INSPECTION OF THINGS AND VEHICLES, SEIZURE OF THINGS AND DOCUMENTS

#### Article 285

Measures to Ensure Proceedings in Cases of Administrative Offenses

For the purposes of suppressing administrative offenses when other measures of influence have been exhausted, establishing identity, drawing up a protocol on an administrative offense when it is impossible to draw it up on the spot, if drawing up a protocol is mandatory, ensuring timely and proper consideration of the case and execution of decisions in cases of administrative offenses, administrative detention, personal search, search of things, vehicles and seizure of things and documents are permitted.

The procedure for administrative detention, personal search, search of things and vehicles, seizure of things and documents for the purposes provided for in this article is determined by this Code and other legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 286. Administrative Detention

A protocol is drawn up on administrative detention, which indicates: the date and place of its drawing up; the position, surname, first name, patronymic of the person who drew up the protocol; information about the identity of the detained person; the time, place and grounds for detention.

The protocol is signed by the official who drew it up and the detained person. In case of refusal of the detained person to sign the protocol, a record of this is made in it.

At the request of the detained person, his relatives, lawyer, administration at the place of work or study are notified of his whereabouts. In case of detention of a minor, his parents or persons replacing them are obligatorily notified.

#### Article 287

Bodies (Officials) Authorized to Carry Out Administrative Detention

Administrative detention may be carried out only by bodies (officials) authorized to do so by the legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan, namely:

See previous version.

1) by internal affairs bodies — upon commission of offenses provided for by Articles 47, 47, 51, 51, 51*, 54, Article 56, Article 561 (use of tobacco products in specially undesignated places on public transport), Articles 57, 58, parts two and three of Article 60, Articles 601, 61, part one of Article 611, Articles 63, 66, 701, 72, 79, 88, 90, 901, 91, 911, 912, 92, 94, 110, 111, 113, 114, 115, 116, 1161, 1162, 1163, 117, part three of Article 118, Articles 121, 123, 125, 1251, 126, 127, 128, 1281, 1282, 1283, 1284, 1285, 1286, 129, 130, 131, 133, 134, 135, 1351, 136, 137, 138, 142, part three of Article 144, Article 146, part one of Article 147, parts two and three of Article 152, Article 154, part four of Article 164, Article 170, parts one, two and three of Article 176, Article 1763, part two of Article 1764, Articles 183, 1842, 1843, 1844, 185, 1851, 1852, 1853, 186, 187, 188, 1883, 189, 1891, 190, 191, 192, 194, 195, 198, 199, 201, 2021, 203, 204, 205, 209, 2091, 210, 218, 220, 224, parts one — three of Article 225, Articles 2251, 228, 239, 240, 241 of this Code;

#### Article 288. Terms of Administrative Detention

Administrative detention may last no more than three hours. In exceptional cases, in connection with special necessity, other terms of administrative detention may be established by legislative acts of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

A person who has violated the border regime or the regime at checkpoints across the state border of the Republic of Uzbekistan may be detained for up to three hours for the preparation of a protocol, and in necessary cases for establishing identity and clarifying the circumstances of the offense — for up to three days with written notification to the prosecutor within twenty-four hours from the moment of detention, or for up to ten days with the sanction of the prosecutor if the offender does not have identity documents.

See previous version.

Persons who have committed administrative offenses provided for by Articles 54, 56, 57, 58, 61, 90, part four of Article 164, Articles 170, 183, 184[2], 184[3], 191, 194, 194[1], 195, 195[1], 201, 204, 205, 206, 210, parts one through three of Article 225, or persons concealing information about

their identity, may be detained until consideration of the case by a court in criminal cases or by the head (deputy head) of an internal affairs body for a period of no more than twenty-four hours.

(part three of Article 288 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 29, 2021 No. 3RU-726 — National Database of Legislation, 30.10.2021, No. 03/21/726/1001)

The term of administrative detention is calculated from the moment of delivery of the offender for the preparation of a protocol, and for a person in a state of intoxication — from the time of sobering up.

#### Article 289. Personal Search and Search of Items

See previous version.

  Personal search may be conducted by authorized persons of internal affairs bodies  None

Search of items, hunting and fishing tools, harvested products and other objects is carried out, as a rule, in the presence of the person in whose ownership or possession they are located. In cases that do not permit delay, the specified items and objects may be subjected to search with the participation of two witnesses in the absence of the owner (possessor).

A protocol is prepared regarding personal search and search of items, or a corresponding entry is made in the protocol on the administrative offense or in the protocol on administrative detention.

Personal search and search of items in customs institutions is conducted in the procedure established by customs legislation.

#### Article 290

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated August 8, 2025 No. 3RU-1080. See official text in Uzbek language. Effective date — November 10, 2025.

Seized orders, medals, badges for honorary titles are subject to return to their lawful owner, and if the owner is not known, they are sent to the appropriate body.

See previous version.

In case of violation of customs rules or tax and currency legislation, seizure of items (valuables) from the offender is permitted to ensure collection of the fine if the latter does not have a permanent place of residence in the Republic of Uzbekistan.

See previous version.

Seizure of the certificate for the right to operate a vehicle is conducted in the procedure established by the Ministry of Internal Affairs of the Republic of Uzbekistan, for the right to operate small vessels — by the Ministry of Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan, and for the right to hunt — by the State Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan on Ecology and Environmental Protection.

See previous version.

(part seven of Article 290 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on September 2, 2022)

To a person who has committed an offense in the performance of official duties, seizure, personal search and search of items are applied in cases that do not permit delay.

#### Article 291. Grounds, procedure for detention and inspection of vehicles

Detention and inspection of vehicles are carried out by bodies (officials) authorized by legislation, until the cause of detention is eliminated and the case of administrative offense is considered, namely:

#### Article 292

Removal from vehicle operation, examination for state of intoxication

A person operating a vehicle, in respect of whom there are sufficient grounds to believe that they are in a state of intoxication, is subject to

removal from vehicle operation and examination in the established procedure for state of intoxication.

Referral for examination and its conduct is carried out in the procedure determined by the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the Ministry of Health and the Ministry of Justice of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### Article 293

Appeal of measures to ensure proceedings in administrative offense cases

Administrative detention, personal search, search of things, detention and search of vehicles, seizure of things and documents, removal from vehicle operation may be appealed by an interested person to a higher body (official), prosecutor or court.

## Chapter XXII

PERSONS PARTICIPATING IN PROCEEDINGS IN ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE CASES

#### Article 294

Rights and obligations of a person brought to administrative responsibility

A person brought to administrative responsibility has the right to familiarize themselves with  None
None
None

See previous version.

A case of administrative offense is considered in the presence of the person brought to administrative responsibility. In the absence of this person, the case may be considered only in cases where there is evidence of timely notification of the place and time of consideration of the case and if no petition for postponement of the case has been received from them, as well as in cases provided for in part one of Article 3091 and in part one of Article 3092 of this Code.

(part two of Article 294 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 4, 2021 No. 3RU-720 — National Database of Legislation, 05.10.2021, No. 03/21/720/0930 — Enters into force on January 6, 2022)

See previous version.

The presence of a person brought to administrative responsibility is mandatory if this Code provides for the application of administrative arrest, confiscation or compensated seizure of items for the offense committed by them. In case of evasion by a person from participation in consideration of a case of such administrative offense, this person may be subjected to forced appearance by the internal affairs body by order of the criminal court. In exceptional cases, a case of administrative offense related to the movement of goods or other valuables across the customs border of the Republic of Uzbekistan in violation of customs legislation, for which confiscation may be applied, is considered in the absence of the person brought to administrative responsibility if this person is outside the Republic of Uzbekistan or there is no possibility to establish their whereabouts.

(part three of Article 294 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

#### Article 295. Victim

A victim is recognized as a person to whom moral, physical or property harm has been caused by an administrative offense.

The victim has the right to familiarize themselves with all materials of the case, give testimony,  None
None
None

#### Article 296. Legal representatives

The interests of a person brought to administrative responsibility, or a victim, who are minors or persons who, due to their physical disabilities or mental disorders, cannot themselves exercise their rights in cases of administrative offenses, may be represented by their legal representatives (parents, spouses, adult children, adoptive parents, guardians, trustees).

(part one of Article 296 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of September 12, 2019 No. 3RU-567 — National Database of Legislation, 13.09.2019, No. 03/19/567/3737)

Legal representatives have the right to familiarize themselves with the materials of the case and, on behalf of the person whose interests they represent, file petitions, appeal the decision on the case.

#### Article 297. Advocate

An advocate may participate in the consideration of a case of administrative offense from the moment of detention of the person who committed it.

The advocate has the right to familiarize themselves with the materials of the case, on behalf of and by instruction of the person who invited them, file petitions and appeal the decision on the case.

See previous version.

The authorized official or court considering the case of administrative offense, at the request of the person who committed the administrative offense, is obliged to take measures to ensure the participation in the case of an advocate providing legal assistance at the expense of the state, in accordance with the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan "On the provision of legal assistance at the expense of the state".

When engaging an advocate providing legal assistance at the expense of the state, the advocate must be provided with time to enter the case of not less than four hours from the moment of confirmation of their participation in it.

#### (Article 297

supplemented by parts three and four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 27, 2024 No. 3RU-915 — National Database of Legislation, 28.02.2024, No. 03/24/915/0160)

#### Article 298. Witness

Any person who may know any circumstances to be established in a given case may be summoned as a witness in a case of administrative offense.

Upon summons of the body (official) in whose proceedings the case is, the witness is obliged to appear at the indicated time, give truthful testimony, report everything known to them about the case and answer the questions posed.

#### Article 299. Expert

See previous version.

A physical person possessing specialized knowledge in the field of science, technology, art or craft necessary for providing an expert opinion may be appointed as an expert.

An expert of a state forensic expert institution, an employee of another enterprise, institution, organization or another physical person may act as an expert.

An expert is appointed by the body (official) in whose proceedings the case of an administrative offense is pending, in cases when specialized knowledge becomes necessary.

Persons recognized as legally incapable or having limited legal capacity in the established manner, as well as persons having an outstanding or unremoved conviction for committing intentional crimes, may not be engaged as an expert.

See previous version.

An expert has the right: to familiarize himself with the materials of the administrative offense case relating to the subject of the expert examination, to extract necessary

information from them or make copies; to file petitions for the submission of additional materials and objects of research necessary for conducting the expert examination; with the permission of the body (official) in whose proceedings the administrative offense case is pending, to be present during the consideration of the case, to ask questions relating to the subject of the expert examination to the person being held liable, the victim, witnesses; to examine physical, written, digital evidence and documents; to set forth in his opinion conclusions not only on the questions that were posed to him, but also on other questions relating to the subject of the expert examination and having significance for the case; to make statements regarding incorrect interpretation by persons participating in the proceedings on the administrative offense case of his opinion or testimony, which are subject to entry in the protocol of consideration of the administrative offense case; to submit an opinion and give testimony in his native language if he does not speak or does not sufficiently speak the language in which the proceedings on the administrative offense case are conducted, and to use the services of an interpreter in this case; to appeal decisions, actions (inaction) of the body (official) in whose proceedings the administrative offense case is pending, if these decisions, actions (inaction) violate his rights and freedoms.

(part five of Article 299 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 21, 2024 No. 3RU-1003 — National Database of Legislation, November 21, 2024, No. 03/24/1003/0943)

An expert is obliged: to conduct a comprehensive and complete examination of the objects of research submitted to him, to provide a substantiated and objective written opinion on the questions posed to him; to appear upon summons of the body (official) in whose proceedings the administrative offense case is pending; to give testimony regarding the expert examination conducted by him and to answer additional questions to clarify the opinion given by him; not to disclose information that became known to him in connection with conducting the expert examination; to ensure the safety of the submitted objects of research and case materials; to observe order during the consideration of the administrative offense case.

(text of Article 299 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated June 1, 2010 No. 3RU-250 — SZ RU, 2010, No. 22, Art. 174)

See previous version.

#### Article 2991. Expert Opinion or Opinion of a Commission of Experts

After conducting expert examinations, an expert or commission of experts prepares an opinion, which is certified by the signature of the expert or each expert who is a member of the commission of experts, respectively.

The opinion must reflect: the date and place of conducting the expert examination; the basis for conducting the expert examination; information about the body (official) that appointed the expert examination; information about the expert (surname, first name, patronymic, education, specialty, work experience, academic degree, academic title, position held) and the organization entrusted with conducting the expert examination; warning to the expert about criminal liability for giving a knowingly false opinion, refusal or evasion from giving an opinion; questions posed to the expert; objects of research and case materials submitted to the expert; information about persons present during the conduct of the expert examination; content and results of expert examinations indicating the methods applied, as well as by whom these expert examinations were conducted, if a commission of experts worked; evaluation of the results of the expert examination, substantiated answers to the questions posed; circumstances having significance for the case and established on the initiative of the expert.

The opinion may indicate the causes of the offense and conditions that facilitated its commission, as well as organizational and technical recommendations for their elimination.

Materials illustrating the opinion and its results are attached to this opinion and serve as its integral part. Materials documenting the course, conditions and results of the expert examination are kept in the state forensic expert institution or other enterprise, institution, organization for the periods established

by legislation. At the request of the body (official) that appointed the expert examination, they are submitted for attachment to the administrative offense case.

The opinion must contain justification for refusal to answer certain

Upon completion of the expert examination, the opinion, objects of research and case materials are sent to the body (official) that appointed the expert examination.

The opinion is not binding on the body (official) in whose proceedings

If the expert becomes convinced that the questions posed cannot be resolved on the basis of his specialized knowledge or the objects of research or materials submitted to him are unsuitable or insufficient for giving an opinion and cannot be supplemented, or the state of science and forensic expert practice does not allow answering the questions posed, he prepares a reasoned act on the impossibility of giving an opinion and sends it to the body (official) that appointed the expert examination.

#### (Article 2991

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated June 1, 2010 No. 3RU-250 — SZ RU, 2010, No. 22, Art. 174)

See previous version.

#### Article 2992. Specialist

For the purpose of providing assistance in the collection, examination and evaluation of evidence by giving consultations (explanations) and assistance in the application of scientific and technical means, an adult person who is not interested in the outcome of the case and possesses special knowledge and skills in the field of science, technology, art or craft may be engaged as a specialist.

Persons recognized as legally incapable or having limited legal capacity in the established manner may not be engaged as a specialist.

See previous version.

A specialist has the right: to familiarize himself with the materials of the case on an administrative offense relating to the subject of examination, to copy necessary information from them or make copies; to file petitions for the submission of additional materials and objects of examination necessary for giving a consultation (explanations); with the permission of the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located, to be present during the consideration of the case, to ask questions to the person being held liable, the victim, witnesses relating to the subject of examination; to examine physical, written, digital evidence and documents; to make statements regarding incorrect interpretation by persons participating in the proceedings on the case of an administrative offense of his consultation (explanations), which are subject to entry in the protocol of consideration of the case on an administrative offense; to submit a consultation and give explanations in his native language if he does not speak or does not sufficiently speak the language in which the proceedings on the case of an administrative offense are conducted, and to use the services of an interpreter in this case; to appeal decisions, actions (inaction) of the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located, if these decisions, actions (inaction) violate his rights and freedoms.

(part three of article 2992 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 21, 2024 No. 3RU-1003 — National Database of Legislation, 21.11.2024, No. 03/24/1003/0943)

A specialist is obliged: to conduct a comprehensive and complete examination of the objects of examination submitted to him, to give a substantiated and objective consultation (explanations) on the questions posed to him; to appear upon summons of the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located; to give testimony regarding the examination conducted and to answer additional questions to clarify the consultation (explanations) given by him; not to disclose information that

became known to him in connection with the conduct of the examination; to ensure the safety of the submitted objects of examination and case materials; to observe order during the consideration of the case on an administrative offense.

The specialist's consultation on all questions that have arisen must be submitted to the court in written form, and the explanations given by him during the consideration of the case on an administrative offense are entered in the protocol of consideration of the case on an administrative offense.

#### (article 2992

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, 30.01.2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

#### Article 300. Interpreter

An interpreter is appointed by the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located.

An interpreter is obliged to appear upon summons of the body (official), to make a complete and accurate translation entrusted to him.

See previous version.

#### Article 301

Expenses subject to reimbursement to victims, witnesses, experts, specialists and interpreters

Victims, witnesses, experts, specialists and interpreters are reimbursed in the established manner for expenses incurred by them in connection with appearing before the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located.

For persons summoned as victims, witnesses, experts, specialists and interpreters, their earnings at their place of work are preserved in the established manner for the time of absence in connection with appearing before the body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located.

An expert, specialist and interpreter have the right to remuneration for the performance of their duties, except in cases when these duties were performed as part of an official assignment.

#### (article 301

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, 30.01.2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

#### Article 302

Compensation for damage caused to a citizen by unlawful bringing to administrative liability

Damage caused to a citizen as a result of unlawful bringing to administrative liability is compensated by the state in full regardless of the fault of the bodies (officials) that considered the case on an administrative offense.

The right to compensation for damage arises on condition of termination of the case on an administrative offense.

The body (official) in whose proceedings the case on an administrative offense is located is obliged to explain to the citizen the procedure for restoring his violated rights and to take measures provided by law to compensate for damage caused to the citizen as a result of unlawful bringing to administrative liability.

## Chapter XXIII. CONSIDERATION OF CASES ON ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSES

#### Article 303. Preparation for consideration of a case on an administrative offense

The body (official) when preparing for consideration of a case on an administrative offense resolves the questions:

1) whether consideration of this case falls within its competence;

2) whether the protocol and other materials of the case on an administrative offense are correctly drawn up;

3) whether the persons participating in the consideration of the case have been notified of the time and place of its consideration;

4) whether the necessary additional materials have been requested;

5) regarding petitions of the person being brought to administrative liability, the victim, legal representatives, lawyer.

See previous version.

#### Article 3031. Return of materials of a case on an administrative offense

In case of lack of jurisdiction (lack of subject-matter jurisdiction) of an administrative case to the given body (official) or sending of materials of a case on an administrative offense in violation of the requirements provided by articles 281 and 282 of this Code, the materials of the case on an administrative offense are returned to the body (official) that drew up the protocol on an administrative offense.

#### (article 3031

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, 30.01.2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

See previous version.

#### Article 304. Place of Consideration of a Case on an Administrative Offense

A case on an administrative offense is considered at the place of its commission.

See previous version.

Cases on administrative offenses provided for by part five of Article 113, Articles 119, 125, 1251, 126, 127, 128, 1281, 1282, 1283, 1284, 1285, 1286, 129, 130, 131, 132, 133, 134, 135, 1351, 136, 139, 140 of this Code may also be considered at the place of registration of vehicles.

(part two of Article 304 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. 3RU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art. 168)

Cases on administrative offenses provided for by Article 187 of this Code are considered at the place of their commission or at the place of residence of the offender.

See previous version.

Commissions on children's issues consider cases on administrative offenses at the place of residence of the offender.

(part four of Article 304 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 11, 2023 No. 3RU-829 — National Database of Legislation, 12.04.2023, No. 03/23/829/0208)

The legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan may provide for another place of consideration of a case on an administrative offense.

#### Article 305. Time Limits for Consideration of a Case on an Administrative Offense

A case on an administrative offense is considered within a fifteen-day period from the date of receipt by the body (official) authorized to consider the case of the protocol on the administrative offense and other case materials.

See previous version.

Cases on administrative offenses provided for by Articles 512, 513, 514, 515, 516, 517, 518, 519, 170, 180, 181, 183, 187, 194, 1941, 1961 of this Code are considered within twenty-four hours, those provided for by Article 47, parts two and three of Article 164, Articles 1975, 1977, 201, 202, 203, 204, 205 of this Code — within seventy-two hours, and by Article 61 of this Code, within a five-day period.

(part two of Article 305 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 25, 2023 No. 3RU-832 — National Database of Legislation, 26.04.2023, No. 03/23/832/0228)

See previous version.

Cases on administrative offenses provided for by Articles 56, 57, 58, part one of Article 611, Articles 94, 1651, 1842, 1843, 189, 1891, 201, 2021, parts one and two of Article 2241, parts one through three of Article 225, Articles 239, 240, 241 of this

Code, when committed by foreign citizens and stateless persons, are considered within twenty-four hours.

(part three of Article 305 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 15, 2022 No. 3RU-753 — National Database of Legislation, 16.02.2022, No. 03/22/753/0133)

See previous version.

In the presence of circumstances provided for by paragraph 11 of Article 271 of this Code, a case on an administrative offense is considered within a thirty-day period from the date of receipt by the body (official) authorized to consider the case of the protocol on the administrative offense and other case materials.

#### (Article 305

supplemented by part four by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 29, 2016 No. 3RU-418 — SZ RU, 2017, No. 1, Art.

#### 1) Article 306. Procedure for Consideration of a Case on an Administrative Offense

Consideration of a case begins with the announcement of the composition of the body or the introduction of the official considering the given case.

See previous version.

When considering a case on an administrative offense in court, on the initiative of the court or at the request of persons participating in the case, audio or video recording of the court session may be conducted.

#### (Article 306

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. 3RU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193)

The presiding officer in the session of the body or the official considering the case announces which case is subject to consideration, who is being held administratively liable, explains to the persons participating in the consideration of the case their rights and obligations, and reads out the protocol on the administrative offense. At the session, persons participating in the consideration of the case are heard, evidence is examined, and petitions are resolved. In the event of a prosecutor's participation in the consideration of the case, his or her opinion is announced.

#### Article 307

Circumstances Subject to Clarification When Considering a Case on an Administrative Offense

The body (official) when considering a case on an administrative offense is obliged to clarify: whether an administrative offense was committed, the time and place of its commission, whether the given person is guilty of its commission, whether he or she is subject to administrative liability, whether there are circumstances mitigating or aggravating liability, whether property damage was caused, as well as other circumstances relevant to the proper consideration of the case.

#### Article 308. Protocol of Consideration of a Case on an Administrative Offense

When considering a case on an administrative offense, a protocol is kept by the body, which indicates:

See previous version.

1) the court case number, date and place of the session;

(paragraph 1 of part one of Article 308 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of September 16, 2021 No. 3RU-716 — National Database of Legislation, 17.09.2021, No. 03/21/716/0877)

2) the name and composition of the body considering the case;

3) the content of the case under consideration;

4) information on the appearance of persons participating in the case;

5) explanations of persons participating in the consideration of the case, their petitions and the results of their consideration;

See previous version.

6) material, written, digital evidence and documents examined during the consideration of the case;

(paragraph 6 of part one of Article 308 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 21, 2024 No. 3RU-1003 — National Database of Legislation, 21.11.2024, No. 03/24/1003/0943)

7) information on the announcement of the adopted decision and explanation of the procedure and time limits for appealing it.

See previous version.

In the event of audio or video recording of a court session, the court session protocol records only matters provided for by paragraphs 1 through 4, 6 of part one of this Article, indicates the start and completion time of explanations of persons participating in the case, testimony of witnesses, oral explanations by experts of their conclusions, debates and the prosecutor's opinion, and also notes the use of technical means of recording the court session. Electronic or other media of audio or video recording are attached to the court session protocol.

In the event of a court session being conducted via videoconference, the court session protocol, in addition to the information provided for in part one of this Article, must also indicate information:

on the conduct of the court session via videoconference;

on the name of the court assisting in conducting the court session via videoconference;

on persons participating in the case and other participants in the judicial process who appeared in the court assisting in conducting the court session via videoconference;

on the information and communication technology staff of the court assisting in conducting the court session via videoconference and the court considering the case.

#### (Article 308

supplemented by parts two and three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. ZRU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193)

At the request of the person brought to administrative liability, the victim and other interested persons, the course of consideration of the case on an administrative offense by an official shall be reflected in a protocol maintained in accordance with the requirements of part one of this article.

The protocol of consideration of a case on an administrative offense is signed respectively by the presiding officer of the body's session and the secretary or by the official considering the case.

See previous version.

The person in respect of whom a decision has been issued in a case on an administrative offense, the victim and the body that drew up the protocol on the administrative offense, have the right to receive a copy of the protocol of consideration of the case on an administrative offense, as well as audio or video recordings of the court session at their own expense.

#### (Article 308

supplemented by part six by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. ZRU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

#### Article 3081

Procedure for resolving the issue of releasing an offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or applying a milder administrative penalty

If a body (official), when considering a case on an administrative offense, comes to the conclusion that the committed administrative offense is insignificant or that there are grounds for applying a penalty below the lowest limit provided for in the sanction of articles of the Special Part of this Code, then the case on the administrative offense is referred to the court with a submission on releasing the offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or on applying a milder administrative penalty.

The submission on releasing an offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or on applying a milder administrative penalty shall set out the motives and grounds by virtue of which the person is subject to release from administrative liability or a milder administrative penalty should be applied to them.

The court, having considered the submission on releasing an offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or on applying a milder administrative penalty, issues one of the decisions provided for by Article 310 of this Code.

The submission on releasing an offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or on applying a milder administrative penalty is considered within three days from the date of receipt by the court of the case on an administrative offense in compliance with the general rules provided for by Articles 303, 304, 306—309 of this Code.

#### (Article 3081

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art.

#### 168) Article 309. Decision on a case on an administrative offense

A body (official), having considered a case on an administrative offense, issues a decision.

See previous version.

The decision must contain: the name of the body (official) that issued the decision; the court case number; the date of consideration of the case; information about the person in respect of whom the case is being considered, including the personal identification number of the offender — individual; a statement of the circumstances established during consideration of the case; reference to the normative act providing for liability for the given administrative offense; the decision made on the case; if available — the personal identification number of the victim — individual or the taxpayer identification number — legal entity, as well as the term and procedure for appealing the decision.

LexUz Commentary

Unofficial translation. Part two of Article 309 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of June 16, 2025 No. ZRU-1069. See official text in Uzbek language.

See previous version.

In cases provided for by Article 3081 of this Code, the decision must also contain a ruling on granting or refusing to grant the submission on releasing an offender from administrative liability due to insignificance of the offense or on applying a milder administrative penalty.

#### (Article 309

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, Art. 168)

See previous version.

If when deciding the issue of applying an administrative penalty by bodies (officials) listed in paragraphs 1 and 3 of Article 242 of this Code, the issue of compensation by the guilty party for property damage is simultaneously decided, then the decision on the case shall indicate the amount of damage subject to recovery, the term and procedure for its compensation.

(part four of Article 309 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 20, 2014 No. ZRU-365 — SZ RU, 2014, No. 4, Art. 45)

The decision on the case must resolve the issue of seized items and documents, as well as detained vehicles.

The decision is adopted by a simple majority of votes of members of the collegial body present at the session.

The decision on a case on an administrative offense is signed by the official, the decision of a body — by the presiding officer and the secretary.

See previous version.

The decision on a case on an administrative offense may be issued and sent for execution in the form of an electronic document through an information system.

#### (Article 309

supplemented by part eight by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. ZRU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193)

In cases provided for by legislation on the application of penalties, a corresponding entry is made in the protocol on the administrative offense.

See previous version.

#### Article 309¹

Decision on the imposition of a fine in cases of recording traffic violations by special automated technical means of photo and video recording

A decision on the imposition of a fine for traffic violations recorded by special automated technical means of photo and video recording shall be issued without the participation of the person who committed the administrative offense.

A decision on the imposition of a fine with attached materials obtained through the use of special automated technical means of photo and video recording shall be executed in the form of an electronic document having legal force and shall be confirmed by the electronic digital signature of the authorized person who compiled it.

A copy of the decision on the imposition of a fine with attached materials obtained through the use of special automated technical means of photo and video recording shall be produced by converting the electronic document into a paper document and sent to the offender by registered mail within three days from the date of issuance of the said decision.

A decision on the imposition of a fine must contain: the date and place of its compilation; the name of the body, position and surname of the person who issued the decision; information about the person being held administratively liable; the time, place and circumstances of the commission of the administrative offense with reference to the part of the article, the article of the Special Part of this Code providing for liability for the given offense; an image of the vehicle with its state registration plate recorded by special automated technical means of photo and video recording at the moment of commission of the offense; the decision taken, the procedure and terms for payment of the imposed fine; the rights and obligations of the person being held administratively liable, as well as the procedural procedure for appealing the decision on the imposition of a fine.

#### (article 309¹

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of August 10, 2015 No. ZRU-389 — SZ RU, 2015, No. 32, art. 425)

See previous version.

#### Article 309²

Decision on the imposition of a fine in cases of recording violations of customs legislation by the Unified Automated Information System of the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan

A decision on the imposition of a fine for violations of customs legislation recorded by the Unified Automated Information System of the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan shall be issued without the participation of the person who committed the administrative offense.

A decision on the imposition of a fine shall be executed in the form of an electronic document having legal force and shall be confirmed by the electronic digital signature of the authorized person who compiled it.

A decision on the imposition of a fine within three days from the date of its issuance shall be sent to the offender in electronic form or handed to him in paper form or sent by mail by registered letter with acknowledgment of receipt.

A decision on the imposition of a fine must contain: the date and place of its compilation; the name of the body, position and surname of the person who issued the decision; information about the

person being held administratively liable; the time, place and circumstances of the commission (detection) of the administrative offense with reference to the part of the article, the article of the Special Part of this Code providing for liability for the given offense; the decision taken, the procedure and terms for payment of the imposed fine; the rights and obligations of the person being held administratively liable, as well as the procedural procedure for appealing the decision on the imposition of a fine.

#### (article 309²

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 4, 2021 No. ZRU-720 — National Database of Legislation, 05.10.2021, No. 03/21/720/0930 — Enters into force on January 6, 2022)

See previous version.

#### Article 309³

Decision on the imposition of a fine for administrative offenses recorded through the Unified Automated Information System of tax authorities

A decision on the imposition of a fine for administrative offenses recorded through the Unified Automated Information System of tax authorities shall be issued without the participation of the person who committed the administrative offense.

A decision on the imposition of a fine for administrative offenses recorded through the Unified Automated Information System of tax authorities shall be executed in the form of an electronic document.

A decision on the imposition of a fine with attached materials obtained through the use of the Unified Automated Information System of tax authorities shall be sent to the personal account of the taxpayer (offender) within three days from the date of issuance of this decision.

A decision on the imposition of a fine for administrative offenses recorded through the Unified Automated Information System of tax authorities must contain: the decision number; the date and place of issuance of the decision; the name of the body that issued the decision; the position, surname, first name, patronymic of the official; the name and identification number of the taxpayer; information about the person being held administratively liable; the time, place and circumstances of the commission of the administrative offense; the article of the Special Part of this Code providing for liability for the committed administrative offense; the imposed fine and the procedure for its payment; the term and procedure for appealing the decision on the imposition of a fine.

#### (article 309³

introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 11, 2022 No. ZRU-758 — National Database of Legislation, 12.03.2022, No. 03/22/758/0207 — Enters into force on June 13, 2022)
