#### Article 310. Types of decisions on cases of administrative offenses

The body (official), having considered a case of an administrative offense, shall issue one of the following decisions:

1) on the application of an administrative penalty;

2) on termination of the case by proceedings.

See previous version.

A decision on termination of the case by proceedings shall be issued in the presence of circumstances provided for by Article 271 of this Code.

(part two of article 310 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, art. 168)

See previous version.

A decision on termination of the case by proceedings by a court shall also be issued upon announcement of a warning.

#### (article 310

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 26, 2011 No. ZRU-289 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 17, art.

#### 168) Article 311

Announcement of the decision on an administrative offense case and delivery of its copy

See previous version. The decision on an administrative offense case is announced immediately upon completion of the consideration of the case and enters into legal force from the moment of its issuance.

(part one of Article 311 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, 30.01.2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

A copy of the decision is delivered or sent within three days to the person in respect of whom it was issued, as well as to the victim at his or her request.

A copy of the decision is delivered against receipt, and in case it is sent, a corresponding entry is made in the case file.

See previous version.

In cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 185, parts three, four and six of Article 220, Article 221 of this Code, in respect of officials of state bodies and employees of legal entities with special service tasks who have the right to use service weapons and ammunition for them, a copy of the decision is sent to the relevant state body or legal entity for information, and to the internal affairs body — for consideration of the issue of prohibiting this person from using weapons.

(part four of Article 311 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on September 2, 2022)

#### Article 312

Bringing the decision on the application of an administrative penalty to the attention of an enterprise, institution, organization and officials

The body (official) considering cases of administrative offenses provided for by Articles 61, 131, 140, 183, 187, 233, 237 of this Code, brings to the attention of the administration at the place of work or study the decision on the application of an administrative penalty to the offender.

The body (official) considering the case of an administrative offense, brings the decision made to the attention of the official who drew up the protocol on the administrative offense.

#### Article 313

Representation on the elimination of causes of committing administrative offenses and conditions conducive to them

The body (official) considering the case, upon identifying the causes of committing administrative offenses and conditions conducive to them, submits to the head of the relevant enterprise, institution, organization a representation on taking measures to eliminate these causes and conditions. The head is obliged within one month from the date of receipt of the representation to inform the body (official) that submitted it of the measures taken.

See previous version.

Chapter XXIII1. PROCEEDINGS ON CASES OF RECONCILIATION

#### Article 3131

Cases of administrative offenses subject to consideration in connection with reconciliation

Cases of administrative offenses provided for by Article 212 of this Code may be considered in connection with reconciliation.

#### Article 3132. Application for reconciliation

An application for reconciliation may be submitted by the victim or his or her legal representative during the consideration of the case by competent bodies (officials), as well as at any stage of judicial proceedings, but before the issuance of a court decision.

The application must indicate the compensation for the damage caused and a request to terminate the proceedings on the administrative offense case in connection with reconciliation.

If an application for reconciliation is submitted during the consideration of the case in the court of first instance, the court immediately proceeds to consider it.

If there are several victims in the case, proceedings on the reconciliation case are possible only upon reaching reconciliation with all victims.

When accepting an application, the competent body (official) and the court are obliged to explain to the victim or his or her legal representative that upon approval of the reconciliation by the court, the victim or he or she loses the right to petition for the resumption of proceedings on the case.

#### Article 3133. Procedure for referring a case of administrative offenses to court

The competent body (official), having received an application for reconciliation from the victim or his or her legal representative, within three days, with the consent of the offender, issues a decision to refer the case to court in connection with the reconciliation of the parties and sends the case according to jurisdiction.

The descriptive part of the decision indicates:

the basis for proceedings on the administrative offense case;

information about the persons who committed the administrative offense and the administrative offenses committed by them;

the content of the application for reconciliation and the offender's attitude to the application.

The operative part of the decision indicates:

the decision to refer the case to court;

See previous version.

information about material, written and digital evidence.

(paragraph three of part three of Article 3133 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated November 21, 2024 No. 3RU-1003 — National Database of Legislation, 21.11.2024, No. 03/24/1003/0943)

If several persons are involved as offenders in the case and reconciliation has not been reached with all of them, the materials in respect of the offenders with whom reconciliation has not been reached are separated and proceedings on them are carried out in compliance with general rules.

The circumstances provided for by part four of this article must be indicated in the decision of the competent body (official) to refer the case to court in connection with the reconciliation of the parties.

#### Article 3134. Judicial proceedings

Judicial proceedings on a reconciliation case must be considered no later than three days from the date of its receipt by the court.

The following participate in the court session: the offender, the victim, their legal representatives and lawyer, if the latter participates in the case.

Judicial proceedings on a reconciliation case begin with the reading of the victim's application for reconciliation.

The court hears the offender and the victim about the circumstances of the committed administrative offense.

The court determines:

the voluntariness of the reconciliation and its motives;

the voluntariness of the offender's admission of guilt;

whether the offender has realized the consequences of the act committed by him or her and has taken measures to redress the damage caused;

whether any pressure was exerted on the victim or the offender;

issues related to compensation for the damage caused;

the consent of the offender, the victim and their legal representatives to reconciliation.

Then the court hears the opinion of the lawyer, if he or she participates in the court session.

If during the court session the involuntariness of the reconciliation, admission of guilt, refusal to compensate for damage are established, the court cancels the decision to refer the case to court in connection with the reconciliation of the parties and considers the case on general grounds.

However, if the act committed contains signs of another administrative offense or elements of a crime, the court cancels the decision to refer the case to court in connection with the reconciliation of the parties and sends the case to the body that drew up the protocol on the administrative offense or initiated the administrative offense case, for consideration in the general procedure or for conducting a pre-investigation check, inquiry and preliminary investigation.

Based on the results of the judicial proceedings, the court issues a decision in the procedure established by law.

During the court session, a protocol is kept according to the rules provided for by Article 308 of this Code.

#### Article 3135. Court Ruling

The introductory part of the court ruling shall indicate:

the time and place of issuance of the ruling;

the name of the court that issued the ruling, the first name, patronymic, surname of the judge, court secretary, parties, interpreter, if the latter participates in the case;

the first name, patronymic and surname of the offender, year, month, day and place of birth, place of residence, place of work, occupation, education and other information relevant to the case.

The descriptive-motivational part of the court ruling shall set out the circumstances that served as the basis for reconciliation, and formulate answers to the questions listed in Article 3134 of this Code.

In the operative part of the ruling, the court decides the following issues:

on termination of the case of administrative offense in connection with reconciliation of the parties or on other grounds specified in Article 310 of this Code, on imposition of administrative penalty or referral of the case to the body that drew up the protocol on administrative offense or initiated the case of administrative offense;

See previous version.

on material, written and digital evidence;

(paragraph three of part three of Article 3135 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 21, 2024 No. ZRU-1003 — National Database of Legislation, November 21, 2024, No. 03/24/1003/0943)

on compensation for damage.

An appeal against the court ruling may be filed by the offender, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer, and a protest by the prosecutor.

(chapter XXIII1 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 4, 2021 No. ZRU-720 — National Database of Legislation, October 5, 2021, No. 03/21/720/0930)

See previous version.

Chapter XXIII2. PROCEEDINGS ON CASES OF PLACEMENT OF A PERSON WITHOUT A FIXED PLACE OF RESIDENCE IN A REHABILITATION CENTER

#### Article 3136

Filing of a Petition for Placement of a Person Without a Fixed Place of Residence in a Rehabilitation Center

The internal affairs body, in case of identification of grounds requiring placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center, must within 48 hours from

the moment of delivery of such person to the internal affairs body prepare materials for submission to the court of a petition for his placement in a rehabilitation center for a period of no more than thirty days.

The petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center and materials confirming the necessity of his placement in such center shall be submitted by the internal affairs body to the district (city) court for criminal cases at the place of discovery of the person without a fixed place of residence or at the location of the rehabilitation center.

#### Article 3137. Form and Content of the Petition

The petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center shall be submitted to the court in written form and signed by the head of the internal affairs body or his deputy.

The petition must indicate:

1) the name of the court to which the petition is submitted;

2) the name of the internal affairs body and its location (postal address);

3) surname, first name, patronymic of the person without a fixed place of residence;

4) circumstances on which the petition is based;

5) evidence confirming the grounds of the submitted petition;

6) request for placement of the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center with indication of the term;

7) list of attached documents.

The petition may indicate telephone numbers, fax numbers, email address of the internal affairs body.

A petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center, submitted in violation of the requirements provided for by this article, shall be returned to the internal affairs body.

#### Article 3138. Documents Attached to the Petition

The following shall be attached to the petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center:

1) a decision approved by the head of the relevant district (city) internal affairs body of the official who identified the person without a fixed place of residence, on submission to the court of a petition for placement of the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center;

2) a certificate from the body issuing information on addresses of residence (address bureau), as well as information on the results of verification of the last known place of residence in the Republic of Uzbekistan of the person without a fixed place of residence;

3) an explanation of the person without a fixed place of residence or a properly drawn up act on refusal to give an explanation by such person;

4) a medical certificate on the results of examination of the person without a fixed place of residence;

5) a protocol on explanation of rights to the person without a fixed place of residence, including the right to use legal assistance of a lawyer and the right to a telephone call to his relatives.

Other documents substantiating the submitted petition may also be attached to the petition.

#### Article 3139. Consideration of the Petition by the Court

The petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center shall be considered at a closed court session by a judge individually within 24 hours from the moment of its submission.

The participation of the person in respect of whom the petition for placement in a rehabilitation center is being considered, and the representative of the internal affairs body in the court session is mandatory. A lawyer may participate in the court session.

Consideration of the petition begins with a report by the representative of the internal affairs body, who substantiates the necessity and term of placement of the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center. Then the person in respect of whom the petition is being considered, the lawyer, if he participates in the court session, other persons who appeared in court are heard, and the submitted materials are examined.

The ruling is issued by the judge in a separate room.

Based on the results of consideration of the petition, the court issues one of the following rulings:

on granting the petition and placing the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center;

on refusal to grant the petition.

The ruling on granting the petition and placing the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center or on refusal to grant the petition shall enter into force from the moment of its announcement and is subject to immediate execution.

The ruling shall be immediately handed over to the representative of the internal affairs body for execution, to the lawyer, if he participated in the court session, and to the person in respect of whom it was issued.

The ruling issued based on the results of consideration of the petition for placement of a person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center may be appealed by the person in respect of whom it was issued, his legal representative and lawyer in the appeal procedure, and the prosecutor may file an appeal protest, within three days from the date of its issuance.

The judge of the court of appeal instance shall individually consider the appeal (protest) against the ruling within three days from the date of its receipt, based on the results of which he issues one of the following rulings:

on leaving the ruling unchanged and the appeal (protest) without satisfaction;

on cancellation of the ruling on granting the petition for placement of the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center and release of the person from the rehabilitation center;

on cancellation of the ruling on leaving the petition without satisfaction and placement of the person without a fixed place of residence in a rehabilitation center.

(chapter XXII2 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 16, 2022 No. ZRU-810 — National Database of Legislation, December 17, 2022, No. 03/22/810/1095)

See previous version.

## CHAPTER XXIV

APPEAL AND PROTEST OF THE RULING (DETERMINATION) ON THE CASE OF ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE

(title of chapter XXIV as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. ZRU-665 — National Database of Legislation, January 13, 2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

#### Article 314

Right to appeal a decision on an administrative offense case

See previous version.

A decision on an administrative offense case may be appealed by the person against whom it was issued, the victim, their legal representatives and attorney, as well as the body that drew up the protocol on the administrative offense.

See previous version.

Appeal of a court decision in criminal cases on an administrative offense case in appellate, cassation and revision procedure is carried out in the manner established by Chapter XXIV1 of this Code.

(part two of Article 314 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 25, 2023 No. 3RU-884 — National Database of Legislation, December 25, 2023, No. 03/23/884/0967 — Enters into force on January 1, 2024)

#### Article 315

Procedure for appealing a decision on an administrative offense case

See previous version.

A decision on an administrative offense case may be appealed to a higher body (official) or to a district (city) court in criminal cases. A decision of an economic court may be appealed in the manner established by the Economic Procedural Code of the Republic of Uzbekistan, and of a court in civil cases — in the manner established by the Civil Procedural Code of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(part one of Article 315 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 25, 2023 No. 3RU-884 — National Database of Legislation, December 25, 2023, No. 03/23/884/0967 — Enters into force on January 1, 2024)

The complaint is filed through the body (official) that issued the decision on the administrative offense case, or directly to the court to which it is addressed.

Upon receiving the complaint, the body (official) that issued the decision on the administrative offense case, within three days, forwards the complaint together with the case to the body (official) authorized in accordance with this article to consider it.

A person who has appealed a decision on an administrative offense case is exempt from payment of state duty.

#### Article 316

Time limit for appealing a decision on an administrative offense case

See previous version.

A complaint against a decision on an administrative offense case, with the exception of a court decision, may be filed within ten days from the date of receipt of a copy of the decision. In case of missing the specified time limit for valid reasons, this time limit may be restored by the body (official) authorized to consider the complaint.

(text of Article 316 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 20, 2019 No. 3RU-531 — National Database of Legislation, March 21, 2019, No. 03/19/531/2799)

#### Article 317. Protest of a decision on an administrative offense case

In an administrative offense case, a protest may be brought by the prosecutor against a decision:

See previous version.

1) of an investigative judge of a court in criminal cases — to the court of appellate, cassation and revision instances in the manner established by Chapter XXIV of this Code;

(paragraph 1 of part one of Article 317 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 28, 2025 No. 3RU-1022 — National Database of Legislation, January 29, 2025, No. 03/25/1022/0083)

2) of another body (official) — to the body (official) that issued it, or to a higher body (official).

See previous version.

A decision of an economic court on an administrative offense case is protested in the manner established by the Economic Procedural Code of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(part two of Article 317 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, January 30, 2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

See previous version.

A decision of a court in civil cases on an administrative offense case is protested in the manner established by the Civil Procedural Code of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

#### (Article 317

supplemented by part three by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 21, 2011 No. 3RU-288 — SZ RU, 2011, No. 16, Art.

#### 162) Article 318

Suspension of execution of a decision on an administrative offense case

Filing a complaint within the established time limit suspends the execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty until consideration of the complaint, with the exception of a decision on the application of administrative arrest and cases of imposition of a fine collected at the place of commission of the administrative offense.

Bringing a protest by the prosecutor suspends the execution of a decision on an administrative offense case until consideration of the protest.

#### Article 319

Consideration of a complaint or protest against a decision on an administrative offense case

A complaint or protest against a decision on an administrative offense case is considered by the bodies (officials) authorized to do so within a ten-day period from the date of their receipt.

The body (official) when considering a complaint or protest against a decision on an administrative offense case verifies the legality, validity and fairness of the issued decision.

See previous version.

A complaint or protest against a decision on an administrative offense case is considered by an investigative judge of a district (city) court in criminal cases individually, and based on the results a decision is made according to the rules provided for by Article 321 of this Code.

(part three of Article 319 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 28, 2025 No. 3RU-1022 — National Database of Legislation, January 29, 2025, No. 03/25/1022/0083)

See previous version.

#### (Article 320

repealed by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 29, 2018 No. 3RU-463 — National Database of Legislation, January 30, 2018, No. 03/18/463/0634 — enters into force on April 1, 2018)

#### Article 321

Decision of the body (official) reviewing a complaint or protest against a ruling on an administrative offense case

The body (official) when reviewing a complaint or protest against a ruling on an administrative offense case shall adopt one of the following decisions:

1) leave the ruling unchanged and the complaint or protest without satisfaction;

2) revoke the ruling and remand the case for new consideration;

3) revoke the ruling and terminate proceedings on the case;

4) modify the measure of penalty within the limits provided by the normative act on liability for administrative offenses, without increasing it.

The grounds for revoking or modifying a ruling on an administrative offense case are: incompleteness or one-sidedness of the case review by the body (official); inconsistency of the decision of the body (official) and the applied norms of the Special Part of this Code and other normative acts providing for administrative liability with the actual circumstances of the case; substantial violations of the rules of proceedings on an administrative offense case; injustice of the applied administrative penalty.

See previous version.

In case of revocation of a ruling with remand for new consideration of cases on traffic violations recorded by special automated technical means of photo and video recording, or cases on

violations of customs legislation recorded by the Unified Automated Information System of the State Customs Committee of the Republic of Uzbekistan, such case against the person who committed the given administrative offense shall be subject to consideration on general grounds taking into account the repetition of offenses previously committed by him.

(part three of Article 321 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan dated October 4, 2021 No. 3RU-720 — National Database of Legislation, 05.10.2021, No. 03/21/720/0930 — Enters into force from January 6, 2022)

A ruling issued by a body (official) not competent to consider the given case shall be revoked, and the case shall be remanded for consideration by the competent body (official).

The decision on a complaint or protest against a ruling on an administrative offense case must indicate: the time and place of its issuance; the official reviewing the complaint or protest; the prosecutor and other participating persons; the person who filed the complaint or the prosecutor who brought the protest; the substance of the appealed or protested ruling on the administrative offense case and the filed complaint, protest; the justification for the decision being issued; the decision on the complaint or protest.

A copy of the decision on a complaint or protest against a ruling on an administrative offense case shall be sent within three days to the person against whom it was issued, to the victim — at his request. The prosecutor shall be informed of the results of reviewing the protest.

#### Article 322

Powers of the body (official) to review a ruling on an administrative offense case

A ruling of a body (official) on an administrative offense case may be revoked or modified by the head of a higher body, and upon protest of the prosecutor also by the body that issued it, on the grounds provided by parts two and three of Article 321 of this Code.

#### Article 323

Protesting a decision on a complaint against a ruling on an administrative offense case

A decision on a complaint against a ruling on an administrative offense case may be protested by the prosecutor.

A protest against a decision on a complaint shall be submitted to a higher body in relation to the body (official) that adopted the decision on the complaint.

#### Article 324

Consequences of revoking a ruling with termination of proceedings on an administrative offense case

Revocation of a ruling with termination of proceedings on an administrative offense case entails the return of collected monetary amounts, items seized for consideration and confiscated items, as well as cancellation of other restrictions associated with the previously adopted ruling. In case of impossibility of returning an item, its value shall be compensated, as well as lost profit, if the given items were intended for entrepreneurship.

Compensation for damage caused to a citizen by unlawful administrative arrest shall be made in the manner established by legislation.

See previous version.

## CHAPTER XXIV

VERIFICATION OF LEGALITY, VALIDITY AND FAIRNESS OF A COURT RULING ON CRIMINAL CASES ON AN ADMINISTRATIVE OFFENSE CASE

#### § 1. Proceedings in the court of appellate instance

#### Article 3241. Procedure for filing an appellate complaint (protest)

A person against whom a court ruling on an administrative offense case has been issued, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer, as well as the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense, have the right to file

an appellate complaint against the ruling of the court of first instance, and the prosecutor — to bring a protest. Persons who filed an appellate complaint (protest) have the right to submit additional materials confirming their arguments.

A copy of the appellate complaint (protest) within twenty-four hours shall be sent to the person against whom the court ruling on the administrative offense case was issued, to the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer, as well as to the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense. The indicated persons have the right to familiarize themselves with the case, including with additional materials, as well as to submit their objections to the court.

A complaint (protest) against a court ruling on an administrative offense case together with the case shall be subject to forwarding to the court of appellate instance within three days.

A complaint (protest) against a court ruling on administrative arrest or administrative expulsion shall be subject to forwarding to the court of appellate instance on the day of receipt of the complaint (protest).

An appellate complaint (protest) shall be addressed to the court of appellate instance, but filed with the court that issued the ruling on the administrative offense case.

#### Article 3242. Courts reviewing cases in appellate procedure

The Court of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regional, Tashkent city courts verify in appellate procedure the legality, validity and fairness of rulings issued by the district (city) court on criminal cases in the first instance based on the results of consideration of administrative offense cases.

#### Article 3243. Time limits for filing an appellate complaint (protest)

An appellate complaint (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance on an administrative offense case may be filed within ten days from the day of announcement of the ruling, and by the person against whom the court ruling was issued and the victim within the same period from the day of delivery or receipt by him of a copy of the ruling.

In case of missing the time limit provided by part one of this article, upon motion of the person filing the appellate complaint (protest), the court that issued the ruling has the right to restore the missed time limit if it recognizes the reasons for missing it as valid, for which a determination shall be issued.

A determination shall be issued on rejection of a motion to restore the time limit for appellate appeal (protest) of a court ruling on an administrative offense, against which a private complaint (private protest) may be filed.

#### Article 3244. Requirements for the Content of an Appeal (Protest)

The appeal (protest) must indicate the following:

1) the name of the court to which the appeal (protest) is directed;

2) the person filing the appeal (protest), their procedural status, information about their place of residence or location;

3) the name of the court that issued the ruling, the date of the ruling, the court case number, information about the person in respect of whom the ruling against which the appeal (protest) is filed was issued;

4) the arguments of the person filing the appeal (protest) as to what in their opinion constitutes the incorrectness of the ruling, as well as the content of their request;

5) evidence by which the person filing the appeal (protest) substantiates their claims, in particular evidence not examined by the court of first instance;

6) newly attached evidence and the reason for its non-submission to the court of first instance;

7) a list of materials attached to the appeal (protest);

8) the date of filing the appeal (protest), the signature of the person filing the appeal (protest).

If the content of the appeal (protest) does not meet the requirements of part one of this article and this subsequently prevents consideration of the case on the merits, the judge of the court of first instance within three days issues a ruling on the return of the appeal (protest) to the person who filed it with the establishment of a deadline for new preparation of the appeal (protest).

In cases where the requirements of the judge's ruling on the return of the appeal (protest) were not fulfilled and the appeal (protest) did not arrive within the established time, it is considered not filed.

#### Article 3245. Withdrawal of an Appeal (Protest)

A person who has filed an appeal against a court ruling on an administrative offense case has the right to withdraw it. The right to withdraw an appellate protest belongs to the prosecutor who filed the protest, as well as to a higher prosecutor.

A person in respect of whom a court ruling on an administrative offense case was issued has the right to withdraw the appeal of their lawyer.

A lawyer, legal representative of a person being brought to administrative responsibility has the right to withdraw their appeal only with the consent of the person being brought to administrative responsibility.

Withdrawal of an appeal (protest) is permitted before the appellate court retires to a separate room to make a decision.

The document on withdrawal of the appeal (protest) is kept in the case file.

#### Article 3246. Time Limits for Consideration of an Appeal (Protest)

An appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance on an administrative offense case is subject to consideration by the appellate court within fifteen days from the date of its receipt with all case materials.

Appeals (protests) against rulings of the court of first instance on administrative offense cases provided for in Articles 512, 513, 514, 515, 516, 517, 518, 519 of this Code are subject to consideration by the appellate court within a five-day period from the date of their receipt with all case materials.

An appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance on administrative arrest or administrative expulsion is subject to consideration by the appellate court within twenty-four hours from the moment of filing the appeal (protest).

#### Article 3247. Court Notices and Summons

Persons participating in the case are notified of the time and place of consideration of the appeal (protest) by court summons, telephone messages, telegrams and other means of communication ensuring recording of the fact of notification.

#### Article 3248. Procedure for Consideration of an Appeal (Protest)

An appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance on an administrative offense case is considered by a judge of the appellate court individually.

The appellate court considers an administrative offense case in a court session according to the rules for consideration of administrative offense cases provided for in Chapter XXIII of this Code, taking into account the specifics established by this paragraph.

When considering an administrative offense case in the appellate instance, the court session is opened by the presiding judge, who announces which case and on whose appeal (protest) is being considered. After this, the presiding judge announces the composition of the court, reports the surname, first name, patronymic of the secretary of the court session, as well as the prosecutor, lawyer and interpreter, if they participate in the court session.

The presiding judge ascertains which of the persons participating in the case and their representatives have appeared, establishes the identity of those who have appeared, verifies the powers of officials and representatives, explains to the persons participating in the case their rights and obligations, and resolves motions filed.

Non-appearance of persons duly notified of the time and place of the court session is not an obstacle to consideration of the case on appeal.

Consideration of an administrative offense case in the appellate court begins with a report by the judge presiding in the court session. During the report, the circumstances of the administrative offense case, the content of the ruling of the court of first instance, and the arguments of the appeal (protest) are set forth.

After the report, explanations are heard from persons participating in the case and their representatives who have appeared in the court session. The first to speak is the person who filed the appeal or their representative, or the prosecutor if an appellate protest was filed by them.

Having heard the statements of persons participating in the case, the appellate court, in the presence of appropriate motions or on its own initiative, examines the evidence available in the case and new evidence submitted to the court (requested by the court).

The court, not being limited by the arguments of the appeal (protest), verifies the additional materials attached to it and the materials of the administrative offense case in full.

Upon completion of clarification of the circumstances of the case and examination of evidence, the appellate court provides persons participating in the case with the opportunity to speak in court debates in the same sequence in which they gave explanations.

Upon completion of court debates, the judge retires to a separate room to make a decision.

Based on the results of consideration of the case on appeal, a ruling is issued.

#### Article 3249. Powers of the appellate court

Based on the results of consideration of an appellate complaint (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance, the appellate court adopts one of the following rulings:

1) to leave the ruling of the court of first instance unchanged, and the appellate complaint (protest) — without satisfaction;

2) to revoke the ruling of the court of first instance, as well as the ruling of the body (official) and to return the case to the body (official) that drew up the protocol on the administrative offense;

3) to amend or revoke the ruling of the court of first instance and issue a new ruling;

4) to revoke the ruling of the court of first instance and terminate the proceedings in the case;

5) to amend the measure of penalty within the limits provided by the act of legislation on liability for administrative offenses, without increasing it;

6) to terminate the appellate proceedings in connection with the withdrawal of the appellate complaint (protest).

The ruling of the appellate court is announced immediately after its issuance.

The ruling of the appellate court may be appealed (protested) in the supervisory review procedure.

Article 32410. Ruling of the appellate court

1) the name and composition of the court that issued the ruling;

2) the number of the court case, the date and place of issuance of the ruling;

3) the case for which the ruling was issued;

4) the person (surname, first name, patronymic) who filed the appellate complaint (protest);

5) the content of the appealed (protested) judicial acts;

6) the conclusions of the court based on the results of consideration of the appellate complaint (protest) and references to laws and other acts of legislation that the court was guided by.

The ruling of the appellate court is signed by the judge.

The ruling of the appellate court enters into legal force from the day of its issuance.

The ruling of the appellate court is sent for execution to the court that issued it no later than three days from the day of issuance together with the case.

Article 32411. Sending (delivery) of a copy of the ruling of the appellate court

A copy of the ruling of the appellate court within three days after its issuance is delivered or sent to the person in respect of whom the ruling was issued in the case of an administrative offense, to the body that drew up the protocol on the administrative offense, as well as to the victim — at his request.

The ruling issued based on the results of consideration of an appellate complaint (protest) against a court ruling on administrative arrest or on administrative expulsion is brought to the attention of the body, official executing the ruling, as well as the person in respect of whom the ruling was issued, and the victim on the day of issuance of the ruling.

#### § 2. Proceedings in the Court of Cassation

Article 32412. Review of Judicial Acts in Cassation Procedure

Rulings of the court of first instance in cases of administrative offenses may be reviewed in cassation procedure if they have not been reviewed in appellate procedure.

Article 32413. Right to File a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

A person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of administrative offense, the victim, their legal representatives, an advocate and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense, have the right to file an appeal against judicial acts in cassation procedure, after the expiration of the time limit for appeal in appellate procedure, if the case has not been reviewed in appellate procedure.

The Prosecutor General of the Republic of Uzbekistan, his deputies, the Prosecutor of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, prosecutors of regions, the city of Tashkent, district, city and equivalent prosecutors, as well as their deputies have the right to protest judicial acts in cassation procedure, after the expiration of the time limit for protest in appellate procedure, if the case has not been reviewed in appellate procedure.

Persons specified in parts one and two of this article have the right to provide additional materials confirming the arguments set forth in the cassation appeal (protest).

Article 32414. Procedure and Time Limit for Filing a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

A cassation appeal (protest) may be filed:

1) against rulings of district (city) courts in criminal cases — to the Court of the Republic of Karakalpakstan, regional, Tashkent city courts;

2) against rulings of territorial military courts — to the Military Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Cassation appeals (protests) are filed through the court that issued the ruling. In case of filing an appeal (protest) directly to the court of cassation instance, the court

forwards them to the court that issued the ruling, for fulfillment of the requirements set forth in Article 3241 of this Code.

A cassation appeal (protest) may be filed within six months from the date of issuance of the ruling of the court of first instance.

In case of missing the time limit provided for by part three of this article, upon the motion of the person filing the cassation appeal (protest), the court of cassation instance has the right to restore the missed time limit if it recognizes the reasons for missing it as valid, whereupon a ruling is issued.

Article 32415. Requirements for the Content of a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

The content of a cassation appeal (protest) must meet the requirements of Article 3244 of this Code.

In case these requirements are not fulfilled, the court adopts one of the decisions provided for by parts two and three of Article 3244 of this Code.

Article 32416. Time Limits for Review of a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

A cassation appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance issued in a case of administrative offense is subject to review by the court of cassation instance within fifteen days from the date of its receipt together with all case materials.

Article 32417. Notification of Persons Participating in the Case

Upon receipt of a cassation appeal (protest), the court that reviewed the case in the first instance notifies within three days the persons participating in the case, if the appeal (protest) affects their interests.

The persons specified in part one of this article are sent a copy of the cassation appeal (protest) or a notification of the possibility to familiarize themselves with its electronic copy through the internet resource of the court that issued the ruling. At the same time, the parties are informed of their right to submit their objections to the cassation appeal (protest) in written form or in the form of an electronic document with indication of the time limit for submitting objections. Objections received to the cassation appeal (protest) are attached to the case.

The persons specified in part one of this article are notified by the court of first instance of the submission of the case for review by the court of cassation instance.

Article 32418. Withdrawal of a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

A person who filed a cassation appeal against a court ruling in a case of administrative offense has the right to withdraw it. The right to withdraw a cassation protest belongs to the prosecutor who filed the protest, as well as to a higher prosecutor.

A person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of administrative offense has the right to withdraw the cassation appeal of his advocate.

An advocate, legal representative of a person brought to administrative responsibility has the right to withdraw his cassation appeal only with the consent of the person brought to administrative responsibility.

Withdrawal of a cassation appeal (protest) is permitted before the court of cassation instance retires to a separate room to make a decision.

Upon withdrawal of a cassation appeal (protest), the court of cassation instance issues a ruling terminating the cassation proceedings, if the ruling of the court has not been appealed by other persons.

The document on withdrawal of a cassation appeal (protest) is kept in the case.

Article 32419. Procedure for Review of a Cassation Appeal (Protest)

A cassation appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance in a case of administrative offense is reviewed by a judge of the court of cassation instance individually.

The court of cassation instance reviews the case of administrative offense

When reviewing a case of administrative offense in the cassation instance, the court session is opened by the presiding judge, who announces which case and upon whose cassation appeal (protest) is being reviewed. After that, the presiding judge announces the composition of the court, the surname, first name, patronymic of the secretary of the court session, as well as the prosecutor, advocate and interpreter, if they participate in the court session.

The presiding judge ascertains which of the persons participating in the case and their representatives have appeared, establishes the identity of those who appeared, verifies the powers of officials and representatives, explains to the persons participating in the case their rights and obligations, and resolves motions filed.

Non-appearance of persons duly notified of the time and place of the court session is not an obstacle to reviewing the case in cassation procedure.

Review of a case of administrative offense in the court of cassation instance begins with a report by the judge presiding in the court session. During the report, the circumstances of the case of administrative offense, the content of the ruling of the court of first instance, the arguments of the cassation appeal (protest) are set forth.

After the report, explanations are heard from persons participating in the case and their representatives who appeared in the court session. The first to speak is the person who filed the cassation appeal, or his representative, or the prosecutor, if a cassation protest has been filed by him.

Having heard the statements of persons participating in the case, the court of cassation instance, upon relevant motions or on its own initiative, examines the evidence available in the case and new evidence submitted to the court (requested by the court).

The court, not being limited by the arguments of the cassation appeal (protest), verifies the additional materials attached to it and the materials of the case of administrative offense in full.

Upon completion of clarifying the circumstances of the case and examining the evidence, the court of cassation instance provides persons participating in the case with the opportunity to speak in court debates in the same sequence in which they gave explanations.

Upon completion of court debates, the judge retires to a separate room to make a decision.

Based on the results of reviewing the case in cassation procedure, a ruling is adopted.

Copies of the ruling are sent to the person who filed the cassation appeal (protest) and other persons participating in the case, in the procedure provided for by Article 32411 of this Code.

Article 32420. Powers of the Court of Cassation Instance

Based on the results of reviewing a cassation appeal (protest) against a ruling of the court of first instance, the court of cassation instance adopts one of the following rulings:

1) to leave the ruling of the court of first instance unchanged, and the cassation appeal (protest) without satisfaction;

2) to cancel the ruling of the court of first instance, as well as the ruling of the body (official) and return the case to the body (official) that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense;

3) to modify or cancel the ruling of the court of first instance and issue a new ruling;

4) to cancel the ruling of the court of first instance and terminate the proceedings in the case;

5) to modify the measure of penalty within the limits provided for by the act of legislation on liability for administrative offense, without increasing it;

6) to terminate the cassation proceedings in connection with the withdrawal of the cassation appeal (protest).

The ruling of the court of cassation instance is announced immediately after its issuance.

The ruling of the court of cassation instance may be appealed (protested) in supervisory procedure.

Article 32421. Grounds for Modification or Cancellation of a Judicial Act

The grounds for modification or cancellation of a judicial act in cassation procedure are the illegality, groundlessness and injustice of the judicial act.

A judicial act that is correct in substance cannot be cancelled on purely formal grounds alone.

Article 32422. Ruling of the Court of Cassation Instance

The ruling of the court of cassation instance must indicate:

1) the name and composition of the court that issued the ruling;

2) the number of the court case, the date and place of issuance of the ruling;

3) the case in respect of which the ruling was issued;

4) the person (surname, first name, patronymic) who filed the cassation appeal (protest);

5) the content of the appealed (protested) judicial acts;

6) the conclusions of the court based on the results of reviewing the cassation appeal (protest) and references to laws and other acts of legislation that the court was guided by.

The ruling of the court of cassation instance is signed by the judge.

The ruling of the court of cassation instance enters into legal force from the day of its issuance.

The ruling of the court of cassation instance is sent for execution to the court that issued it no later than three days from the date of issuance together with the case.

#### § 3. Proceedings in the Court of Supervisory Review

Article 32423. Rulings of the Court that May Be Reviewed in Supervisory Review Proceedings

Rulings of district (city) courts in criminal cases, territorial military courts reviewed in appellate or cassation proceedings, as well as rulings of the court of appellate or cassation instance are reviewed in supervisory review proceedings by the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Article 32424. Persons Having the Right to File a Complaint Against Judicial Acts in Supervisory Review Proceedings

The person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense, have the right to file a complaint in supervisory review proceedings against rulings of the court of first instance reviewed in appellate or cassation proceedings, as well as against rulings of the court of appellate or cassation instance.

Article 32425. Persons Having the Right to File a Protest Against Judicial Acts in Supervisory Review Proceedings

The Prosecutor General of the Republic of Uzbekistan or his deputies have the right to file a protest in supervisory review proceedings against rulings of the court of first instance reviewed in

appellate or cassation proceedings, as well as against rulings of courts of appellate or cassation instances to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Article 32426. Time Period Within Which Judicial Acts May Be Reviewed in Supervisory Review Proceedings

A complaint (protest) may be filed within six months from the date of issuance of the ruling of the court of appellate or cassation instance.

In case of missing the time period provided for in part one of this article, upon the motion of the person filing the complaint (protest), the court of supervisory review instance has the right to restore the missed time period if it recognizes the reasons for missing it as valid, about which a determination is issued.

Article 32427. Requirements for the Content of a Complaint (Protest) Filed in Supervisory Review Proceedings

A complaint (protest) in supervisory review proceedings must indicate the following:

1) the name of the court reviewing the case in supervisory review proceedings, to which the complaint (protest) is directed;

2) the person who filed the complaint (protest), his procedural status, information about his place of residence or location;

3) the number of the court case, the date of issuance of the ruling against which the complaint (protest) is filed, the name of the court of first, appellate, cassation instance;

4) the arguments of the person filing the complaint (protest) about what, in his opinion, constitutes the incorrectness of the ruling, as well as the content of his request;

5) evidence by which the person who filed the complaint (protest) substantiates his demands;

6) a list of materials attached to the complaint (protest);

7) the date of filing the complaint (protest), the signature of the person who filed the complaint (protest).

Article 32428. Requesting a Case of an Administrative Offense for Review in Supervisory Review Proceedings and the Time Period for Consideration of a Complaint (Protest) in Supervisory Review Proceedings

The Chairman of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies, as well as a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan have the right to request a case of an administrative offense for review from the court upon a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings.

The Prosecutor General of the Republic of Uzbekistan and his deputies have the right to request from the court a case of an administrative offense to decide the question of filing a protest in supervisory review proceedings. The basis for requesting cases of an administrative offense are complaints of persons specified in Article 32424 of this Code.

A complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings is subject to consideration within a period of up to one month, and in case of requesting a case of an administrative offense — within a period of up to two months.

Article 32429. Withdrawal of a Complaint (Protest) Filed in Supervisory Review Proceedings

A person who filed a complaint in supervisory review proceedings against a court ruling in a case of an administrative offense has the right to withdraw it. The right to withdraw a protest filed in supervisory review proceedings belongs to the prosecutor who filed the protest, as well as to a higher prosecutor.

A person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense has the right to withdraw the complaint of his lawyer filed in supervisory review proceedings.

A lawyer, legal representative of a person brought to administrative responsibility has the right to withdraw his complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings only with the consent of the person brought to administrative responsibility.

Withdrawal of a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings is permitted before the court of supervisory review instance retires to a separate room.

The document on withdrawal of a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings is left in the case.

Article 32430. Return of a Complaint (Protest) Filed in Supervisory Review Proceedings Without Consideration

A complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings is returned without consideration if:

1) the complaint (protest) does not meet the requirements provided for in Article 32427 of this Code;

2) the complaint (protest) was not filed by persons specified in Articles 32424 and 32425 of this Code;

3) the complaint (protest) was filed after the expiration of the established time period;

4) an application for withdrawal of a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings was received;

5) the case of an administrative offense is not subject to consideration in supervisory review proceedings.

After elimination of the circumstances specified in paragraphs 1 and 2 of part one of this article, the person who filed the complaint (protest) has the right to apply again on general grounds.

Article 32431. Decisions Made on Complaints Filed in Supervisory Review Proceedings Received by the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

A complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings is studied by a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan together with the materials attached to it, and in necessary cases with the requesting of the case.

Based on the results of studying the complaint, a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan by his determination makes one of the following decisions:

1) on the absence of grounds for review of judicial decisions in supervisory review proceedings;

2) on transfer of the complaint together with the case for consideration to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

The decision made is communicated within three days to the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings.

Article 32432. Determination on the Absence of Grounds for Review of Judicial Decisions in Supervisory Review Proceedings in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

The determination on the absence of grounds for review of judicial decisions in supervisory review proceedings in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan must indicate:

1) the place and date of issuance of the determination;

2) the surname, first name, patronymic of the judge who issued the determination;

3) data about the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings;

4) the name of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance that issued the ruling, the date of issuance of the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance;

5) the content of the complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings;

6) the reasons and grounds for refusal to transfer the complaint in supervisory review proceedings for consideration to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

The determination of a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan on the absence of grounds for review of judicial decisions in supervisory review proceedings in the Judicial Panel for

Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan may be appealed to the Chairman of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan, his deputy by the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings.

The Chairman of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan, his deputy upon the complaint of the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings has the right to issue a determination on cancellation of the determination of a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan on the absence of grounds for review of judicial decisions in supervisory review proceedings and transfer the complaint together with the case for consideration in supervisory review proceedings to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Article 32433. Determination on Transfer of a Complaint Filed in Supervisory Review Proceedings for Consideration to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

The determination of a judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan on transfer of a complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings for consideration to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan must indicate:

1) the place and date of issuance of the determination;

2) the surname, first name, patronymic of the judge who issued the determination;

3) data about the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings;

4) the name of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance that issued the ruling, the date of issuance of the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance;

5) the content of the complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings;

6) the reasons and grounds for transfer of the complaint in supervisory review proceedings for consideration to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan;

7) the place and date of consideration of the case in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

A judge of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan with the determination issued by him transfers the complaint filed in supervisory review proceedings together with the case to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Article 32434. Notice of Filing a Complaint (Protest) to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan for Consideration in Supervisory Review Proceedings

The person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense, if the complaint (protest) affects their interests, are notified of the filing of a complaint (protest) to the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan for consideration in supervisory review proceedings.

The persons specified in part one of this article are sent a copy of the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings or are informed of the possibility of familiarizing themselves with its electronic copy through the internet resource of the court that issued the ruling.

Article 32435. Preparation of a Case for Consideration in Supervisory Review Proceedings in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

After receipt of the case together with the determination of a judge of the Supreme Court and a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings, the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan no later than three days before the beginning of consideration of the case on filing a complaint (protest) in supervisory review proceedings notifies the person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense,

the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense if the complaint (protest) concerns their interests. These persons are sent a copy of the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings or are informed of the possibility of familiarizing themselves with its electronic copy through the internet resource of the court that issued the ruling. At the same time, the parties are explained their right to submit their objections to the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings in written form or in the form of an electronic document with indication of the time period for submitting objections. Objections received to the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings are attached to the case.

After fulfillment of the requirements provided for in part one of this article, the person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense if the complaint (protest) affects their interests are notified no later than three days before the beginning of consideration of the case of the time of consideration of the case in supervisory review proceedings.

Article 32436. Participation of Parties When Considering a Case of an Administrative Offense in Supervisory Review Proceedings in a Court Session of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

When considering a case of an administrative offense in supervisory review proceedings, the person against whom a court ruling has been issued in a case of an administrative offense, the victim, their legal representatives, lawyer and the body that compiled the protocol on the administrative offense, if the complaint (protest) affects their interests, have the right to participate in the court session of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

In cases in which a protest has been filed in supervisory review proceedings by a prosecutor, the participation of the Prosecutor General of the Republic of Uzbekistan, his deputy or a prosecutor authorized by them is mandatory.

Article 32437. Procedure for Consideration of a Case in Supervisory Review Proceedings in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan

In supervisory review proceedings in the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan, a case is considered by a judge of the Supreme Court individually.

When considering a case of an administrative offense in supervisory review proceedings by the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan, the court session is opened by the presiding judge in the case, who announces which case and upon whose complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings is being considered. After this, the presiding judge announces the composition of the court, reports the surname, first name, patronymic of the secretary of the court session, as well as the prosecutor, lawyer and interpreter, if they participate in the court session.

The presiding judge ascertains which of the persons participating in the case, their representatives have appeared, establishes the identity of those who appeared, verifies the powers of officials and representatives, explains to the persons participating in the case their rights and obligations, resolves filed motions.

Non-appearance of persons duly notified of the time and place of the court session is not an obstacle to consideration of the case in supervisory review proceedings.

Consideration of a case of an administrative offense in the court of supervisory review instance begins with a report by the judge presiding in the court session. During the report, the circumstances of the case of an administrative offense, the content of the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance, the arguments of the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings are set forth.

After the report, explanations of persons participating in the case who appeared in the court session, their representatives are heard. The first to speak is the person who filed the complaint in supervisory review proceedings, or his representative, or the prosecutor, if a protest has been filed by him in supervisory review proceedings.

Having heard the speeches of persons participating in the case, the court of supervisory review instance, in the presence of appropriate motions or on its own initiative, examines the evidence available in the case and new evidence submitted to the court (requested by the court).

The court, not being limited to the arguments of the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings, verifies the additional materials attached to it and the materials of the case of an administrative offense in full.

Upon completion of clarification of the circumstances of the case and examination of evidence, the court of supervisory review instance provides persons participating in the case with the opportunity to speak in court debates in the same sequence in which they gave explanations.

Upon completion of court debates, the judge retires to a separate room to make a decision.

Based on the results of consideration of the case in supervisory review proceedings, a ruling is made.

On the course and content of the court proceedings, in accordance with the requirements established in Article 308 of this Code, a protocol of the court session is compiled.

Article 32438. Powers of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan When Considering a Case in Supervisory Review Proceedings

Based on the results of consideration of a case in supervisory review proceedings, the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan makes one of the following rulings:

1) on leaving the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance unchanged, and the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings without satisfaction;

2) on cancellation of the ruling of the court of appellate or cassation instance and leaving unchanged the ruling of the court of first instance;

3) on cancellation of all judicial decisions made in the case and termination of proceedings in the case;

4) on amendment or cancellation of the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance and adoption of a new ruling;

5) on amendment of the measure of penalty within the limits provided for by the act of legislation on responsibility for an administrative offense, without strengthening it;

6) on termination of proceedings in supervisory review proceedings, in case of withdrawal of a complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings.

Article 32439. Ruling of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan Considering a Case in Supervisory Review Proceedings

The ruling of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan considering a case in supervisory review proceedings must indicate:

1) the number of the court case, the date and place of issuance of the ruling;

2) the name and composition of the court, the prosecutor and other persons who participated in consideration of the case;

3) the person (surname, first name, patronymic) who filed the complaint (protest) in supervisory review proceedings;

4) the content of the operative part of the ruling against which the complaint (protest) was filed in supervisory review proceedings;

5) the content of the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings, objections and explanations of persons participating in consideration of the case in supervisory review proceedings, as well as the opinion of the prosecutor;

6) the decision of the court considering the case upon the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings.

In case of leaving the complaint (protest) filed in supervisory review proceedings without satisfaction, the ruling of the court considering the case in supervisory review proceedings must indicate the grounds for recognizing the ruling of the court of first, appellate or cassation instance as lawful, substantiated and fair, leaving the complaint (protest) without satisfaction.

In case of cancellation or amendment of the ruling, the ruling of the court considering the case in supervisory review proceedings must indicate the arguments about which norms of law were violated and how the unreasonableness and unfairness of the cancelled or amended decision is expressed.

The ruling of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan is signed by the judge.

The ruling of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan considering a case in supervisory review proceedings is announced in the courtroom and enters into legal force from the day of its issuance.

The ruling of the Judicial Panel for Criminal Cases of the Supreme Court of the Republic of Uzbekistan issued based on the results of consideration of a case in supervisory review proceedings is final.

The ruling of the court considering a case in supervisory review proceedings is sent for execution to the court that issued it or to another court together with the case no later than three days from the day of issuance.

#### § 4

Proceedings for the review of judicial acts that have entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

Article 32440. Right of the court to review a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

A criminal court may review a judicial act issued by it that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances on the grounds and in the manner provided for in this paragraph.

Article 32441. Courts reviewing judicial acts that have entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

A ruling that has entered into legal force, issued by a court of first instance, shall be reviewed on the basis of newly discovered circumstances by the criminal court that issued that ruling.

Review on the basis of newly discovered circumstances of rulings of an appellate, cassation or supervisory instance court that amended a judicial act or adopted a new judicial act shall be carried out in the instance in which the judicial act was amended or a new judicial act was adopted.

Article 32442. Grounds for review of judicial acts on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

The grounds for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances are:

1) circumstances material to the case that existed at the time the judicial act was adopted but were not and could not have been known to the applicant;

2) knowingly false expert opinion, knowingly false testimony of a witness, knowingly incorrect translation, established by a court verdict that has entered into legal force, which resulted in the adoption of an unlawful or unfounded judicial act in the given case;

3) criminal acts of a person participating in the case or his representative, or criminal acts of a judge committed during the consideration of the given case, established by a verdict or ruling of a court that has entered into legal force;

4) revocation of an act of a criminal court or a civil court or an economic court, or a decision or other legal document of another body that served as the basis for the adoption of a judicial act in the given case.

#### Article 324

[43]. Procedure and time limit for filing an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

  None  None
None  None

Documents confirming the sending of copies of the application and documents attached to it to other persons participating in the case shall be attached to the application.

At the request of the person who filed the application, the missed time limit for filing the application may be restored by the court provided that the request is filed later than one month from the date of discovery of the circumstances that are grounds for review, and the court recognizes the reasons for missing the time limit as valid.

Applications filed after the expiration of one year from the date of issuance of the judicial act shall not be subject to consideration.

#### Article 324

[44]. Form and content of an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

An application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances shall be filed with the court in written form. The application shall be signed by the person filing it or his representative.

The application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances must indicate:

1) the name of the court to which the application is filed;

2) the persons (surname, first name, patronymic) participating in the case, their location (postal address) or place of residence;

3) the name of the court that issued the judicial act, the review of which on the basis of newly discovered circumstances is requested by the applicant; the case number, the date of issuance of the judicial act;

4) the newly discovered circumstance that, in the opinion of the applicant, is the ground for review of the judicial act that has entered into legal force, with reference to documents;

5) the demand of the person filing the application;

6) a list of attached documents.

The following must be attached to the application:

1) copies of documents confirming the newly discovered circumstances;

2) a copy of the judicial act the review of which is requested by the applicant;

3) in case the application is signed by a representative — a document confirming the authority to sign it.

#### Article 324

[45]. Acceptance of an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances for proceedings

An application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances, filed in compliance with the requirements imposed by this Code on its form and content, shall be accepted for proceedings by the relevant court.

The question of accepting the application for court proceedings shall be decided by a judge individually no later than five days from the date of its receipt by the court.

The court shall issue a ruling on the acceptance of the application for proceedings.

Copies of the ruling shall be sent to the persons participating in the case in the manner provided for in Article 324[1] of this Code.

#### Article 324

[46]. Return of an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

The court shall return an application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances if, when deciding the question of accepting it for proceedings, it establishes that:

1) the application was filed in violation of the rules established by Articles 324[43] and 324[44] of this Code;

2) the application was filed after the expiration of the established time limit and there is no request for its restoration;

3) the reasons for missing the time limit are not valid, and therefore there is no ground for restoring the missed time limit for filing the application.

A ruling shall be issued on the return of the application.

The court's ruling on the return of the application may be appealed (protested).
