After elimination of the circumstances specified in paragraphs 1 and 2 of part one of this article, the person who filed the application has the right to apply to the court again with an application.

#### Article 324

[47]. Procedure and time limit for consideration of an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

An application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances shall be considered in a court session within a period not exceeding fifteen days from the date of its receipt by the court, with notification of the persons participating in the case.

The failure to appear of duly notified persons shall not be an obstacle to the consideration of the application.

#### Article 324

[48]. Ruling adopted by the court based on the results of consideration of an application for review of a judicial act on the basis of newly discovered circumstances

Based on the results of consideration of an application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances, the court shall issue a ruling on granting the application and revoking the judicial act previously adopted by it, or on refusing to grant the application.

Copies of the ruling issued based on the results of consideration of an application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances shall be sent to the persons participating in the case.

The court's ruling issued based on the results of consideration of an application for review of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances may be appealed (protested).

In case of revocation of a judicial act that has entered into legal force on the basis of newly discovered circumstances, the case shall be reconsidered by the same court that revoked the judicial act previously adopted by it, in the general procedure established by this Code.

## (chapter XXIV

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 25, 2023 No. ZRU-884 — National Database of Legislation, December 25, 2023, No. 03/23/884/0967 — Enters into force on January 1, 2024)

SECTION FIVE EXECUTION OF RULINGS ON THE APPLICATION OF ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTIES Chapter XXV. BASIC PROVISIONS

#### Article 325

Mandatory nature of execution of a ruling on the application of an administrative penalty

A ruling on the application of an administrative penalty is mandatory for execution by enterprises, institutions, organizations, officials and citizens.

#### Article 326

Enforcement of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

A decision on the application of an administrative penalty is subject to enforcement from the moment of its issuance, unless otherwise established by this Code and other legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

See previous version.

When a decision on the application of an administrative penalty is appealed or protested in appellate proceedings, it is subject to enforcement after the complaint or protest is left without satisfaction, with the exception of a decision on the imposition of a fine collected at the place of commission of the administrative offense.

(part two of Article 326 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

See previous version.

A decision on the imposition of a fine is subject to compulsory enforcement upon expiration of the period established by Article 332 of this Code for voluntary execution.

(part three of Article 326 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 23, 2019 No. 3RU-542 — National Database of Legislation, 24.05.2019, No. 03/19/542/317)

A decision on the application of an administrative penalty is referred for enforcement by the body (official) that issued it.

#### Article 327. Execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

A decision on the application of an administrative penalty is executed by bodies (officials) authorized to do so in the manner established by this Code and other legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

In the event that several decisions on the application of administrative penalties are issued against one person, each of them is executed independently.

#### Article 328

Postponement of execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

In the presence of circumstances due to which immediate execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty in the form of administrative arrest or a fine (with the exception of collection of a fine on the spot) is impossible, the body (official) that issued it may postpone execution of the decision for a period of up to one month.

#### Article 329

Termination of execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

The body (official) that issued a decision on the application of an administrative penalty terminates its execution in the following cases:

1) issuance of an act of amnesty, if it eliminates the application of an administrative penalty;

2) repeal of a normative act establishing administrative liability;

3) death of the person against whom the decision was issued.

#### Article 330

Limitation period for execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

A decision on the application of an administrative penalty is not subject to execution if it has not been referred for execution within three months from the date of issuance. In the event of suspension of execution of the decision in accordance with Article 318 of this Code, the running of the limitation period is suspended until consideration of the complaint or protest. In the event of postponement of execution of the decision in accordance with Article 328 of this Code, the running of the limitation period is suspended until expiration of the postponement period.

#### Article 331

Resolution of issues related to execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty

Issues related to execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty are resolved by the body (official) that issued it.

Control over the correct and timely execution of a decision on the application of an administrative penalty is assigned to the body (official) that issued it.

Complaints against actions of bodies (officials) in executing decisions on the application of administrative penalties are resolved by the bodies specified in Article 315, in compliance with the requirements of Articles 319 and 320 of this Code.

## Chapter XXVI. PROCEEDINGS FOR EXECUTION OF A DECISION ON THE IMPOSITION OF A FINE

#### Article 332. Time limits and procedure for execution of a decision on the imposition of a fine

See previous version.

A fine must be paid by the offender no later than thirty days from the date of service upon him of the decision on the imposition of a fine, and in cases of its appeal or protest — no later than fifteen days from the date of notification of leaving the complaint or protest without satisfaction, with the exception of part two of this Article.

(part one of Article 332 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 17, 2020 No. 3RU-612 — National Database of Legislation, 18.03.2020, No. 03/20/612/0326)

See previous version.

A fine imposed for violation of traffic rules must be paid by the offender no later than sixty days from the date of issuance of the decision on the imposition of a fine, and in cases of its appeal or protest — no later than thirty days from the date of notification of leaving the complaint or protest without satisfaction.

(part two of Article 332 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 23, 2019 No. 3RU-542 — National Database of Legislation, 24.05.2019, No. 03/19/542/317)

In the absence of independent earnings for persons aged sixteen to eighteen years, the fine is collected from parents or persons replacing them.

See previous version.

A fine imposed for commission of an administrative offense is paid by the offender to an institution of the corresponding bank, with the exception of a fine collected at the place of commission of the offense.

#### (Article 332

as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 14, 2009 No. 3RU-199 — SZ RU, 2009, No. 3, Art. 9)

See previous version.

#### Article 3321 Simplified procedure for execution of a decision on the imposition of a fine

See previous version.

In the event of voluntary payment by the offender of fifty percent of the amount of the fine within fifteen days or seventy percent of the amount of the fine within thirty days from the date of service upon him of the decision on the imposition of a fine, he is released from payment of the remaining amount of the fine, with the exception of cases provided for in part two of this Article.

The simplified procedure for execution of a decision on the imposition of a fine does not apply in the following cases:

commission of offenses provided for in Articles 131, 132, 136 and 140 of this Code;

appeal or filing of a protest against a decision on the imposition of a fine;

repeated commission of the same offense within one year after application of an administrative penalty.

(text of Article 3321 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 18, 2023 No. 3RU-830 — National Database of Legislation, 19.04.2023, No. 03/23/830/0216 — Enters into force on May 1, 2023)

#### Article 333. Compulsory execution of a decision on the imposition of a fine

See previous version. In the event of non-payment of a fine by the offender within the period established by Article 332 of this Code, the decision on the imposition of a fine is sent for withholding of the fine in a compulsory manner from his wages or other earnings, pension or stipend in accordance with the rules established by legislation on execution of judicial acts and acts of other bodies.

See previous version.

If the person subjected to a fine is not employed or collection of the fine from the wages or other income, pension or stipend of the offender is impossible for other reasons, collection of the fine is carried out on the basis of a decision of the body (official) on the imposition of a fine by a state executor by levying execution on the property of the offender, as well as on his share in common property.

(part two of Article 333 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 16, 2017 No. 3RU-448 — National Database of Legislation, 2017)

Collection of a fine may not be levied on property on which, in accordance with the legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan, execution may not be levied under enforcement documents.


This excerpt sits under: Date of entry into force > (Chapter XXIV

#### Article 334. Execution of a decision to impose a fine collected on the spot

When collecting a fine in accordance with Article 283 of this Code on the spot where the administrative offense was committed, the offender is issued a receipt of the established form, which is a document of strict financial accountability.

In case of non-payment of the fine on the spot where the administrative offense was committed, the proceedings on the case and execution of the decision are carried out in the manner provided by Articles 332 and 333 of this Code.

#### Article 335. Completion of proceedings for execution of a decision to impose a fine

A decision to impose a fine, under which the collection of the fine has been made in full, with a mark of execution is returned to the body (official) that issued it, or to the court.

See previous version.

After execution of a decision to impose a fine sent for compulsory execution, the state executor issues a decision on completion of enforcement proceedings, one copy of which is sent to the body (official) that issued it, or to the court.

#### (Article 335

supplemented by part two by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of March 17, 2020 No. 3RU-612 — National Database of Legislation, 18.03.2020, No. 03/20/612/0326)

## Chapter XXVII. PROCEEDINGS FOR EXECUTION OF A DECISION ON PAID SEIZURE OF AN OBJECT

#### Article 336. Execution of a decision on paid seizure of an object

See previous version.

Objects seized on the basis of a decision on paid seizure are sold in the established manner on a contractual-commission basis or at auction sales.

(part one of Article 336 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 14, 2009 No. 3RU-199 — SZ RU, 2009, No. 3, Art. 9)

See previous version.

A decision on paid seizure of weapons and ammunition for them is executed by internal affairs bodies.

(part two of Article 336 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of May 31, 2022 No. 3RU-772 — National Database of Legislation, 01.06.2022, No. 03/22/772/0460 — Enters into force on September 2, 2022)

Amounts received from the sale of the object seized for payment, in accordance with Article 26 of this Code, are transferred to the former owner minus the costs of

selling the seized object.

## Chapter XXVIII. PROCEEDINGS FOR EXECUTION OF A DECISION ON CONFISCATION OF AN OBJECT

See previous version.

#### Article 337. Execution of a decision on confiscation of an object

See previous version.

A decision on confiscation of an object that was an instrument of commission or a direct object of an administrative offense is executed by state executors of the bodies of the Bureau of Compulsory Execution under the General Prosecutor's Office of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(text of Article 337 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 16, 2017 No. 3RU-448 — National Database of Legislation, 2017)

#### Article 338. Procedure for sale or destruction of a confiscated object

Sale or destruction of a confiscated object that was an instrument of commission or a direct object of an administrative offense is carried out in the established manner.

#### Article 339

Completion of proceedings for execution of a decision on confiscation of an object

See previous version.

After execution of a decision on confiscation of an object that was an instrument of commission or a direct object of an administrative offense, the state executor issues a decision on completion of enforcement proceedings, one copy of which is sent to the criminal court that issued it.

(text of Article 339 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

## Chapter XXIX. PROCEEDINGS FOR EXECUTION OF A DECISION ON DEPRIVATION OF A SPECIAL RIGHT

#### Article 340. Bodies executing decisions on deprivation of a special right

A decision on deprivation of the right to operate vehicles is executed by officials of internal affairs bodies specified in Article 248 of this Code.

See previous version.

A decision on deprivation of boat operators of the right to operate small vessels is executed by officials of the Inspection for Small Vessels of the Ministry of Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan specified in part four of Article 2491 of this Code.

(part two of Article 340 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. 3RU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193 — Enters into force on March 26, 2020)

See changes entering into force on May 10, 2026

A decision on deprivation of the right to hunt is executed by officials of bodies exercising state control over compliance with hunting rules specified in part two of Article 261 of this Code.

#### Article 341

Procedure for execution of a decision on deprivation of the right to operate vehicles or small vessels

Execution of a decision on deprivation of the right to operate vehicles or small vessels is carried out by seizing the driver's license, as well as the coupon attached to it.

If a driver or boat operator is deprived of the right to operate not all types of vehicles or small vessels, then the driver's license and the coupon attached

to it indicate which types of vehicles or small vessels he is deprived of the right to operate.

See previous version.

The procedure for seizing licenses for the right to operate vehicles is established by the Ministry of Internal Affairs of the Republic of Uzbekistan, and licenses for the right to operate small vessels — by the Inspection for Small Vessels of the Ministry of Emergency Situations of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(part three of Article 341 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of December 24, 2019 No. 3RU-597 — National Database of Legislation, 25.12.2019, No. 03/19/597/4193 — Enters into force on March 26, 2020)

#### Article 342. Procedure for execution of a decision on deprivation of the right to hunt

Execution of a decision on deprivation of the right to hunt is carried out by seizing the hunting license.

In case of evasion by a person deprived of the right to hunt from surrendering the hunting license, the bodies exercising state control over compliance with hunting rules carry out seizure of the hunting license in the established manner.

#### Article 343. Grounds and procedure for reduction of the term of deprivation of a special right

See previous version.

In case of conscientious attitude to work and exemplary behavior of a person deprived for a certain term of the right to operate a vehicle or the right to hunt, the criminal court that imposed the penalty may, upon expiration of at least half of the assigned term and after payment of the fine imposed by the court, reduce upon petition of the administration of an enterprise, institution, organization the term of deprivation of the specified right.

(text of Article 343 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of January 12, 2021 No. 3RU-665 — National Database of Legislation, 13.01.2021, No. 03/21/665/0015)

#### Article 344. Calculation of the term of deprivation of a special right

Drivers of vehicles and persons who violated hunting rules are considered deprived of a special right from the day of issuance of the decision on deprivation of this right.

Upon expiration of the term of deprivation of a special right, as well as in case of its reduction in accordance with Article 343 of this Code, the documents seized from him are returned in the established manner to the person subjected to this administrative penalty.

In case of evasion by a driver deprived of the right to operate a vehicle from surrendering the driver's license, the term of deprivation is calculated from the moment of its seizure, but not later than the expiration of the term of deprivation determined by the body that made the decision.

## Chapter XXX. PROCEEDINGS FOR THE EXECUTION OF A RULING ON ADMINISTRATIVE ARREST

#### Article 345. Execution of a ruling on administrative arrest

A ruling on administrative arrest shall be executed immediately after it is issued.

#### Article 346. Procedure for serving administrative arrest

Persons subjected to administrative arrest shall be held in custody in places determined by internal affairs bodies. When executing a ruling on administrative arrest, arrested persons shall be subjected to personal search.

The term of administrative detention shall be counted toward the term of administrative arrest.

The serving of administrative arrest shall be carried out according to the rules established by legislative acts of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

Persons subjected to administrative arrest shall be employed in physical labor.

The organization of labor employment of persons subjected to administrative arrest shall be assigned to district (city) hokimiyats.

Persons subjected to administrative arrest shall not be paid wages at their place of permanent employment for the time spent under arrest.

See previous version.

Chapter XXX1. PROCEEDINGS FOR THE EXECUTION OF A RULING ON ADMINISTRATIVE EXPULSION OF FOREIGN CITIZENS AND STATELESS PERSONS BEYOND THE BORDERS OF THE REPUBLIC OF UZBEKISTAN

#### Article 3461

Bodies executing a ruling on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan

A ruling on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan shall be executed by the following bodies:

See previous version.

internal affairs bodies — when foreign citizens and stateless persons commit offenses provided for in Articles 512, 518, 519, 56, 57, 58, part one of Article 611, Articles 94, 1651, 1842, 1843, 189, 1891, 201, 2021, parts one through three of Article 225, Articles 239, 240, 241 of this Code;

(paragraph two of Article 3461 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of November 5, 2019 No. 3RU-579 — National Database of Legislation, 06.11.2019, No. 03/19/579/3994)

See previous version.

Border Troops of the State Security Service of the Republic of Uzbekistan — when foreign citizens and stateless persons commit an offense provided for in parts three and four of Article 2241 of this Code.

(paragraph three of Article 3461 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 10, 2024 No. 3RU-974 — National Database of Legislation, 10.10.2024, No. 03/24/974/0804)

#### Article 3462

Execution of a ruling on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan

Execution of a ruling on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan shall be carried out by official transfer of such persons to a representative of the authority of a foreign state to whose territory the said persons are expelled, or by controlled independent departure of the expelled person beyond the borders of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

See previous version.

If the transfer of the expelled person to a representative of a foreign state is not provided for by a treaty of the Republic of Uzbekistan with the said state, expulsion shall be carried out at a place determined by the Border Troops of the State Security Service of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

(part two of Article 3462 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of February 15, 2022 No. 3RU-753 — National Database of Legislation, 16.02.2022, No. 03/22/753/0133)

The authorities of a foreign state to (through) whose territory the said person is expelled shall be notified of the expulsion of a foreign citizen or stateless person from a checkpoint across the State Border of the Republic of Uzbekistan, if this is provided for by a treaty of the Republic of Uzbekistan with the said state.

Execution of a ruling on administrative expulsion of foreign citizens and stateless persons shall be formalized by an act.

(Chapter XXX1 introduced by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of April 25, 2016 No. 3RU-405 — SZ RU, 2016, No. 17, Art.

## 173) Chapter XXXI

PROCEEDINGS FOR THE EXECUTION OF A RULING REGARDING COMPENSATION FOR PROPERTY DAMAGE

#### Article 347

Procedure and time limits for execution of a ruling regarding compensation for property damage

A ruling on a case of administrative offense regarding compensation for property damage shall be executed in the procedure established by this Code and other legislation of the Republic of Uzbekistan.

A ruling on a case of administrative offense regarding compensation for property damage shall be an enforcement document.

Property damage must be compensated by the offender no later than fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling upon him (

#### Article 311

of this Code), and in case of appeal or protest thereof — no later than fifteen days from the date of notification of leaving the complaint or protest without satisfaction.


This excerpt sits under: Date of entry into force

#### Article 348

Consequences of non-execution of a ruling in the part of compensation for property damage

See previous version.

In case of non-execution of a ruling in the part of compensation for property damage within the period established by part three of Article 347 of this Code, it shall be sent to a state executor for forced execution in the manner established by legislation.

(text of Article 348 as amended by the Law of the Republic of Uzbekistan of October 16, 2017 No. 3RU-448 — National Database of Legislation, 2017)
